ny_tz = "America/New_York"

// ET clock slice for Market row: Asian 20:00–03:00, London 03:00–09:30, New York 09:30–20:00
f_market_session_et() =>
    int hm = hour(time, ny_tz) * 60 + minute(time, ny_tz)
    if hm >= 20 * 60 or hm < 3 * 60
        "Asian"
    else if hm < 9 * 60 + 30
        "London"
    else
        "New York"

// Same session logic, but evaluated at an explicit timestamp (used by the Historical Bar Panel, since it looks at a past bar rather than "now")
f_market_session_et_at(int t) =>
    int hm = hour(t, ny_tz) * 60 + minute(t, ny_tz)
    if hm >= 20 * 60 or hm < 3 * 60
        "Asian"
    else if hm < 9 * 60 + 30
        "London"
    else
        "New York"

// Arranges three labeled values in true ascending numeric order (e.g. "ema9(3) 7599.2<*price: 7602.5*<ema20(3) 7600.05")
// rather than a fixed label order, since which of EMA9/EMA20/price is lowest changes with trend direction.
// star_label: if it matches l1/l2/l3 exactly, that entry gets wrapped in asterisks, wherever it lands in the order.
f_order3(float v1, string l1, float v2, string l2, float v3, string l3, string star_label) =>
    string s1 = (l1 == star_label ? "*" : "") + l1 + " " + str.tostring(v1, "#.##") + (l1 == star_label ? "*" : "")
    string s2 = (l2 == star_label ? "*" : "") + l2 + " " + str.tostring(v2, "#.##") + (l2 == star_label ? "*" : "")
    string s3 = (l3 == star_label ? "*" : "") + l3 + " " + str.tostring(v3, "#.##") + (l3 == star_label ? "*" : "")
    string result = ""
    if v1 <= v2 and v2 <= v3
        result := s1 + "<" + s2 + "<" + s3
    else if v1 <= v3 and v3 <= v2
        result := s1 + "<" + s3 + "<" + s2
    else if v2 <= v1 and v1 <= v3
        result := s2 + "<" + s1 + "<" + s3
    else if v2 <= v3 and v3 <= v1
        result := s2 + "<" + s3 + "<" + s1
    else if v3 <= v1 and v1 <= v2
        result := s3 + "<" + s1 + "<" + s2
    else
        result := s3 + "<" + s2 + "<" + s1
    result

// Classifies price relative to two EMAs regardless of which one is higher: "Above Both", "Below Both", or "Between".
f_price_vs_emas(float price, float ema_a, float ema_b) =>
    float lo = math.min(ema_a, ema_b)
    float hi = math.max(ema_a, ema_b)
    string result = price > hi ? "Above Both" : price < lo ? "Below Both" : "Between"
    result

// Formats a UTC ms timestamp as "M/D H:MM ET" in New York time. Used for "last flip" displays
// (Status table, Historical Bar Panel, alert email) so they all read the same way.
f_fmt_ny_time(int t) =>
    string result = na(t) ? "—" : str.tostring(month(t, ny_tz), "#") + "/" + str.tostring(dayofmonth(t, ny_tz), "#") + " " + (hour(t, ny_tz) < 10 ? "0" : "") + str.tostring(hour(t, ny_tz), "#") + ":" + (minute(t, ny_tz) < 10 ? "0" : "") + str.tostring(minute(t, ny_tz), "#") + " ET"
    result

// Abbreviates a Structure classification string ("Higher High" -> "HH", etc.) for compact table display.
f_struct_abbrev(string s) =>
    string result = s == "Higher High" ? "HH" : s == "Lower High" ? "LH" : s == "Higher Low" ? "HL" : s == "Lower Low" ? "LL" : s
    result

// Level Proximity Alerts (ported from CBC Wire): returns a formatted bullet-text string if price
// is within the CROSSING distance of level_val, else "". "Crossing" here means "currently very
// close to" the level (either side), not a directional cross event like the existing VWAP
// Break/EMA 200 Break alert types.
f_prox_crossing(float price, float level_val, string level_name) =>
    string result = ""
    if not na(level_val)
        float dist = math.abs(price - level_val)
        if dist <= prox_crossing_dist
            string dir_word = price > level_val ? "above" : "below"
            result := level_name + " Crossing (" + str.tostring(dist, "#.##") + " points " + dir_word + " Price, " + level_name + ": " + str.tostring(level_val, "#.##") + ")"
    result

// Same, but for the wider APPROACHING band (farther than crossing distance, within approaching
// distance). Only called when a full crossing pass has already found nothing.
f_prox_approaching(float price, float level_val, string level_name) =>
    string result = ""
    if not na(level_val)
        float dist = math.abs(price - level_val)
        if dist > prox_crossing_dist and dist <= prox_approach_dist
            string dir_word = price > level_val ? "above" : "below"
            result := level_name + " Approaching (" + str.tostring(dist, "#.##") + " points " + dir_word + " Price, " + level_name + ": " + str.tostring(level_val, "#.##") + ")"
    result

// Asian OR anchor: 8 PM ET; bars after midnight before 3 AM use previous calendar day's 8 PM open
f_asia_or_open_ms() =>
    int cand = timestamp(ny_tz, year(time, ny_tz), month(time, ny_tz), dayofmonth(time, ny_tz), 20, 0)
    hour(time, ny_tz) < 3 and time < cand ? cand - 86400000 : cand

// ===========================
// LTF EMA Cloud
// ===========================
ema9 = ta.ema(close, 9)
ema20 = ta.ema(close, 20)

ema_bullish = ema9 >= ema20
// EMA9/20 Cross alert conditions — the crossing MOMENT, distinct from ema_bullish above which
// just reports the current cloud state every bar.
ema920_cross_bull = ta.crossover(ema9, ema20)
ema920_cross_bear = ta.crossunder(ema9, ema20)

// Plot EMA lines (OR-style: named args, title= last)
plotEMA20 = plot(ema20, color=#C8C8C8, linewidth=2, style=plot.style_line, title="EMA 20")
plotEMA9 = plot(ema9, color=#808080, linewidth=1, style=plot.style_line, title="EMA 9")

// Fill between the same two plots (no duplicate hidden series — avoids extra Style rows)
fill(plotEMA9, plotEMA20, color=ema_bullish ? color.new(col_ema_cloud_bull, ema_cloud_transp) : color.new(col_ema_cloud_bear, ema_cloud_transp), title="EMA Cloud")

// HTF EMA 9/20 cloud (fill only; plots hidden — agreement colors at 50% transparency)
// v4.27: added htf_ema200 to this SAME request.security() tuple (a 3rd return value) instead of a
// new call — request.security() calls are the expensive thing to duplicate, not tuple elements.
[htf_ema9, htf_ema20, htf_ema200] = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), htf_timeframe, [ta.ema(close, 9), ta.ema(close, 20), ta.ema(close, 200)], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_on)
htf_ema_bullish = htf_ema9 >= htf_ema20
plotHTFEMA9 = plot(show_htf_ema_cloud ? htf_ema9 : na, title="HTF EMA 9")
plotHTFEMA20 = plot(show_htf_ema_cloud ? htf_ema20 : na, title="HTF EMA 20")
fill(plotHTFEMA9, plotHTFEMA20, color=show_htf_ema_cloud ? (htf_ema_bullish ? color.new(col_cbc_bar_long, col_cbc_bar_cloud_transp) : color.new(col_cbc_bar_short, col_cbc_bar_cloud_transp)) : na, title="HTF EMA Cloud")
show_htf_ema200 = input.bool(false, "HTF EMA 200", inline="htfema200", group="HTF Signals", tooltip="Plots the HTF-timeframe 200 EMA as a line.")
col_htf_ema200 = input.color(#ffeb3a, "", inline="htfema200", group="HTF Signals")
plot(show_htf_ema200 ? htf_ema200 : na, title="HTF EMA 200", color=col_htf_ema200, linewidth=2)

// ===========================
// VWAP / EMA
// ===========================
vwap_value = ta.vwap(hlc3)
ema200 = ta.ema(close, 200)

// --- Level Proximity Alerts: values not already computed elsewhere ---
// Bill Breakers: nearest 100-point round numbers above/below price (ported from CBC Wire).
bb_above = math.ceil(close / 100) * 100
bb_below = math.floor(close / 100) * 100
// Swing pivots: 1-bar-lookback fractal high/low (ta.pivothigh/pivotlow must run unconditionally
// every bar, top-level) — same underlying concept as this script's existing "Pivot (LTF/HTF/MTF)"
// rows (also fractal swing highs/lows via ta.pivothigh/pivotlow), just a fixed 1-bar lookback
// specifically for level-proximity checking rather than the LTF/HTF/MTF-configurable version.
var float prox_last_ph = na
var float prox_last_pl = na
prox_ph = ta.pivothigh(high, 1, 1)
prox_pl = ta.pivotlow(low, 1, 1)
if not na(prox_ph)
    prox_last_ph := prox_ph
if not na(prox_pl)
    prox_last_pl := prox_pl
// One-shot dedup per pivot value (crossing only, matching CBC Wire) — reset whenever a NEW pivot
// value forms, so the next crossing of that new value can still alert once.
var float prox_alerted_ph = na
var float prox_alerted_pl = na
if not na(prox_ph)
    prox_alerted_ph := na
if not na(prox_pl)
    prox_alerted_pl := na

// --- Pivot Points (Daily, Standard/Floor formula) ---
// Distinct from BOTH the fractal swing-pivot rows above AND the existing "Pivot (LTF/HTF/MTF)"
// Status table rows — this is the classic floor-trader formula, from the prior COMPLETED daily
// bar's High/Low/Close (not a running/live calculation).
[piv_prev_h, piv_prev_l, piv_prev_c] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "D", [high[1], low[1], close[1]], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_on)
piv_range = piv_prev_h - piv_prev_l
piv_pp = pivot_formula == "Woodie" ? (piv_prev_h + piv_prev_l + 2 * piv_prev_c) / 4 : (piv_prev_h + piv_prev_l + piv_prev_c) / 3
piv_r1 = pivot_formula == "Camarilla" ? piv_prev_c + piv_range * 1.1 / 12 : pivot_formula == "Fibonacci" ? piv_pp + 0.382 * piv_range : 2 * piv_pp - piv_prev_l
piv_s1 = pivot_formula == "Camarilla" ? piv_prev_c - piv_range * 1.1 / 12 : pivot_formula == "Fibonacci" ? piv_pp - 0.382 * piv_range : 2 * piv_pp - piv_prev_h
piv_r2 = pivot_formula == "Camarilla" ? piv_prev_c + piv_range * 1.1 / 6 : pivot_formula == "Fibonacci" ? piv_pp + 0.618 * piv_range : piv_pp + piv_range
piv_s2 = pivot_formula == "Camarilla" ? piv_prev_c - piv_range * 1.1 / 6 : pivot_formula == "Fibonacci" ? piv_pp - 0.618 * piv_range : piv_pp - piv_range
piv_r3 = pivot_formula == "Camarilla" ? piv_prev_c + piv_range * 1.1 / 4 : pivot_formula == "Fibonacci" ? piv_pp + 1.000 * piv_range : piv_prev_h + 2 * (piv_pp - piv_prev_l)
piv_s3 = pivot_formula == "Camarilla" ? piv_prev_c - piv_range * 1.1 / 4 : pivot_formula == "Fibonacci" ? piv_pp - 1.000 * piv_range : piv_prev_l - 2 * (piv_prev_h - piv_pp)

// Crossing detection for each level — bullish = crossed above (closed on the opposite/upper side
// from the prior bar), bearish = crossed below. Checked independently per level (not a two-pass
// priority scan like Level Proximity above), since these are genuinely static levels for the
// whole day rather than a moving band — more than one CAN legitimately cross the same bar.
piv_pp_cross_up = ta.crossover(close, piv_pp)
piv_pp_cross_dn = ta.crossunder(close, piv_pp)
piv_r1_cross_up = ta.crossover(close, piv_r1)
piv_r1_cross_dn = ta.crossunder(close, piv_r1)
piv_s1_cross_up = ta.crossover(close, piv_s1)
piv_s1_cross_dn = ta.crossunder(close, piv_s1)
piv_r2_cross_up = ta.crossover(close, piv_r2)
piv_r2_cross_dn = ta.crossunder(close, piv_r2)
piv_s2_cross_up = ta.crossover(close, piv_s2)
piv_s2_cross_dn = ta.crossunder(close, piv_s2)
piv_r3_cross_up = ta.crossover(close, piv_r3)
piv_r3_cross_dn = ta.crossunder(close, piv_r3)
piv_s3_cross_up = ta.crossover(close, piv_s3)
piv_s3_cross_dn = ta.crossunder(close, piv_s3)

// Packed into arrays specifically so f_fire_alerts() below can reference 3 external elements
// (the arrays) instead of 21 separate scalars (7 prices + 14 cross booleans) — that many new
// scalar references pushed f_fire_alerts itself over Pine's 254-external-element ceiling
// (CE10116, was at 263) once this feature was added. ONE canonical order used everywhere this
// feature appears (alerts, table, chart lines): index 0=R3, 1=R2, 2=R1, 3=PP, 4=S1, 5=S2, 6=S3.
var float[] piv_price_arr = array.new_float(7, na)
array.set(piv_price_arr, 0, piv_r3)
array.set(piv_price_arr, 1, piv_r2)
array.set(piv_price_arr, 2, piv_r1)
array.set(piv_price_arr, 3, piv_pp)
array.set(piv_price_arr, 4, piv_s1)
array.set(piv_price_arr, 5, piv_s2)
array.set(piv_price_arr, 6, piv_s3)
var bool[] piv_cross_up_arr = array.new_bool(7, false)
array.set(piv_cross_up_arr, 0, piv_r3_cross_up)
array.set(piv_cross_up_arr, 1, piv_r2_cross_up)
array.set(piv_cross_up_arr, 2, piv_r1_cross_up)
array.set(piv_cross_up_arr, 3, piv_pp_cross_up)
array.set(piv_cross_up_arr, 4, piv_s1_cross_up)
array.set(piv_cross_up_arr, 5, piv_s2_cross_up)
array.set(piv_cross_up_arr, 6, piv_s3_cross_up)
var bool[] piv_cross_dn_arr = array.new_bool(7, false)
array.set(piv_cross_dn_arr, 0, piv_r3_cross_dn)
array.set(piv_cross_dn_arr, 1, piv_r2_cross_dn)
array.set(piv_cross_dn_arr, 2, piv_r1_cross_dn)
array.set(piv_cross_dn_arr, 3, piv_pp_cross_dn)
array.set(piv_cross_dn_arr, 4, piv_s1_cross_dn)
array.set(piv_cross_dn_arr, 5, piv_s2_cross_dn)
array.set(piv_cross_dn_arr, 6, piv_s3_cross_dn)
var string[] piv_labels = array.from("R3", "R2", "R1", "PP", "S1", "S2", "S3")
var bool[] piv_enabled = array.new_bool(7, false)
array.set(piv_enabled, 0, show_pivot_points and show_pivot_r3)
array.set(piv_enabled, 1, show_pivot_points and show_pivot_r2)
array.set(piv_enabled, 2, show_pivot_points and show_pivot_r1)
array.set(piv_enabled, 3, show_pivot_points and show_pivot_pp)
array.set(piv_enabled, 4, show_pivot_points and show_pivot_s1)
array.set(piv_enabled, 5, show_pivot_points and show_pivot_s2)
array.set(piv_enabled, 6, show_pivot_points and show_pivot_s3)


plot(show_levels_master and show_vwap ? vwap_value : na, color=col_vwap, linewidth=vwap_width, style=plot.style_line, linestyle=plot.linestyle_solid, title="VWAP")
plot(show_levels_master and show_ema200_line ? ema200 : na, color=col_ema200, linewidth=ema200_width, style=plot.style_line, title="EMA 200")

// LTF touch dots — bar range crosses the level (same pattern as EMA20)
price_touches_ema20 = show_ema_touch_dots and (low <= ema20 and high >= ema20)
plot(price_touches_ema20 ? ema20 : na, color=#FFFFFF, linewidth=2, style=plot.style_circles, title="EMA20 Touch")
price_touches_vwap = show_vwap_touch_dots and (low <= vwap_value and high >= vwap_value)
plot(price_touches_vwap ? vwap_value : na, color=#FF69B4, linewidth=2, style=plot.style_circles, title="VWAP Touch")
price_touches_ema200 = show_ema200_touch_dots and (low <= ema200 and high >= ema200)
plot(price_touches_ema200 ? ema200 : na, color=#FF9800, linewidth=3, style=plot.style_circles, title="EMA200 Touch")

// ===========================
// Bill Breaker (ThinkScript MapleStax_BillBreaker parity: ratchet base, gap plot when base steps, five levels)
// ===========================
var float bb_base = na
if na(bb_base[1])
    bb_base := math.round(open / bb_multiplier) * bb_multiplier
else
    float bb_prev = bb_base[1]
    if close >= bb_prev + 2 * bb_multiplier
        bb_base := bb_prev + bb_multiplier
    else if close <= bb_prev - 2 * bb_multiplier
        bb_base := bb_prev - bb_multiplier
    else
        bb_base := bb_prev

bool bb_base_changed = not na(bb_base[1]) and bb_base != bb_base[1]
bool bb_plot_seg = show_bill_breaker and not bb_base_changed
float bb_l2 = bb_base - 2.0 * bb_multiplier
float bb_l1 = bb_base - bb_multiplier
float bb_m0 = bb_base
float bb_u1 = bb_base + bb_multiplier
float bb_u2 = bb_base + 2.0 * bb_multiplier

plot(bb_plot_seg ? bb_l2 : na, title="BB Lower 2", color=col_bb, linewidth=1, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle = plot.linestyle_dotted)
plot(bb_plot_seg ? bb_l1 : na, title="BB Lower 1", color=col_bb, linewidth=1, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle = plot.linestyle_dotted)
plot(bb_plot_seg ? bb_m0 : na, title="BB Base", color=col_bb, linewidth=1, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle = plot.linestyle_dotted)
plot(bb_plot_seg ? bb_u1 : na, title="BB Upper 1", color=col_bb, linewidth=1, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle = plot.linestyle_dotted)
plot(bb_plot_seg ? bb_u2 : na, title="BB Upper 2", color=col_bb, linewidth=1, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle = plot.linestyle_dotted)

// ===========================
// Reference levels (YDH/YDL/YDC from prior completed NY RTH; PMH/PML from premarket) — MapleStax_Levels-style line.new
// ===========================
getLevelLabelText(level_name, level_price) =>
    string result = ""
    if show_level_name and show_level_price
        result := level_name + " " + str.tostring(level_price, "#.##")
    else if show_level_name
        result := level_name
    else if show_level_price
        result := str.tostring(level_price, "#.##")
    result

is_premarket = not na(time(timeframe.period, "1800-0929", ny_tz))
is_regular = not na(time(timeframe.period, "0930-1600", ny_tz))
new_day = is_regular and not is_regular[1]
premarket_start = is_premarket and not is_premarket[1]

// YDH/YDL/YDC: do not use D1 on `ticker.standard` — that daily is typically full ETH/Globex session, so high[1]/low[1] are not RTH-only. Track H/L/last close only while `is_regular`, commit when RTH block ends.
bool rth_enter = is_regular and not is_regular[1]
bool rth_exit = not is_regular and is_regular[1]
var float rth_run_hi = na
var float rth_run_lo = na
var float ydh_prev_rth = na
var float ydl_prev_rth = na
var float ydc_prev_rth = na
if rth_exit
    ydh_prev_rth := rth_run_hi
    ydl_prev_rth := rth_run_lo
    ydc_prev_rth := close[1]
if rth_enter
    rth_run_hi := high
    rth_run_lo := low
else if is_regular
    rth_run_hi := na(rth_run_hi) ? high : math.max(rth_run_hi, high)
    rth_run_lo := na(rth_run_lo) ? low : math.min(rth_run_lo, low)

float YDH = ydh_prev_rth
float YDL = ydl_prev_rth
float YDC = ydc_prev_rth

// London / Asian session High-Low: same "track while in session, commit on session exit" pattern as
// YDH/YDL above, but for the full London (03:00–09:30 ET) and Asian (20:00–03:00 ET) session windows —
// this is the whole session's range, not just its opening range.
bool is_ln_sess = not na(time(timeframe.period, "0300-0930", ny_tz))
bool is_as_sess = not na(time(timeframe.period, "2000-0300", ny_tz))
bool is_ny_full_sess = not na(time(timeframe.period, "0930-2000", ny_tz))
bool ln_sess_enter = is_ln_sess and not is_ln_sess[1]
bool ln_sess_exit = not is_ln_sess and is_ln_sess[1]
bool as_sess_enter = is_as_sess and not is_as_sess[1]
bool as_sess_exit = not is_as_sess and is_as_sess[1]

var float ln_run_hi = na
var float ln_run_lo = na
var float ln_prev_hi = na
var float ln_prev_lo = na
var int ln_start_t = na
var int ln_prev_start_t = na
var float as_run_hi = na
var float as_run_lo = na
var float as_prev_hi = na
var float as_prev_lo = na
var int as_start_t = na
var int as_prev_start_t = na

if ln_sess_exit
    ln_prev_hi := ln_run_hi
    ln_prev_lo := ln_run_lo
if ln_sess_enter
    ln_prev_start_t := ln_start_t
    ln_start_t := time
    ln_run_hi := high
    ln_run_lo := low
else if is_ln_sess
    ln_run_hi := na(ln_run_hi) ? high : math.max(ln_run_hi, high)
    ln_run_lo := na(ln_run_lo) ? low : math.min(ln_run_lo, low)

if as_sess_exit
    as_prev_hi := as_run_hi
    as_prev_lo := as_run_lo
if as_sess_enter
    as_prev_start_t := as_start_t
    as_start_t := time
    as_run_hi := high
    as_run_lo := low
else if is_as_sess
    as_run_hi := na(as_run_hi) ? high : math.max(as_run_hi, high)
    as_run_lo := na(as_run_lo) ? low : math.min(as_run_lo, low)

float LNDH = ln_prev_hi
float LNDL = ln_prev_lo
float ASDH = as_prev_hi
float ASDL = as_prev_lo

var float PMH = na
var float PML = na
var float current_pm_high = na
var float current_pm_low = na
var int day_start_t = na
var int pm_start_t = na
var int pmh_origin_t = na
var int pml_origin_t = na
var int prev_day_start_t = na

if premarket_start
    current_pm_high := high
    current_pm_low := low
    pm_start_t := time
    pmh_origin_t := time
    pml_origin_t := time

if is_premarket
    if na(current_pm_high) or high > current_pm_high
        current_pm_high := high
        pmh_origin_t := time
    if na(current_pm_low) or low < current_pm_low
        current_pm_low := low
        pml_origin_t := time

if new_day
    prev_day_start_t := day_start_t
    PMH := current_pm_high
    PML := current_pm_low
    day_start_t := time

if is_regular
    if na(day_start_t)
        day_start_t := time

var line ydh_line = na
var line ydl_line = na
var line ydc_line = na
var line pmh_line = na
var line pml_line = na
var line ln_h_line = na
var line ln_l_line = na
var line as_h_line = na
var line as_l_line = na
var label ydh_lbl = na
var label ydl_lbl = na
var label ydc_lbl = na
var label pmh_lbl = na
var label pml_lbl = na
var label ln_h_lbl = na
var label ln_l_lbl = na
var label as_h_lbl = na
var label as_l_lbl = na
var line[] piv_lines = array.new_line(7, na)
var label[] piv_lbls = array.new_label(7, na)

if new_day
    line.delete(ydh_line)
    line.delete(ydl_line)
    line.delete(ydc_line)
    line.delete(pmh_line)
    line.delete(pml_line)
    line.delete(ln_h_line)
    line.delete(ln_l_line)
    line.delete(as_h_line)
    line.delete(as_l_line)
    label.delete(ydh_lbl)
    label.delete(ydl_lbl)
    label.delete(ydc_lbl)
    label.delete(pmh_lbl)
    label.delete(pml_lbl)
    label.delete(ln_h_lbl)
    label.delete(ln_l_lbl)
    label.delete(as_h_lbl)
    label.delete(as_l_lbl)
    for pi = 0 to 6
        line.delete(array.get(piv_lines, pi))
        label.delete(array.get(piv_lbls, pi))
        array.set(piv_lines, pi, na)
        array.set(piv_lbls, pi, na)
    ydh_line := na
    ydl_line := na
    ydc_line := na
    pmh_line := na
    pml_line := na
    ln_h_line := na
    ln_l_line := na
    as_h_line := na
    as_l_line := na
    ydh_lbl := na
    ydl_lbl := na
    ydc_lbl := na
    pmh_lbl := na
    pml_lbl := na
    ln_h_lbl := na
    ln_l_lbl := na
    as_h_lbl := na
    as_l_lbl := na

pmh_display = is_premarket ? current_pm_high : PMH
pml_display = is_premarket ? current_pm_low : PML

if not na(YDH)
    int lvl_bar_ms = int(timeframe.in_seconds(timeframe.period) * 1000)
    int lvl_line_t2 = time + extend_levels_bars * lvl_bar_ms
    int yd_line_t1 = int(nz(prev_day_start_t, nz(day_start_t, time)))
    int pmh_line_t1 = is_premarket ? int(nz(pm_start_t, time)) : int(nz(pmh_origin_t, nz(day_start_t, time)))
    int pml_line_t1 = is_premarket ? int(nz(pm_start_t, time)) : int(nz(pml_origin_t, nz(day_start_t, time)))
    // v4.34 token-trim: YDH/YDL/PMH/PML all shared the "show_levels_master and" prefix — nested them
    // under one wrapping "if show_levels_master" (same collapse as alert_session_ok/htf_early_time_ok
    // elsewhere) instead of repeating it 4 times. Pivot Points and YDC are independently toggled (not
    // part of the "master" switch), so they're moved after this block instead of staying interleaved
    // between YDL and PMH — pure reordering, neither reads state the other writes, so execution order
    // between them doesn't matter.
    if show_levels_master
        if show_ydh
            string ydh_style = f_ref_line_style(ydh_line_style)
            if na(ydh_line)
                ydh_line := line.new(yd_line_t1, YDH, lvl_line_t2, YDH, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_ydh, width=ydh_width, style=ydh_style)
            else
                line.set_x1(ydh_line, yd_line_t1)
                line.set_y1(ydh_line, YDH)
                line.set_x2(ydh_line, lvl_line_t2)
                line.set_y2(ydh_line, YDH)
                line.set_color(ydh_line, col_ydh)
                line.set_width(ydh_line, ydh_width)
                line.set_style(ydh_line, ydh_style)
            if show_level_name or show_level_price
                if na(ydh_lbl)
                    ydh_lbl := label.new(lvl_line_t2, YDH, getLevelLabelText("YDH", YDH), xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=col_ydh, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
                else
                    label.set_x(ydh_lbl, lvl_line_t2)
                    label.set_y(ydh_lbl, YDH)
                    label.set_text(ydh_lbl, getLevelLabelText("YDH", YDH))
                    label.set_textcolor(ydh_lbl, col_ydh)
        if show_ydl
            string ydl_style = f_ref_line_style(ydl_line_style)
            if na(ydl_line)
                ydl_line := line.new(yd_line_t1, YDL, lvl_line_t2, YDL, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_ydl, width=ydl_width, style=ydl_style)
            else
                line.set_x1(ydl_line, yd_line_t1)
                line.set_y1(ydl_line, YDL)
                line.set_x2(ydl_line, lvl_line_t2)
                line.set_y2(ydl_line, YDL)
                line.set_color(ydl_line, col_ydl)
                line.set_width(ydl_line, ydl_width)
                line.set_style(ydl_line, ydl_style)
            if show_level_name or show_level_price
                if na(ydl_lbl)
                    ydl_lbl := label.new(lvl_line_t2, YDL, getLevelLabelText("YDL", YDL), xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=col_ydl, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
                else
                    label.set_x(ydl_lbl, lvl_line_t2)
                    label.set_y(ydl_lbl, YDL)
                    label.set_text(ydl_lbl, getLevelLabelText("YDL", YDL))
                    label.set_textcolor(ydl_lbl, col_ydl)
        if show_pmh and not na(pmh_display)
            string pmh_style = f_ref_line_style(pmh_line_style)
            if na(pmh_line)
                pmh_line := line.new(pmh_line_t1, pmh_display, lvl_line_t2, pmh_display, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_pmh, width=pmh_width, style=pmh_style)
            else
                line.set_x1(pmh_line, pmh_line_t1)
                line.set_y1(pmh_line, pmh_display)
                line.set_x2(pmh_line, lvl_line_t2)
                line.set_y2(pmh_line, pmh_display)
                line.set_color(pmh_line, col_pmh)
                line.set_width(pmh_line, pmh_width)
                line.set_style(pmh_line, pmh_style)
            if show_level_name or show_level_price
                if na(pmh_lbl)
                    pmh_lbl := label.new(lvl_line_t2, pmh_display, getLevelLabelText("PMH", pmh_display), xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=col_pmh, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
                else
                    label.set_x(pmh_lbl, lvl_line_t2)
                    label.set_y(pmh_lbl, pmh_display)
                    label.set_text(pmh_lbl, getLevelLabelText("PMH", pmh_display))
                    label.set_textcolor(pmh_lbl, col_pmh)
        if show_pml and not na(pml_display)
            string pml_style = f_ref_line_style(pml_line_style)
            if na(pml_line)
                pml_line := line.new(pml_line_t1, pml_display, lvl_line_t2, pml_display, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_pml, width=pml_width, style=pml_style)
            else
                line.set_x1(pml_line, pml_line_t1)
                line.set_y1(pml_line, pml_display)
                line.set_x2(pml_line, lvl_line_t2)
                line.set_y2(pml_line, pml_display)
                line.set_color(pml_line, col_pml)
                line.set_width(pml_line, pml_width)
                line.set_style(pml_line, pml_style)
            if show_level_name or show_level_price
                if na(pml_lbl)
                    pml_lbl := label.new(lvl_line_t2, pml_display, getLevelLabelText("PML", pml_display), xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=col_pml, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
                else
                    label.set_x(pml_lbl, lvl_line_t2)
                    label.set_y(pml_lbl, pml_display)
                    label.set_text(pml_lbl, getLevelLabelText("PML", pml_display))
                    label.set_textcolor(pml_lbl, col_pml)
    // Pivot Points (Daily, Standard) — anchored to the START OF TODAY (not yesterday, since these
    // levels apply to today's session even though they're computed from yesterday's H/L/C), same
    // extend-to-current-bar pattern as YDH/YDL above. Looped over the 7 levels (reusing the same
    // packed arrays as the alerts/table) instead of 7 near-identical ~15-line blocks, to help
    // stay under Pine's compiled token ceiling (CE10117) now that the script has grown this large.
    int piv_line_t1 = int(nz(day_start_t, time))
    for pi = 0 to 6
        if show_pivot_lines and array.get(piv_enabled, pi)
            float pi_val = array.get(piv_price_arr, pi)
            color pi_col = pi == 3 ? col_piv_pp : pi < 3 ? col_piv_r : col_piv_s
            string pi_lbl_txt = array.get(piv_labels, pi) + " D"
            line pi_line = array.get(piv_lines, pi)
            if na(pi_line)
                array.set(piv_lines, pi, line.new(piv_line_t1, pi_val, lvl_line_t2, pi_val, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=pi_col, width=piv_line_width, style=line.style_dashed))
            else
                line.set_x1(pi_line, piv_line_t1)
                line.set_y1(pi_line, pi_val)
                line.set_x2(pi_line, lvl_line_t2)
                line.set_y2(pi_line, pi_val)
            if show_level_name or show_level_price
                label pi_lbl = array.get(piv_lbls, pi)
                if na(pi_lbl)
                    array.set(piv_lbls, pi, label.new(lvl_line_t2, pi_val, getLevelLabelText(pi_lbl_txt, pi_val), xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=pi_col, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small))
                else
                    label.set_x(pi_lbl, lvl_line_t2)
                    label.set_y(pi_lbl, pi_val)
                    label.set_text(pi_lbl, getLevelLabelText(pi_lbl_txt, pi_val))
                    label.set_textcolor(pi_lbl, pi_col)
    if show_ydc
        string ydc_style = f_ref_line_style(ydc_line_style)
        if na(ydc_line)
            ydc_line := line.new(yd_line_t1, YDC, lvl_line_t2, YDC, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_ydc, width=ydc_width, style=ydc_style)
        else
            line.set_x1(ydc_line, yd_line_t1)
            line.set_y1(ydc_line, YDC)
            line.set_x2(ydc_line, lvl_line_t2)
            line.set_y2(ydc_line, YDC)
            line.set_color(ydc_line, col_ydc)
            line.set_width(ydc_line, ydc_width)
            line.set_style(ydc_line, ydc_style)
        if show_level_name or show_level_price
            if na(ydc_lbl)
                ydc_lbl := label.new(lvl_line_t2, YDC, getLevelLabelText("YDC", YDC), xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=col_ydc, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
            else
                label.set_x(ydc_lbl, lvl_line_t2)
                label.set_y(ydc_lbl, YDC)
                label.set_text(ydc_lbl, getLevelLabelText("YDC", YDC))
                label.set_textcolor(ydc_lbl, col_ydc)

if not na(LNDH)
    int lvl_bar_ms2 = int(timeframe.in_seconds(timeframe.period) * 1000)
    int lvl_line_t2b = time + extend_levels_bars * lvl_bar_ms2
    int ln_line_t1 = int(nz(ln_prev_start_t, nz(ln_start_t, time)))
    if show_levels_master and show_ln_hl
        if na(ln_h_line)
            ln_h_line := line.new(ln_line_t1, LNDH, lvl_line_t2b, LNDH, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_ln_h, width=ln_hl_width, style=line.style_dashed)
        else
            line.set_x1(ln_h_line, ln_line_t1)
            line.set_y1(ln_h_line, LNDH)
            line.set_x2(ln_h_line, lvl_line_t2b)
            line.set_y2(ln_h_line, LNDH)
            line.set_color(ln_h_line, col_ln_h)
            line.set_width(ln_h_line, ln_hl_width)
        if show_level_name or show_level_price
            if na(ln_h_lbl)
                ln_h_lbl := label.new(lvl_line_t2b, LNDH, getLevelLabelText("LN-H", LNDH), xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=col_ln_h, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
            else
                label.set_x(ln_h_lbl, lvl_line_t2b)
                label.set_y(ln_h_lbl, LNDH)
                label.set_text(ln_h_lbl, getLevelLabelText("LN-H", LNDH))
                label.set_textcolor(ln_h_lbl, col_ln_h)
        if not na(LNDL)
            if na(ln_l_line)
                ln_l_line := line.new(ln_line_t1, LNDL, lvl_line_t2b, LNDL, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_ln_l, width=ln_hl_width, style=line.style_dashed)
            else
                line.set_x1(ln_l_line, ln_line_t1)
                line.set_y1(ln_l_line, LNDL)
                line.set_x2(ln_l_line, lvl_line_t2b)
                line.set_y2(ln_l_line, LNDL)
                line.set_color(ln_l_line, col_ln_l)
                line.set_width(ln_l_line, ln_hl_width)
            if show_level_name or show_level_price
                if na(ln_l_lbl)
                    ln_l_lbl := label.new(lvl_line_t2b, LNDL, getLevelLabelText("LN-L", LNDL), xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=col_ln_l, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
                else
                    label.set_x(ln_l_lbl, lvl_line_t2b)
                    label.set_y(ln_l_lbl, LNDL)
                    label.set_text(ln_l_lbl, getLevelLabelText("LN-L", LNDL))
                    label.set_textcolor(ln_l_lbl, col_ln_l)

if not na(ASDH)
    int lvl_bar_ms3 = int(timeframe.in_seconds(timeframe.period) * 1000)
    int lvl_line_t2c = time + extend_levels_bars * lvl_bar_ms3
    int as_line_t1 = int(nz(as_prev_start_t, nz(as_start_t, time)))
    if show_levels_master and show_as_hl
        if na(as_h_line)
            as_h_line := line.new(as_line_t1, ASDH, lvl_line_t2c, ASDH, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_as_h, width=as_hl_width, style=line.style_dashed)
        else
            line.set_x1(as_h_line, as_line_t1)
            line.set_y1(as_h_line, ASDH)
            line.set_x2(as_h_line, lvl_line_t2c)
            line.set_y2(as_h_line, ASDH)
            line.set_color(as_h_line, col_as_h)
            line.set_width(as_h_line, as_hl_width)
        if show_level_name or show_level_price
            if na(as_h_lbl)
                as_h_lbl := label.new(lvl_line_t2c, ASDH, getLevelLabelText("AS-H", ASDH), xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=col_as_h, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
            else
                label.set_x(as_h_lbl, lvl_line_t2c)
                label.set_y(as_h_lbl, ASDH)
                label.set_text(as_h_lbl, getLevelLabelText("AS-H", ASDH))
                label.set_textcolor(as_h_lbl, col_as_h)
        if not na(ASDL)
            if na(as_l_line)
                as_l_line := line.new(as_line_t1, ASDL, lvl_line_t2c, ASDL, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_as_l, width=as_hl_width, style=line.style_dashed)
            else
                line.set_x1(as_l_line, as_line_t1)
                line.set_y1(as_l_line, ASDL)
                line.set_x2(as_l_line, lvl_line_t2c)
                line.set_y2(as_l_line, ASDL)
                line.set_color(as_l_line, col_as_l)
                line.set_width(as_l_line, as_hl_width)
            if show_level_name or show_level_price
                if na(as_l_lbl)
                    as_l_lbl := label.new(lvl_line_t2c, ASDL, getLevelLabelText("AS-L", ASDL), xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=col_as_l, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
                else
                    label.set_x(as_l_lbl, lvl_line_t2c)
                    label.set_y(as_l_lbl, ASDL)
                    label.set_text(as_l_lbl, getLevelLabelText("AS-L", ASDL))
                    label.set_textcolor(as_l_lbl, col_as_l)

// ===========================
// Opening Range — ET session opens: NY 9:30, London 3:00, Asian 20:00; first or_minutes from each (HALO-style per session)
// ===========================
int or_ms = or_minutes * 60000
ny_or_open = timestamp(ny_tz, year(time, ny_tz), month(time, ny_tz), dayofmonth(time, ny_tz), 9, 30)
ny_or_close = ny_or_open + or_ms
ny_or_active = time >= ny_or_open and time < ny_or_close
ln_or_open = timestamp(ny_tz, year(time, ny_tz), month(time, ny_tz), dayofmonth(time, ny_tz), 3, 0)
ln_or_close = ln_or_open + or_ms
ln_or_active = time >= ln_or_open and time < ln_or_close
as_or_open = f_asia_or_open_ms()
as_or_close = as_or_open + or_ms
as_or_active = time >= as_or_open and time < as_or_close

var float ORHigh_NY = na
var float ORLow_NY = na
var float ORHigh_LN = na
var float ORLow_LN = na
var float ORHigh_AS = na
var float ORLow_AS = na

if or_ny and ny_or_active and not ny_or_active[1]
    ORHigh_NY := na
    ORLow_NY := na
if or_london and ln_or_active and not ln_or_active[1]
    ORHigh_LN := na
    ORLow_LN := na
if or_asian and as_or_active and not as_or_active[1]
    ORHigh_AS := na
    ORLow_AS := na

if or_ny and ny_or_active
    ORHigh_NY := na(ORHigh_NY) ? high : math.max(ORHigh_NY, high)
    ORLow_NY := na(ORLow_NY) ? low : math.min(ORLow_NY, low)
if or_london and ln_or_active
    ORHigh_LN := na(ORHigh_LN) ? high : math.max(ORHigh_LN, high)
    ORLow_LN := na(ORLow_LN) ? low : math.min(ORLow_LN, low)
if or_asian and as_or_active
    ORHigh_AS := na(ORHigh_AS) ? high : math.max(ORHigh_AS, high)
    ORLow_AS := na(ORLow_AS) ? low : math.min(ORLow_AS, low)

ny_done = or_ny and not ny_or_active and not na(ORHigh_NY)

// Startup / Connected alert: fires exactly once, the moment the script enters real-time mode —
// same "instant discovery" pattern CBC Wire uses (var one-shot guard + barstate.isrealtime).
var bool cbc_startup_sent = false
bool cbc_startup_new = not cbc_startup_sent and barstate.isrealtime
if cbc_startup_new
    cbc_startup_sent := true
ln_done = or_london and not ln_or_active and not na(ORHigh_LN)
as_done = or_asian and not as_or_active and not na(ORHigh_AS)

// ===========================
// Level Proximity Alerts: two-pass scan (any CROSSING always beats any APPROACHING), ported
// from CBC Wire. Runs every bar (top-level, like the rest of this script's state tracking) so
// the "var" latch below persists correctly; the actual alert() firing is gated separately by
// alert_on_level_proximity further down. Uses whichever session's OR is currently active,
// matching the "Opening range" alert field.
// ===========================
string prox_mkt = f_market_session_et()
float prox_or_hi = prox_mkt == "Asian" ? ORHigh_AS : prox_mkt == "London" ? ORHigh_LN : ORHigh_NY
float prox_or_lo = prox_mkt == "Asian" ? ORLow_AS : prox_mkt == "London" ? ORLow_LN : ORLow_NY
bool prox_or_done = prox_mkt == "Asian" ? as_done : prox_mkt == "London" ? ln_done : ny_done

// Level Proximity: 10 non-pivot levels (was 9; v4.27 added HTF EMA 200) packed into parallel
// arrays and looped (was 18 near-identical sequential if-blocks, one per level per pass) to help
// with Pine's total compiled token ceiling (CE10117). Pivots are kept as their own 2 blocks below,
// not loop-friendly, since each needs its own one-shot dedup state (prox_alerted_ph/pl) that
// doesn't fit this pattern.
var float[] lp_vals = array.new_float(10, na)
var string[] lp_names = array.new_string(10, "")
var bool[] lp_enabled = array.new_bool(10, false)
array.set(lp_vals, 0, vwap_value)
array.set(lp_names, 0, "VWAP")
array.set(lp_enabled, 0, prox_check_vwap)
array.set(lp_vals, 1, ema20)
array.set(lp_names, 1, "EMA 20")
array.set(lp_enabled, 1, prox_check_ema20)
array.set(lp_vals, 2, ema200)
array.set(lp_names, 2, "EMA 200")
array.set(lp_enabled, 2, prox_check_ema200)
array.set(lp_vals, 3, bb_above)
array.set(lp_names, 3, "Bill Breaker " + str.tostring(bb_above, "#"))
array.set(lp_enabled, 3, prox_check_billbreaker)
array.set(lp_vals, 4, bb_below)
array.set(lp_names, 4, "Bill Breaker " + str.tostring(bb_below, "#"))
array.set(lp_enabled, 4, prox_check_billbreaker)
array.set(lp_vals, 5, YDH)
array.set(lp_names, 5, "YDH")
array.set(lp_enabled, 5, prox_check_ydhl)
array.set(lp_vals, 6, YDL)
array.set(lp_names, 6, "YDL")
array.set(lp_enabled, 6, prox_check_ydhl)
array.set(lp_vals, 7, prox_or_hi)
array.set(lp_names, 7, "ORB High")
array.set(lp_enabled, 7, prox_check_orb and prox_or_done)
array.set(lp_vals, 8, prox_or_lo)
array.set(lp_names, 8, "ORB Low")
array.set(lp_enabled, 8, prox_check_orb and prox_or_done)
array.set(lp_vals, 9, htf_ema200)
array.set(lp_names, 9, "HTF EMA 200")
array.set(lp_enabled, 9, prox_check_htf_ema200)

// PASS 1: Crossing (highest priority)
string level_bullet = ""
for lpi = 0 to 9
    if level_bullet == "" and array.get(lp_enabled, lpi)
        level_bullet := f_prox_crossing(close, array.get(lp_vals, lpi), array.get(lp_names, lpi))
// Pivots — crossing only, one-shot per pivot value (matches CBC Wire).
if level_bullet == "" and prox_check_pivot and not na(prox_last_ph) and prox_alerted_ph != prox_last_ph
    float dist_ph = math.abs(close - prox_last_ph)
    if dist_ph <= prox_crossing_dist
        string dir_ph = close > prox_last_ph ? "above" : "below"
        level_bullet := "Pivot High Crossing (" + str.tostring(dist_ph, "#.##") + " points " + dir_ph + " Price, Pivot High: " + str.tostring(prox_last_ph, "#.##") + ")"
        prox_alerted_ph := prox_last_ph
if level_bullet == "" and prox_check_pivot and not na(prox_last_pl) and prox_alerted_pl != prox_last_pl
    float dist_pl = math.abs(close - prox_last_pl)
    if dist_pl <= prox_crossing_dist
        string dir_pl = close > prox_last_pl ? "above" : "below"
        level_bullet := "Pivot Low Crossing (" + str.tostring(dist_pl, "#.##") + " points " + dir_pl + " Price, Pivot Low: " + str.tostring(prox_last_pl, "#.##") + ")"
        prox_alerted_pl := prox_last_pl

// PASS 2: Approaching (only checked if PASS 1 found nothing at all)
for lpi2 = 0 to 9
    if level_bullet == "" and array.get(lp_enabled, lpi2)
        level_bullet := f_prox_approaching(close, array.get(lp_vals, lpi2), array.get(lp_names, lpi2))

// Latch — one alert per "visit", suppressing repeats while price stays in the same level's
// band. Latch key is just the level NAME portion (before " Crossing ("/" Approaching ("), so
// crossing/approaching flip-flopping right at the 0.5pt boundary doesn't refire — same
// technique as CBC Wire, just implemented as a substring match instead of a pipe-split.
f_prox_latch_name(string bullet) =>
    string result = bullet
    int sep_c = str.pos(bullet, " Crossing (")
    int sep_a = str.pos(bullet, " Approaching (")
    if not na(sep_c)
        result := str.substring(bullet, 0, sep_c)
    else if not na(sep_a)
        result := str.substring(bullet, 0, sep_a)
    result

var string prox_latched_name = ""
string prox_this_name = level_bullet != "" ? f_prox_latch_name(level_bullet) : ""
if level_bullet == ""
    prox_latched_name := ""
// Hard floor on top of the visit-latch above: even if the latch resets early (e.g. price
// oscillating right across the Approaching-distance boundary re-triggers a "new visit" a few bars
// later for what's really the same visit), don't fire again until prox_cooldown_bars have passed
// since the last proximity alert actually fired.
var int prox_last_alert_bar = -1000000
bool prox_cooldown_ok = (bar_index - prox_last_alert_bar) >= prox_cooldown_bars
bool level_alert_new_visit = level_bullet != "" and prox_this_name != prox_latched_name and prox_cooldown_ok
if level_alert_new_visit
    prox_latched_name := prox_this_name
    prox_last_alert_bar := bar_index

// ===========================
// ORB Retest / "Break & Base" — recognizes the setup in three stages, tracked every bar (not just on
// the last bar) so the multi-bar state survives across history:
//   1) BREAKOUT  — a bar closes outside the session's completed Opening Range (don't chase this bar).
//   2) RETEST    — price later comes back and touches the broken level again, from the other side,
//                  within a tolerance zone sized off that session's own OR range ("the base").
//   3) CONFIRM   — a bar closes back in the breakout direction, i.e. the level held as new
//                  support (Long) or new resistance (Short).
// Whichever session (Asian/London/New York) is presently the reference is the one tracked, matching
// the Opening Range status row / alert body.
// ===========================
string orb_r_mkt = f_market_session_et()
float orb_r_hi = orb_r_mkt == "Asian" ? ORHigh_AS : orb_r_mkt == "London" ? ORHigh_LN : ORHigh_NY
float orb_r_lo = orb_r_mkt == "Asian" ? ORLow_AS : orb_r_mkt == "London" ? ORLow_LN : ORLow_NY
bool orb_r_on = (orb_r_mkt == "Asian" and or_asian) or (orb_r_mkt == "London" and or_london) or (orb_r_mkt == "New York" and or_ny)
bool orb_r_ready = orb_r_on and (orb_r_mkt == "Asian" ? as_done : orb_r_mkt == "London" ? ln_done : ny_done)

var bool orb_r_broke_up = false
var bool orb_r_broke_dn = false
var bool orb_r_retest_up_seen = false
var bool orb_r_retest_dn_seen = false
var int  orb_r_bars_since_up = 0
var int  orb_r_bars_since_dn = 0

// Reset all state whenever a NEW opening range window starts for whichever session is tracked —
// each session gets its own fresh breakout/retest cycle.
if (or_ny and ny_or_active and not ny_or_active[1]) or (or_london and ln_or_active and not ln_or_active[1]) or (or_asian and as_or_active and not as_or_active[1])
    orb_r_broke_up := false
    orb_r_broke_dn := false
    orb_r_retest_up_seen := false
    orb_r_retest_dn_seen := false
    orb_r_bars_since_up := 0
    orb_r_bars_since_dn := 0

float orb_r_rng = (orb_r_ready and not na(orb_r_hi) and not na(orb_r_lo)) ? (orb_r_hi - orb_r_lo) : na
float orb_r_zone = na(orb_r_rng) ? na : orb_r_rng * orb_retest_tolerance
float orb_r_min_break = na(orb_r_rng) ? na : orb_r_rng * orb_retest_min_break

// --- Bullish: breakout above ORB High -> retest of ORB High from above -> close back above confirms the base
if orb_r_ready and not na(orb_r_min_break) and close > orb_r_hi + orb_r_min_break
    orb_r_broke_up := true
    orb_r_bars_since_up := 0
if orb_r_broke_up and not orb_r_retest_up_seen
    orb_r_bars_since_up := orb_r_bars_since_up + 1
    if low <= orb_r_hi + orb_r_zone and low >= orb_r_hi - orb_r_zone
        orb_r_retest_up_seen := true
bool orb_r_fail_up = orb_r_broke_up and low < orb_r_lo
if orb_r_fail_up
    // Full round-trip back below the range invalidates the setup — this is "ORB Flip", not a stall.
    orb_r_broke_up := false
    orb_r_retest_up_seen := false

// "ORB Fail from +" — never confirmed AND never flipped before the retest window expired, stuck
// back inside the range. Checked after orb_r_fail_up above, so a same-bar Flip already reset
// orb_r_broke_up to false and takes priority (this condition reads orb_r_broke_up AFTER that reset).
bool orb_r_timeout_up = orb_r_broke_up and orb_r_bars_since_up > orb_retest_max_bars
if orb_r_timeout_up
    orb_r_broke_up := false
    orb_r_retest_up_seen := false

bool orb_r_confirm_up = orb_r_broke_up and orb_r_retest_up_seen and close > orb_r_hi and orb_r_bars_since_up <= orb_retest_max_bars
bool orb_r_enter_long = orb_r_confirm_up and not orb_r_confirm_up[1]
if orb_r_enter_long
    orb_r_broke_up := false
    orb_r_retest_up_seen := false

// --- Bearish: breakout below ORB Low -> retest of ORB Low from below -> close back below confirms the base
if orb_r_ready and not na(orb_r_min_break) and close < orb_r_lo - orb_r_min_break
    orb_r_broke_dn := true
    orb_r_bars_since_dn := 0
if orb_r_broke_dn and not orb_r_retest_dn_seen
    orb_r_bars_since_dn := orb_r_bars_since_dn + 1
    if high >= orb_r_lo - orb_r_zone and high <= orb_r_lo + orb_r_zone
        orb_r_retest_dn_seen := true
bool orb_r_fail_dn = orb_r_broke_dn and high > orb_r_hi
if orb_r_fail_dn
    orb_r_broke_dn := false
    orb_r_retest_dn_seen := false

// "ORB Fail from -" — bearish-side mirror of orb_r_timeout_up above.
bool orb_r_timeout_dn = orb_r_broke_dn and orb_r_bars_since_dn > orb_retest_max_bars
if orb_r_timeout_dn
    orb_r_broke_dn := false
    orb_r_retest_dn_seen := false

bool orb_r_confirm_dn = orb_r_broke_dn and orb_r_retest_dn_seen and close < orb_r_lo and orb_r_bars_since_dn <= orb_retest_max_bars
bool orb_r_enter_short = orb_r_confirm_dn and not orb_r_confirm_dn[1]
if orb_r_enter_short
    orb_r_broke_dn := false
    orb_r_retest_dn_seen := false

// On-chart markers — SQUARES (not triangles), specifically so these can never be mistaken for a
// CBC flip triangle at a glance or when bars are tight — the CBC LTF/HTF flip triangles are blank
// (no text) and use similarly-red colors for their bearish/short case, so shape is now the only
// thing that reliably tells the two families apart regardless of zoom level or color perception.
// Offset the same way the CBC flip triangles are (above/below the bar).
float orb_r_mk_up_y = low - (high - low) * 0.30
float orb_r_mk_dn_y = high + (high - low) * 0.30
// Genuinely hollow this time: box.new() has real separate border_color/bgcolor params (unlike
// label.new(), where "color" is the shape's solid fill with no way to make just the outline
// visible) — bgcolor=color(na) here is truly transparent, showing the chart through the middle,
// not just revealing whatever's behind it in the label z-stack the way v3.69's attempt did.
// Sized at roughly HALF the v3.67 footprint (half the price-offset, 1-bar wide instead of a
// fixed pixel size — box coordinates are data-space, not pixel-space, so width scales with
// zoom/timeframe rather than staying visually fixed the way label sizes do).
float orb_r_box_half = (high - low) * 0.15
if show_orb_retest_markers
    if orb_r_enter_long
        box.new(left=bar_index - 1, right=bar_index, top=orb_r_mk_up_y + orb_r_box_half, bottom=orb_r_mk_up_y - orb_r_box_half, border_color=col_orb_retest_success, border_width=2, bgcolor=color(na))
        label.new(x=bar_index, y=orb_r_mk_up_y, text="", tooltip=tt_orb_r_success, color=color.new(color.black, 100), style=label.style_circle, size=size.tiny)
    if orb_r_enter_short
        box.new(left=bar_index - 1, right=bar_index, top=orb_r_mk_dn_y + orb_r_box_half, bottom=orb_r_mk_dn_y - orb_r_box_half, border_color=col_orb_retest_success, border_width=2, bgcolor=color(na))
        label.new(x=bar_index, y=orb_r_mk_dn_y, text="", tooltip=tt_orb_r_success, color=color.new(color.black, 100), style=label.style_circle, size=size.tiny)
    // Failed retest — price fully round-tripped through the opposite side of the range before ever
    // confirming the base. Marked in red regardless of direction, at the bar where the round-trip
    // itself happened (the bar whose low/high broke the far side), not at some later bar.
    if orb_r_fail_up
        box.new(left=bar_index - 1, right=bar_index, top=orb_r_mk_dn_y + orb_r_box_half, bottom=orb_r_mk_dn_y - orb_r_box_half, border_color=col_orb_retest_fail, border_width=2, bgcolor=color(na))
        label.new(x=bar_index, y=orb_r_mk_dn_y, text="", tooltip=tt_orb_r_fail, color=color.new(color.black, 100), style=label.style_circle, size=size.tiny)
    if orb_r_fail_dn
        box.new(left=bar_index - 1, right=bar_index, top=orb_r_mk_up_y + orb_r_box_half, bottom=orb_r_mk_up_y - orb_r_box_half, border_color=col_orb_retest_fail, border_width=2, bgcolor=color(na))
        label.new(x=bar_index, y=orb_r_mk_up_y, text="", tooltip=tt_orb_r_fail, color=color.new(color.black, 100), style=label.style_circle, size=size.tiny)
    // Stalled/timed-out — never confirmed, never flipped, retest window simply expired with price stuck
    // back inside the range. Plotted on the SAME side as the matching Pass marker (not the opposite side
    // like Flip) since nothing reversed — the attempt just fizzled where it started.
    if orb_r_timeout_up
        box.new(left=bar_index - 1, right=bar_index, top=orb_r_mk_up_y + orb_r_box_half, bottom=orb_r_mk_up_y - orb_r_box_half, border_color=col_orb_retest_stall, border_width=2, bgcolor=color(na))
        label.new(x=bar_index, y=orb_r_mk_up_y, text="", tooltip=tt_orb_r_stall, color=color.new(color.black, 100), style=label.style_circle, size=size.tiny)
    if orb_r_timeout_dn
        box.new(left=bar_index - 1, right=bar_index, top=orb_r_mk_dn_y + orb_r_box_half, bottom=orb_r_mk_dn_y - orb_r_box_half, border_color=col_orb_retest_stall, border_width=2, bgcolor=color(na))
        label.new(x=bar_index, y=orb_r_mk_dn_y, text="", tooltip=tt_orb_r_stall, color=color.new(color.black, 100), style=label.style_circle, size=size.tiny)


bool show_or_hl_vis = show_levels_master and show_or_hl
// Shade ON only while inside that session's OWN time window — clearing the moment the NEXT session
// begins. So Asia's fill clears at 03:00 (London start); London's clears at 09:30 (NY start); New York's
// clears at 20:00 (Asia start). Once that clears, the fill disappears and only the dashed reference line
// (plotORNYH/L etc. below) keeps extending, using the Line color/width inputs, until that session's next
// occurrence replaces it.
bool ny_shade_on = orb_shade_mode == "During OR formation only" ? ny_or_active : ny_done and is_ny_full_sess
bool ln_shade_on = orb_shade_mode == "During OR formation only" ? ln_or_active : ln_done and is_ln_sess
bool as_shade_on = orb_shade_mode == "During OR formation only" ? as_or_active : as_done and is_as_sess
plotORNYH = plot(show_or_hl_vis and ny_done ? ORHigh_NY : na, color=col_orb_ny_line, linewidth=orb_ny_line_w, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle=plot.linestyle_dashed, title="OR NY High")
plot(show_or_hl_vis and ny_done ? ORLow_NY : na, color=col_orb_ny_line, linewidth=orb_ny_line_w, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle=plot.linestyle_dashed, title="OR NY Low")
plot(show_or_hl_vis and show_ln_orb_hl and ln_done ? ORHigh_LN : na, color=col_orb_ln_line, linewidth=orb_ln_line_w, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle=plot.linestyle_dashed, title="OR London High")
plot(show_or_hl_vis and show_ln_orb_hl and ln_done ? ORLow_LN : na, color=col_orb_ln_line, linewidth=orb_ln_line_w, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle=plot.linestyle_dashed, title="OR London Low")
plot(show_or_hl_vis and as_done ? ORHigh_AS : na, color=col_orb_as_line, linewidth=orb_as_line_w, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle=plot.linestyle_dashed, title="OR Asian High")
plot(show_or_hl_vis and as_done ? ORLow_AS : na, color=col_orb_as_line, linewidth=orb_as_line_w, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle=plot.linestyle_dashed, title="OR Asian Low")
// Session-scoped shading via box.new() instead of plot()+fill() — boxes don't count against the 64-plot
// budget, unlike the plot-pair-per-fill approach this replaced. Each box grows rightward bar-by-bar
// while shading is "on" per orb_shade_mode below — either during the OR formation window itself, or
// from the moment it closes through to the end of that session — and gets deleted the instant that
// window ends, leaving only the dashed reference lines above, which keep extending on their own.

    else

    else

        box.set_top(ln_or_shade_box, ORHigh_LN)
        box.set_bottom(ln_or_shade_box, ORLow_LN)

if show_or_hl_vis and as_shade_on
    if na(as_or_shade_box)
        as_or_shade_box := box.new(left=time, top=ORHigh_AS, right=time, bottom=ORLow_AS, xloc=xloc.bar_time, bgcolor=col_orb_as_fill, border_color=color.new(color.white, 100))
    else
        box.set_right(as_or_shade_box, time)
        box.set_top(as_or_shade_box, ORHigh_AS)
        box.set_bottom(as_or_shade_box, ORLow_AS)
else
    box.delete(as_or_shade_box)
    as_or_shade_box := na
plotORNYActH = plot(show_or_hl_vis and or_ny and ny_or_active ? ORHigh_NY : na, color=col_orb_ny_lbl, linewidth=1, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle=plot.linestyle_dotted, title="OR NY Active High")
plotORNYActL = plot(show_or_hl_vis and or_ny and ny_or_active ? ORLow_NY : na, color=col_orb_ny_lbl, linewidth=1, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle=plot.linestyle_dotted, title="OR NY Active Low")
plotORLNActH = plot(show_or_hl_vis and show_ln_orb_hl and or_london and ln_or_active ? ORHigh_LN : na, color=col_orb_ln_lbl, linewidth=1, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle=plot.linestyle_dotted, title="OR London Active High")
plotORLNActL = plot(show_or_hl_vis and show_ln_orb_hl and or_london and ln_or_active ? ORLow_LN : na, color=col_orb_ln_lbl, linewidth=1, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle=plot.linestyle_dotted, title="OR London Active Low")
plotORASActH = plot(show_or_hl_vis and or_asian and as_or_active ? ORHigh_AS : na, color=col_orb_as_lbl, linewidth=1, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle=plot.linestyle_dotted, title="OR Asian Active High")
plotORASActL = plot(show_or_hl_vis and or_asian and as_or_active ? ORLow_AS : na, color=col_orb_as_lbl, linewidth=1, style=plot.style_steplinebr, linestyle=plot.linestyle_dotted, title="OR Asian Active Low")
fill(plotORNYActH, plotORNYActL, color=col_orb_ny_fill, title="OR NY Active Fill")
fill(plotORLNActH, plotORLNActL, color=col_orb_ln_fill, title="OR London Active Fill")
fill(plotORASActH, plotORASActL, color=col_orb_as_fill, title="OR Asian Active Fill")

    orb_ny_l_lbl := na

                orb_ny_l_lbl := label.new(or_lbl_x, y_ny_l, getLevelLabelText("NY ORB-L", y_ny_l), color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=col_orb_ny_lbl, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
            else
                label.set_x(orb_ny_l_lbl, or_lbl_x)
                label.set_y(orb_ny_l_lbl, y_ny_l)
                label.set_text(orb_ny_l_lbl, getLevelLabelText("NY ORB-L", y_ny_l))
        else

            orb_ny_l_lbl := na
    // London labels
    if not (or_london and show_ln_orb_hl)
        label.delete(orb_ln_h_lbl)

        orb_ln_l_lbl := na
    else

            else
                label.set_x(orb_ln_l_lbl, or_lbl_x)

            else

                label.set_y(orb_as_h_lbl, y_as_h)

                orb_as_l_lbl := label.new(or_lbl_x, y_as_l, getLevelLabelText("AS ORB-L", y_as_l), color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=col_orb_as_lbl, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
            else
                label.set_x(orb_as_l_lbl, or_lbl_x)
                label.set_y(orb_as_l_lbl, y_as_l)
