            label.delete(orb_as_l_lbl)
            orb_as_l_lbl := na

// CBC state logic: strict prior-bar breaks only (AsiaRoo / HOS-style: close < low[1] / close > high[1]).
f_cbc_state() =>
    var bool state = false
    state := state[1]
    bool bear_flip_lvl = close < low[1]
    bool bull_flip_lvl = close > high[1]
    if state and bear_flip_lvl
        state := false
    if not state and bull_flip_lvl
        state := true
    state

// Current timeframe CBC logic
cbc = f_cbc_state()

// Counts confirmed bars since a bool state series last flipped, on that series' OWN timeframe: 1 on the
// flip bar itself (it's the first candle of the new direction), 2 on the next bar after the flip, 3 on the
// one after that, etc. Used directly for the LTF count (cbc, the chart's own timeframe) and, via
// request.security with the same [1]/lookahead=off trick as htf_cbc_labels_f, for the HTF count on its own native bars.
f_bars_since_flip(bool st) =>
    var int cnt = 0
    bool flip = st != st[1]
    cnt := flip ? 1 : cnt + 1
    cnt

ltf_bars_since_flip = f_bars_since_flip(cbc)
// v4.50: persistent "has a real LTF flip happened yet" latch — true from the flip bar onward through
// every subsequent bar in that direction, only re-armed (still true) by the next flip. Replaces a
// fixed bar-count recency cap for cf_f1_flip below: valid for the whole post-flip life, not just the
// first few bars.
var bool ltf_has_flipped = false
if ltf_bars_since_flip == 1
    ltf_has_flipped := true

// CBC flips (same as ThinkScript BullFlip / BearFlip)
bull_flip = cbc and not cbc[1]
bear_flip = not cbc and cbc[1]
any_flip = bull_flip or bear_flip
// CBC Reversal: prior bar flipped and this bar flips again (double-flip; highlight the second candle)
reversal = any_flip and any_flip[1]

// Timestamp (that bar's own close time) of the last confirmed LTF CBC flip — for the "CBC (LTF) last flip"
// display in the Status table / Historical Bar Panel / alert email.
var int ltf_last_flip_t = na
var bool ltf_last_flip_bull = bool(na)
if bull_flip or bear_flip
    ltf_last_flip_t := time_close
    ltf_last_flip_bull := bull_flip

// Counts how many times price has moved INTO the zone between EMA9(LTF) and EMA20(LTF) since the last LTF flip.
// Same rules as the HTF version: resets to 0 on every LTF flip, only counts the transition into the zone —
// consecutive bars that stay inside it don't add to the count.
float ltf_ema_lo = math.min(ema9, ema20)
float ltf_ema_hi = math.max(ema9, ema20)
bool ltf_between_now = close >= ltf_ema_lo and close <= ltf_ema_hi
bool ltf_between_enter = ltf_between_now and not ltf_between_now[1]
var int ltf_between_count = 0
if bull_flip or bear_flip
    ltf_between_count := 0
if ltf_between_enter
    ltf_between_count := ltf_between_count + 1

// When Show Reversal is off, Reversal bars use normal Bear/Bull styling
bear_plain = bear_flip and (not reversal or not show_reversal)
bull_plain = bull_flip and (not reversal or not show_reversal)
bear_reversal_mk = show_reversal and bear_flip and reversal
bull_reversal_mk = show_reversal and bull_flip and reversal

// CBC triangles — constant #hex per row (Style tab).
cbc_flip_arrow_sz = cbc_flip_triangle_size == "Tiny" ? size.tiny : cbc_flip_triangle_size == "Small" ? size.small : cbc_flip_triangle_size == "Normal" ? size.normal : cbc_flip_triangle_size == "Large" ? size.large : size.huge
float ltf_arrow_up_y = low - (high - low) * 0.15
float ltf_arrow_dn_y = high + (high - low) * 0.15
if bear_plain
    label.new(x=bar_index, y=ltf_arrow_dn_y, text="", color=col_cbc_short, style=label.style_triangledown, size=cbc_flip_arrow_sz)
if bull_plain
    label.new(x=bar_index, y=ltf_arrow_up_y, text="", color=col_cbc_long, style=label.style_triangleup, size=cbc_flip_arrow_sz)
if bear_reversal_mk
    label.new(x=bar_index, y=ltf_arrow_dn_y, text="", color=#F9CB00, style=label.style_triangledown, size=cbc_flip_arrow_sz)
if bull_reversal_mk
    label.new(x=bar_index, y=ltf_arrow_up_y, text="", color=#F9CB00, style=label.style_triangleup, size=cbc_flip_arrow_sz)

// HTF label anchor on LTF: bar in completed HTF window whose low (LONG) or high (SHORT) is closest to HTF signal extreme; tie → larger offset (earlier bar).
f_htf_anchor_long(float sig_lo, int t_open, int t_close, int max_off) =>
    int best_off = 0
    float best_err = na
    bool found = false
    for off = 0 to max_off
        if bar_index >= off
            int ot = time[off]
            if not na(ot) and not na(t_open) and not na(t_close) and ot >= t_open and ot <= t_close
                float err = math.abs(low[off] - sig_lo)
                if not found or err < best_err or (err == best_err and off > best_off)
                    best_err := err
                    best_off := off
                    found := true
    int off_use = found ? best_off : 0
    [bar_index - off_use, low[off_use] - (high[off_use] - low[off_use]) * 0.3]

f_htf_anchor_short(float sig_hi, int t_open, int t_close, int max_off) =>
    int best_off = 0
    float best_err = na
    bool found = false
    for off = 0 to max_off
        if bar_index >= off
            int ot = time[off]
            if not na(ot) and not na(t_open) and not na(t_close) and ot >= t_open and ot <= t_close
                float err = math.abs(high[off] - sig_hi)
                if not found or err < best_err or (err == best_err and off > best_off)
                    best_err := err
                    best_off := off
                    found := true
    int off_use = found ? best_off : 0
    [bar_index - off_use, high[off_use] + (high[off_use] - low[off_use]) * 0.3]

// Background colour
// bgcolor(show_background ? (cbc and not cbc[1] ? color.rgb(255, 235, 59, 71)  : not cbc and cbc[1] ? color.rgb(5, 185, 240, 59) : na) : na) 

// Higher timeframe CBC logic (native HTF OHLC CBC state) + prior HTF bar high/low for flip-level dash
[htf_cbc, htf_hi1, htf_lo1] = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), htf_timeframe, [f_cbc_state(), high[1], low[1]], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_on)
htf_cbc_labels_f = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), htf_timeframe, f_cbc_state()[1] ? 1.0 : 0.0, lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)
// Confirmed HTF bars since the last confirmed HTF flip, counted on the HTF's own native bars (not LTF bars).
htf_bars_since_flip = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), htf_timeframe, f_bars_since_flip(f_cbc_state())[1], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)
// v4.46: widened this same call with 2 more tuple elements (sig_htf_vol/sig_htf_vol_avg) for the new
// Volume Spike confluence factor below — no new request.security call, this one already ran every bar.
// v4.64 widened this with 2 more elements (sig_htf_hi3/sig_htf_lo3) for the generic HTF Fair Value
// Gap feature; v4.66 reverted that alongside commenting out the rest of that feature (see below),
// since those 2 values had no other reader once it's disabled.
[sig_htf_lo, sig_htf_hi, sig_htf_cl, sig_htf_op, sig_htf_t_open, sig_htf_t_close, sig_htf_vol, sig_htf_vol_avg] = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), htf_timeframe, [low[1], high[1], close[1], open[1], time[1], time_close[1], volume[1], ta.sma(volume, htf_vol_avg_len)[1]], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)
// Identity of the CURRENT, still-forming HTF bar (its scheduled close time) — used to de-dup the
// early/unconfirmed alerts so the same still-open HTF bar + same live direction doesn't re-email on
// every LTF bar. Changes only when a new HTF bar starts forming, unlike sig_htf_t_close (the last
// CLOSED HTF bar's close time, used for the confirmed-alert de-dup above/below). v4.16: also fetches
// this same live bar's OPEN time (htf_live_t_open) in the same call — no new request.security added —
// so elapsed-time-into-the-bar can be computed below for the Agreement-Early time gate.
[htf_live_t_open, htf_live_t_close] = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), htf_timeframe, [time, time_close], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_on)
// v4.16/v4.17 — Both early/unconfirmed HTF alerts ("HTF CBC Flip - Early" and "LTF + HTF Agreement -
// Early") are only allowed to fire in the LAST 20% of the current, still-forming HTF bar (e.g. minutes
// 12-15 of a 15m HTF), so neither fires the moment the HTF bar opens — both wait until that bar is
// nearly done forming (less likely to revert by close). timenow is the actual wall-clock time at each
// realtime evaluation (unlike `time`, which stays fixed at the bar's own open) — compared against this
// live bar's own open/scheduled-close window. On historical bars timenow is just "whenever the script
// last compiled," which always reads as past the gate — harmless, since alert() only ever actually
// fires in realtime anyway.
// Multiplication form (not division) so a zero/negative span can't throw or need a guard clause —
// if htf_live_t_close <= htf_live_t_open, the right side is <= 0 and the left side (elapsed) is always
// >= 0, so the comparison is just naturally true, no separate "or" branch needed.
bool htf_early_time_ok = (timenow - htf_live_t_open) >= 0.8 * (htf_live_t_close - htf_live_t_open)
// Signed height (close − open) of that closed HTF candle — the one whose close triggered the flip. Down candle -> negative, up candle -> positive.
float sig_htf_candle_h = sig_htf_cl - sig_htf_op
string sig_htf_candle_h_s = (sig_htf_candle_h >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sig_htf_candle_h, "#.##")
// Retracement% — how much of the candle's move got given back before close, direction-aware: for a DOWN
// candle, measured as distance ABOVE the low (shown negative — 0% means no retracement, it closed right
// at the low; a bigger negative magnitude means more of the down move got retraced/given back by close).
// For an UP candle, measured as distance BELOW the high (shown positive — 0% means no retracement, closed
// right at the high). Same "down = negative, up = positive" sign convention as Height above.
float sig_htf_range = sig_htf_hi - sig_htf_lo
bool sig_htf_is_down = sig_htf_candle_h < 0
float sig_htf_retrace_pct = (na(sig_htf_range) or sig_htf_range == 0) ? na : (sig_htf_is_down ? -1 * (sig_htf_cl - sig_htf_lo) / sig_htf_range * 100 : (sig_htf_hi - sig_htf_cl) / sig_htf_range * 100)
string sig_htf_close_pct_s = na(sig_htf_retrace_pct) ? "—" : (sig_htf_retrace_pct >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sig_htf_retrace_pct, "#.#") + "%"

// The flipped HTF candle's own high/low, frozen at the moment of each confirmed HTF flip and held constant
// until the NEXT HTF flip. (Local flip-detection here since htf_flip_bull/htf_flip_bear aren't defined
// until later in the script, but this needs to be available earlier for the on-chart BRSG lines below.)
bool htf_just_flipped_for_zone = not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and not na(htf_cbc_labels_f[1]) and htf_cbc_labels_f != htf_cbc_labels_f[1]
var float htf_flip_candle_hi = na
var float htf_flip_candle_lo = na
// v4.46: same freeze, 2 more fields — the flip candle's own volume and its trailing average
// (sig_htf_vol/sig_htf_vol_avg, pulled via the widened request.security tuple above), feeding the
// new Volume Spike confluence factor.
var float htf_flip_candle_vol = na
var float htf_flip_candle_vol_avg = na
if htf_just_flipped_for_zone
    htf_flip_candle_hi := sig_htf_hi
    htf_flip_candle_lo := sig_htf_lo
    htf_flip_candle_vol := sig_htf_vol
    htf_flip_candle_vol_avg := sig_htf_vol_avg

// Fair Value Gap (FVG) of the HTF flip candle — the classic 3-candle ICT imbalance, with the flip
// candle itself as the middle bar. Candle-before (prev) is read off sig_htf_hi/lo shifted back one
// LTF bar at the exact instant of the flip: sig_htf_hi/lo has just rolled forward to the flip candle
// on THIS bar, so its value one LTF bar earlier is still the candle before it — no new
// request.security call, same freeze trigger as htf_flip_candle_hi/lo just above. Candle-after
// (next) can't exist until one more HTF bar closes, so it's captured once htf_bars_since_flip
// (already computed above) reaches 1, and reset to na on every new flip so a stale value from the
// previous flip cycle can never leak into this one.
var float htf_fvg_prev_hi = na
var float htf_fvg_prev_lo = na
var float htf_fvg_next_hi = na
var float htf_fvg_next_lo = na
var bool htf_fvg_bull = bool(na)
if htf_just_flipped_for_zone
    htf_fvg_prev_hi := sig_htf_hi[1]
    htf_fvg_prev_lo := sig_htf_lo[1]
    htf_fvg_next_hi := na
    htf_fvg_next_lo := na
    htf_fvg_bull := htf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0
if htf_bars_since_flip == 1
    htf_fvg_next_hi := sig_htf_hi
    htf_fvg_next_lo := sig_htf_lo
bool htf_fvg_ready = not na(htf_fvg_prev_hi) and not na(htf_fvg_prev_lo) and not na(htf_fvg_next_hi) and not na(htf_fvg_next_lo)
bool htf_fvg_exists = htf_fvg_ready and (htf_fvg_bull ? htf_fvg_next_lo > htf_fvg_prev_hi : htf_fvg_prev_lo > htf_fvg_next_hi)
float htf_fvg_top = htf_fvg_exists ? (htf_fvg_bull ? htf_fvg_next_lo : htf_fvg_prev_lo) : na
float htf_fvg_bot = htf_fvg_exists ? (htf_fvg_bull ? htf_fvg_prev_hi : htf_fvg_next_hi) : na
// v4.68: persistent "has THIS flip's own FVG been filled yet" latch, per follow-up ("#9 uses fair
// value gaps, once filled they are invalid"). Replaces the old htf_has_flipped one-way latch, which
// was set true on the first-ever HTF flip and never reset — so cf_f9 stayed true forever after that,
// regardless of what price did afterward. Cleared on every new flip (a fresh, as-yet-untouched gap),
// then latches true the first time price trades back into the gap zone (same touch condition Zone/
// cf_f5 already uses against this same htf_fvg_top/bot) and stays true — i.e. the gap stays "filled"
// — until the next flip opens a new one.
var bool htf_fvg_filled = false
if htf_just_flipped_for_zone
    htf_fvg_filled := false
if htf_fvg_exists and low <= htf_fvg_top and high >= htf_fvg_bot
    htf_fvg_filled := true
// v4.64: generic Fair Value Gap boxes — runs every bar (unlike the barstate.islast-only render
// functions used for the Status table/Fib Zone/etc.), since each individual gap must be captured and
// boxed at the historical bar it actually forms on, then persist (extending right) across every
// later bar until either mitigated or bumped out by the per-side cap. `var box[]`/`array.new_box()`
// (bracket-style typed arrays) match this file's own existing convention (piv_lines/piv_lbls above
// use `var line[]`/`array.new_line()`), not the newer generic `array<box>` syntax, which has no
// precedent in this file. Both loops guard `array.size(boxes) > 0` first — looping `to
// array.size(boxes) - 1` on an empty array (size 0, so -1) would otherwise run backward through i=0
// and i=-1 (Pine's for-loop auto-reverses when start > end), and array.get() on an empty array errors.
// v4.66: CE10117 fix (compiled at 100566/100256, over by 310) — simplified this function to close
// the gap. Two changes, both a genuine simplification (not just a budget hack): (1) dropped the
// parallel tops[]/bots[] float arrays entirely — box.get_top()/box.get_bottom() already return a
// box's own stored bounds, so tracking a duplicate copy in 2 more arrays (with its own push/remove/
// shift at every call site) was pure redundancy. (2) switched from manually calling box.set_right()
// in a per-bar loop to extend=extend.right at creation, which makes each box auto-extend to the
// current right edge on its own — this is also a more correct match for "unfilled gap extends until
// mitigated" than a loop re-asserting the same thing every bar. Net effect: one array instead of
// three, and one fewer loop. No behavior change — still last-N-per-side, still auto-removed on fill.
// f_manage_fvg(bool newGap, float gapTop, float gapBot, int leftBar, bool isBull, color col, int maxCount) =>
//     var box[] boxes = array.new_box(0, na)
//     // Mitigation: a bull gap is filled once price trades back down through its bottom edge; a bear
//     // gap once price trades back up through its top edge. Checked against the CURRENT bar's real
//     // low/high (not a lagged HTF value), so an HTF-timeframe gap can be marked filled intrabar,
//     // without waiting for that HTF bar to formally close.
//     if array.size(boxes) > 0
//         for i = array.size(boxes) - 1 to 0
//             box b = array.get(boxes, i)
//             bool filled = isBull ? low <= box.get_bottom(b) : high >= box.get_top(b)
//             if filled
//                 box.delete(b)
//                 array.remove(boxes, i)
//     if newGap
//         box newId = box.new(left=leftBar, top=gapTop, bottom=gapBot, right=bar_index, border_color=col, bgcolor=color.new(col, 30), extend=extend.right)
//         array.push(boxes, newId)
//         if array.size(boxes) > maxCount
//             box.delete(array.shift(boxes))
//     true
//
// // LTF: classic 3-candle imbalance using this chart's own bars — candle A (2 bars back) vs candle C
// // (current), skipping candle B. Bull: A.high < C.low (gap = high[2] .. low). Bear: A.low > C.high
// // (gap = high .. low[2]).
// if fvg_show_ltf
//     f_manage_fvg(low > high[2], low, high[2], bar_index - 2, true, fvg_col_bull, fvg_max_count_ltf)
//     f_manage_fvg(high < low[2], low[2], high, bar_index - 2, false, fvg_col_bear, fvg_max_count_ltf)
//
// // HTF: same pattern, one HTF bar per "candle" (sig_htf_hi/lo = candle C = the last CLOSED HTF bar;
// // sig_htf_hi3/lo3 = candle A = 2 HTF bars before that). These request.security values stay constant
// // across every LTF bar within the same still-forming HTF bar, so fvg_htf_new_bar (same
// // "sig_htf_t_close != last-seen" idiom already used elsewhere in this file, e.g.
// // last_agree_long_htf_close_t) gates detection to fire exactly once per newly closed HTF bar, not
// // once per LTF bar.
// var int fvg_last_htf_check_t = -1
// bool fvg_htf_new_bar = sig_htf_t_close != fvg_last_htf_check_t
// if fvg_htf_new_bar
//     fvg_last_htf_check_t := sig_htf_t_close
// if fvg_show_htf
//     f_manage_fvg(fvg_htf_new_bar and sig_htf_lo > sig_htf_hi3, sig_htf_lo, sig_htf_hi3, bar_index - fvg_htf_box_bars, true, fvg_col_bull, fvg_max_count_htf)
//     f_manage_fvg(fvg_htf_new_bar and sig_htf_hi < sig_htf_lo3, sig_htf_lo3, sig_htf_hi, bar_index - fvg_htf_box_bars, false, fvg_col_bear, fvg_max_count_htf)
// v4.41: LTF-bar counter since the HTF flip — distinct from htf_bars_since_flip above (which counts
// HTF bars via request.security); this one counts plain LTF bars and feeds the "stop after N LTF bars
// post-flip" alert cutoff below. Resets on the same htf_just_flipped_for_zone trigger used to freeze
// the FVG candles above.
var int ltf_bars_since_htf_flip = 0
if htf_just_flipped_for_zone
    ltf_bars_since_htf_flip := 0
else
    ltf_bars_since_htf_flip := ltf_bars_since_htf_flip + 1

// BRSG (Buy Red Sell Green) zone bounds — anchored to the HTF candle that caused the LAST CONFIRMED HTF
// flip (frozen above), NOT the chart's own last candle. Stays fixed until the next HTF flip, at which
// point htf_flip_candle_hi/lo update and this zone recalculates off the new flipped candle. Used by the
// on-chart BRSG lines, the Status table / Historical Bar Panel "Buy/Sell Zone" row, and the alert email.
float brsg_zone_rng = htf_flip_candle_hi - htf_flip_candle_lo
float brsg_zone_top = na(brsg_zone_rng) ? na : htf_flip_candle_lo + brsg_zone_rng * brsg_high_mult
float brsg_zone_bot = na(brsg_zone_rng) ? na : htf_flip_candle_lo + brsg_zone_rng * brsg_low_mult

// HTF + LTF pivot detection (ta.pivothigh/low: classical N-left N-right). Replace-only persistence: a line stays until a NEW same-type pivot prints in the same timeframe.
[htf_piv_hi_raw, htf_piv_lo_raw, htf_piv_bar_t] = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), htf_timeframe, [ta.pivothigh(high, htf_piv_n, htf_piv_n), ta.pivotlow(low, htf_piv_n, htf_piv_n), time[htf_piv_n]], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)
ltf_piv_hi_raw = ta.pivothigh(high, ltf_piv_n, ltf_piv_n)
ltf_piv_lo_raw = ta.pivotlow(low, ltf_piv_n, ltf_piv_n)
int ltf_piv_bar_t = time[ltf_piv_n]

var float h_last_hi = na
var int   h_last_hi_t = na
var float h_last_lo = na
var int   h_last_lo_t = na
var float l_last_hi = na
var int   l_last_hi_t = na
var float l_last_lo = na
var int   l_last_lo_t = na

// Market structure — classifies each new confirmed pivot against the PREVIOUS same-type pivot:
// a fresh pivot high above the last one is a "Higher High", otherwise a "Lower High"; a fresh pivot
// low below the last one is a "Lower Low", otherwise a "Higher Low". Kept per timeframe (chart/LTF and
// HTF), since the two can disagree (e.g. LTF printing a Lower Low inside an HTF Higher Low pullback).
var string ltf_hi_struct = "—"
var string ltf_lo_struct = "—"
var string htf_hi_struct = "—"
var string htf_lo_struct = "—"
// v4.43: Break of Structure / Change of Character — a persistent HTF trend-state sitting on top of
// the HH/LH/HL/LL classifier just above. A fresh Higher High while the trend is already Bullish (or
// a fresh Lower Low while already Bearish) is a BOS (continuation). A fresh Higher High while the
// trend was Bearish (or a fresh Lower Low while it was Bullish) is a CHoCH (reversal) — the trend
// flips. The very first pivot ever classified just establishes the trend baseline, not a "break" of
// anything yet. No new request.security call — reuses htf_piv_hi_raw/htf_piv_lo_raw, already pulled
// above for the plain HH/LH/HL/LL classification.
var string htf_struct_trend = "—"
bool htf_choch_bull = false
bool htf_bos_bull = false
bool htf_choch_bear = false
bool htf_bos_bear = false

if not na(htf_piv_hi_raw)
    if not na(h_last_hi)
        htf_hi_struct := htf_piv_hi_raw > h_last_hi ? "Higher High" : "Lower High"
        if htf_hi_struct == "Higher High"
            if htf_struct_trend == "Bearish"
                htf_choch_bull := true
            else if htf_struct_trend == "Bullish"
                htf_bos_bull := true
            htf_struct_trend := "Bullish"
    h_last_hi := htf_piv_hi_raw
    h_last_hi_t := htf_piv_bar_t
if not na(htf_piv_lo_raw)
    if not na(h_last_lo)
        htf_lo_struct := htf_piv_lo_raw < h_last_lo ? "Lower Low" : "Higher Low"
        if htf_lo_struct == "Lower Low"
            if htf_struct_trend == "Bullish"
                htf_choch_bear := true
            else if htf_struct_trend == "Bearish"
                htf_bos_bear := true
            htf_struct_trend := "Bearish"
    h_last_lo := htf_piv_lo_raw
    h_last_lo_t := htf_piv_bar_t
// Bars since the last confirmed break, one counter per direction — lets the Confluence Score factor
// below stay true for a window after the event instead of a single-bar pulse (pivot confirmation is
// itself lagged, so a single-bar pulse would almost never land on the same bar as other factors).
// Reuses confluence_recency_ltf_bars (added in v4.42) as that window rather than adding a new input.
var int ltf_bars_since_htf_bull_break = 999999
var int ltf_bars_since_htf_bear_break = 999999
if htf_choch_bull or htf_bos_bull
    ltf_bars_since_htf_bull_break := 0
else
    ltf_bars_since_htf_bull_break := ltf_bars_since_htf_bull_break + 1
if htf_choch_bear or htf_bos_bear
    ltf_bars_since_htf_bear_break := 0
else
    ltf_bars_since_htf_bear_break := ltf_bars_since_htf_bear_break + 1
if not na(ltf_piv_hi_raw)
    if not na(l_last_hi)
        ltf_hi_struct := ltf_piv_hi_raw > l_last_hi ? "Higher High" : "Lower High"
    l_last_hi := ltf_piv_hi_raw
    l_last_hi_t := ltf_piv_bar_t
if not na(ltf_piv_lo_raw)
    if not na(l_last_lo)
        ltf_lo_struct := ltf_piv_lo_raw < l_last_lo ? "Lower Low" : "Higher Low"
    l_last_lo := ltf_piv_lo_raw
    l_last_lo_t := ltf_piv_bar_t

// ===========================
// MTF (Max Timeframe) — a third tier tracked the same way as HTF: CBC state, structure, last flip, EMA
// cloud. Gives a longer-term read alongside LTF (chart) and HTF, without altering either of those.
// ===========================
[mtf_cbc, mtf_hi1, mtf_lo1] = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), mtf_timeframe, [f_cbc_state(), high[1], low[1]], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_on)
mtf_cbc_labels_f = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), mtf_timeframe, f_cbc_state()[1] ? 1.0 : 0.0, lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)
// Confirmed MTF bars since the last confirmed MTF flip, counted on the MTF's own native bars.
mtf_bars_since_flip = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), mtf_timeframe, f_bars_since_flip(f_cbc_state())[1], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)
[sig_mtf_lo, sig_mtf_hi, sig_mtf_cl, sig_mtf_op, sig_mtf_t_open, sig_mtf_t_close] = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), mtf_timeframe, [low[1], high[1], close[1], open[1], time[1], time_close[1]], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)

[mtf_ema9, mtf_ema20] = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), mtf_timeframe, [ta.ema(close, 9), ta.ema(close, 20)], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_on)
mtf_ema_bullish = mtf_ema9 >= mtf_ema20

// Detect MTF CBC change when the last CLOSED MTF state changes (no signal while the current MTF bar is still forming).
mtf_flip_bull = mtf_enabled and not na(mtf_cbc_labels_f) and not na(mtf_cbc_labels_f[1]) and mtf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0 and mtf_cbc_labels_f[1] == 0.0
mtf_flip_bear = mtf_enabled and not na(mtf_cbc_labels_f) and not na(mtf_cbc_labels_f[1]) and mtf_cbc_labels_f == 0.0 and mtf_cbc_labels_f[1] == 1.0

// Timestamp + direction of the last confirmed MTF CBC flip (that MTF bar's own close time), for the
// "CBC (MTF) last flip" display in the Status table / Historical Bar Panel / alert email.
var int mtf_last_flip_t = na
var bool mtf_last_flip_bull = bool(na)
if mtf_flip_bull or mtf_flip_bear
    mtf_last_flip_t := sig_mtf_t_close
    mtf_last_flip_bull := mtf_flip_bull

// MTF pivot detection + structure classification (Higher High/Lower High/Higher Low/Lower Low), same
// "replace-only persistence, classify against the previous same-type pivot" pattern as LTF/HTF above.
[mtf_piv_hi_raw, mtf_piv_lo_raw, mtf_piv_bar_t] = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), mtf_timeframe, [ta.pivothigh(high, mtf_piv_n, mtf_piv_n), ta.pivotlow(low, mtf_piv_n, mtf_piv_n), time[mtf_piv_n]], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)
var float m_last_hi = na
var int   m_last_hi_t = na
var float m_last_lo = na
var int   m_last_lo_t = na
var string mtf_hi_struct = "—"
var string mtf_lo_struct = "—"
if not na(mtf_piv_hi_raw)
    if not na(m_last_hi)
        mtf_hi_struct := mtf_piv_hi_raw > m_last_hi ? "Higher High" : "Lower High"
    m_last_hi := mtf_piv_hi_raw
    m_last_hi_t := mtf_piv_bar_t
if not na(mtf_piv_lo_raw)
    if not na(m_last_lo)
        mtf_lo_struct := mtf_piv_lo_raw < m_last_lo ? "Lower Low" : "Higher Low"
    m_last_lo := mtf_piv_lo_raw
    m_last_lo_t := mtf_piv_bar_t

// ===========================
// Trailing Stop (LTF-based) — matches the actual manual method: initial stop = the HIGH or LOW of the
// prior LTF (chart-timeframe) candle that's already closed; a close beyond it stops you out. If not
// breached, wait for the current candle to close, then move the stop to the new prior candle's high/
// low — repeating every LTF candle until price has moved far enough in favor to switch to breakeven +
// a fixed-point step trail. Direction is taken from the chart's own confirmed CBC state (this is LTF,
// not HTF, on purpose — kept under the same variable names used throughout the rest of the script).
// ===========================
bool htf_dir_bull_now = not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and htf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0
bool htf_dir_bear_now = not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and htf_cbc_labels_f == 0.0
// LTF EMA20 Reclaim: HTF already confirmed a direction, and price just crossed back onto the
// confirming side of the LTF's own EMA20 — a pullback-then-reclaim entry signal.
// LTF EMA20 Reclaim condition moved below (needs htf_between_enter, defined later in the script).
// Initial/trailing stop base: the prior LTF candle's low (bull) or high (bear) — already closed as of
// this bar, and re-evaluated fresh every bar (not just at swing points).
// v3.75: the stop's underlying candle timeframe depends on the CHART's own timeframe, not always
// the chart's own candle — 3m and 15m charts both now use the 3m candle; a 60m (1H) chart uses the
// 15m candle instead; a 240m (4H) chart uses the 60m (1H) candle instead. NOTE: Pine can't re-run a
// script more often than the chart's own bars close, so on a 60m or 240m chart this still only
// refreshes once per chart bar (at that bar's close) — it fetches the correct SOURCE candle's level,
// but won't update mid-hour/mid-4-hours the way it would if you were actually viewing that lower
// timeframe directly.
f_stop_tf() =>
    string ctf = timeframe.period
    ctf == "3" or ctf == "15" ? "3" : ctf == "60" ? "15" : ctf == "240" ? "60" : ctf

stop_tf = f_stop_tf()
[stop_lo, stop_hi] = request.security(ticker.standard(syminfo.tickerid), stop_tf, [low[1], high[1]], lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)
float htf_trail_stop_cbc = cbc ? stop_lo : stop_hi

// Breakeven + step-trail overlay: tracks the price at the LTF flip (entry) and the best price reached
// since (max favorable excursion), then — once that favorable move clears the trigger/step size —
// computes a managed stop that starts at breakeven and steps up/down in the same increments, always
// one step behind the best price reached. Auto trigger/step by symbol root (MES/MGC=5, MNQ=30, M2K=3,
// other=5), or the manual override if set above.
f_be_trail_step() =>
    string root = syminfo.root
    float auto_pts = root == "MES" or root == "MGC" ? 5.0 : root == "MNQ" ? 30.0 : root == "M2K" ? 3.0 : 5.0
    be_trail_points_override > 0 ? be_trail_points_override : auto_pts

be_trail_step = f_be_trail_step()

var float htf_entry_price = na
var float htf_mfe_price = na
bool htf_just_flipped_local = any_flip
if htf_just_flipped_local
    htf_entry_price := close
    htf_mfe_price := close
else if cbc
    htf_mfe_price := math.max(nz(htf_mfe_price, high), high)
else if not cbc
    htf_mfe_price := math.min(nz(htf_mfe_price, low), low)

// v3.75: the CBC-based stop above (prior closed LTF candle's low/high) has no ratchet on its own —
// on a pullback candle it can actually retreat (a lower low while still confirmed bullish, or a
// higher high while still confirmed bearish), which isn't how a real trailing stop should behave. This
// tracks the best (highest for bull / lowest for bear) CBC-based level seen since the last flip, so
// the stop only ever holds or moves in the favorable direction — resets fresh on each new flip.
var float htf_trail_stop_cbc_ratchet = na
if htf_just_flipped_local
    htf_trail_stop_cbc_ratchet := htf_trail_stop_cbc
else if cbc
    htf_trail_stop_cbc_ratchet := math.max(nz(htf_trail_stop_cbc_ratchet, htf_trail_stop_cbc), htf_trail_stop_cbc)
else if not cbc
    htf_trail_stop_cbc_ratchet := math.min(nz(htf_trail_stop_cbc_ratchet, htf_trail_stop_cbc), htf_trail_stop_cbc)

float htf_favorable_move = na(htf_entry_price) ? na : (cbc ? htf_mfe_price - htf_entry_price : htf_entry_price - htf_mfe_price)
int htf_be_steps = na(htf_favorable_move) ? 0 : int(math.floor(htf_favorable_move / be_trail_step))
float htf_managed_stop = (enable_be_trail and htf_be_steps >= 1) ? htf_entry_price + (htf_be_steps - 1) * be_trail_step * (cbc ? 1 : -1) : na

// This is the level everything downstream (Status table, Historical panel, alert email, the on-chart line
// itself) reads as "the" Trailing Stop — whichever of the ratcheted CBC-based level or the managed
// level is currently more protective (higher while bullish, lower while bearish).
float htf_trail_stop = na(htf_managed_stop) ? htf_trail_stop_cbc_ratchet : (cbc ? math.max(htf_trail_stop_cbc_ratchet, htf_managed_stop) : math.min(htf_trail_stop_cbc_ratchet, htf_managed_stop))
// v3.75: on the bar the LTF direction flips (the closed position's exit), keep showing the OLD stop
// level through THIS candle's close — that's where the position actually got stopped/exited — and
// only start the new direction's level on the very next bar. Combined with stepline rendering below
// (horizontal-then-vertical steps, no diagonal interpolation), this makes the old stop visually
// terminate flat at the flip candle's close, then jump cleanly to the new level.
float htf_trail_stop_disp = htf_just_flipped_local ? htf_trail_stop[1] : htf_trail_stop
// The LINE's color must lag the same way its VALUE does above: on the flip bar itself it's still
// showing the OLD (pre-flip) direction's stop level, so it needs the OLD direction's color too —
// otherwise the flip bar would show last direction's stop level painted in the NEW direction's color.
// The LABEL isn't lagged (it always shows the current htf_trail_stop), so its color just follows cbc.
bool sl_line_is_bull = htf_just_flipped_local ? not cbc : cbc
color sl_line_col = sl_line_is_bull ? col_sl_line_long : col_sl_line_short
color sl_lbl_col = cbc ? col_sl_line_long : col_sl_line_short
// v3.75: switched from style_linebr (which draws a diagonal interpolated line between ANY two
// consecutive values) to style_stepline (horizontal segment, then a vertical jump, then horizontal
// again). linebr was drawing a sloped "teleport" line not just at CBC flips but on every incremental
// breakeven/step-trail move too, since the stop level changes bar-to-bar as price makes new highs/
// lows — stepline is the correct rendering for any trailing-stop-style level.
plot(show_sl_line ? htf_trail_stop_disp : na, title="SL", color=sl_line_col, linewidth=2, style=plot.style_steplinebr)
var label sl_lbl = na
if show_sl_line and not na(htf_trail_stop)
    int sl_lbl_t = time + extend_levels_bars * int(timeframe.in_seconds(timeframe.period) * 1000)
    if na(sl_lbl)
        sl_lbl := label.new(sl_lbl_t, htf_trail_stop, "SL", xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=sl_lbl_col, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
    else
        label.set_x(sl_lbl, sl_lbl_t)
        label.set_y(sl_lbl, htf_trail_stop)
        label.set_textcolor(sl_lbl, sl_lbl_col)
else
    label.delete(sl_lbl)
    sl_lbl := na
// "CBC" when the original CBC-based level is the one currently governing, "Trail" when the managed
// breakeven/step level has taken over as the more protective of the two.
string htf_stop_method = (not na(htf_managed_stop) and ((cbc and htf_managed_stop >= htf_trail_stop_cbc_ratchet) or ((not cbc) and htf_managed_stop <= htf_trail_stop_cbc_ratchet))) ? "Trail" : "CBC"

// Drawn as a manual step-line via line.new() rather than plot() — doesn't count against the 64-plot budget.
// Starts a new line segment each time htf_trail_stop's value changes (a "step"), and just extends the
// current segment's right edge while the value holds steady.
var line htf_stop_line = na
var float htf_stop_line_val = na
if show_htf_stop and not na(htf_trail_stop)
    if na(htf_stop_line) or htf_trail_stop != htf_stop_line_val
        htf_stop_line := line.new(bar_index, htf_trail_stop, bar_index, htf_trail_stop, color=col_htf_stop, width=htf_stop_width, style=line.style_solid)
        htf_stop_line_val := htf_trail_stop
    else
        line.set_x2(htf_stop_line, bar_index)
else if not show_htf_stop and not na(htf_stop_line)
    line.delete(htf_stop_line)
    htf_stop_line := na
    htf_stop_line_val := na


int htf_sec_lbl = timeframe.in_seconds(htf_timeframe)
int ch_sec_lbl = timeframe.in_seconds(timeframe.period)
int max_htf_lbl_off = 32
if not na(htf_sec_lbl) and not na(ch_sec_lbl) and ch_sec_lbl > 0
    max_htf_lbl_off := int(math.ceil(htf_sec_lbl / ch_sec_lbl)) + 4
    max_htf_lbl_off := int(math.min(max_htf_lbl_off, 32))
ltf_relocate_htf_labels = not na(htf_sec_lbl) and not na(ch_sec_lbl) and ch_sec_lbl > 0 and ch_sec_lbl < htf_sec_lbl
bool htf_same_tf_chart = not na(htf_sec_lbl) and not na(ch_sec_lbl) and ch_sec_lbl == htf_sec_lbl

// Next flip prices (ThinkScript parity): bulls flip bear at prior low; bears flip bull at prior high (no offset)
ltf_flip_price = cbc[1] ? low[1] : high[1]
htf_flip_price = htf_cbc[1] ? htf_lo1 : htf_hi1
mtf_flip_price = mtf_cbc[1] ? mtf_lo1 : mtf_hi1

var line cbc_flip_ltf_line = na
var line cbc_flip_htf_line = na
var line brsg_line_top = na
var line brsg_line_bot = na
var float brsg_line_top_val = na
var float brsg_line_bot_val = na
var linefill brsg_fill = na
var line htf_fvg_line_top = na
var line htf_fvg_line_bot = na
var float htf_fvg_line_top_val = na
var float htf_fvg_line_bot_val = na
var linefill htf_fvg_fill = na
var line h_piv_hi_ln = na
var line h_piv_lo_ln = na
var linefill h_piv_fill = na
var line l_piv_hi_ln = na
var line l_piv_lo_ln = na
var linefill l_piv_fill = na
var line m_piv_hi_ln = na
var line m_piv_lo_ln = na
var linefill m_piv_fill = na
if barstate.islast
    // v4.25 token-trim: dropped the "if not na(x)" guard on each of these 14 deletes — Pine's
    // *.delete() functions already treat a na id as a no-op (proven elsewhere in this same file:
    // the new_day reset block above, lines ~970-990, already calls line.delete()/label.delete() on
    // ydh_line/ydh_lbl/etc. completely unguarded). Removing a redundant guard is a pure token-count
    // cut with zero behavior change, not a new pattern being tested blind.
    line.delete(cbc_flip_ltf_line)
    cbc_flip_ltf_line := na
    line.delete(cbc_flip_htf_line)
    cbc_flip_htf_line := na
    line.delete(brsg_line_top)
    brsg_line_top := na
    line.delete(brsg_line_bot)
    brsg_line_bot := na
    line.delete(h_piv_hi_ln)
    h_piv_hi_ln := na
    line.delete(h_piv_lo_ln)
    h_piv_lo_ln := na
    linefill.delete(h_piv_fill)
    h_piv_fill := na
    line.delete(l_piv_hi_ln)
    l_piv_hi_ln := na
    line.delete(l_piv_lo_ln)
    l_piv_lo_ln := na
    linefill.delete(l_piv_fill)
    l_piv_fill := na
    line.delete(m_piv_hi_ln)
    m_piv_hi_ln := na
    line.delete(m_piv_lo_ln)
    m_piv_lo_ln := na
    linefill.delete(m_piv_fill)
    m_piv_fill := na
    linefill.delete(brsg_fill)
    brsg_fill := na
    line.delete(htf_fvg_line_top)
    htf_fvg_line_top := na
    line.delete(htf_fvg_line_bot)
    htf_fvg_line_bot := na
    linefill.delete(htf_fvg_fill)
    htf_fvg_fill := na
    if bar_index > 0 and not na(time[1]) and not na(time_close[1])
        if show_cbc_flip_ltf and not na(ltf_flip_price)
            cbc_flip_ltf_line := line.new(time[1], ltf_flip_price, time_close[1], ltf_flip_price, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=cbc[1] ? col_ltf_flip_l : col_ltf_flip_h, width=cbc_flip_ltf_width, style=line.style_solid, extend=extend.none)
        if htf_enabled and show_cbc_flip_htf and not na(htf_flip_price)
            cbc_flip_htf_line := line.new(time[1], htf_flip_price, time_close[1], htf_flip_price, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=htf_cbc[1] ? col_htf_flip_l : col_htf_flip_h, width=cbc_flip_htf_width, style=line.style_solid, extend=extend.none)
        if show_brsg_zone and not na(brsg_zone_top)
            if na(brsg_line_top) or brsg_zone_top != brsg_line_top_val
                brsg_line_top := line.new(bar_index, brsg_zone_top, bar_index, brsg_zone_top, color=col_brsg_zone, width=cbc_flip_ltf_width, style=line.style_solid)
                brsg_line_top_val := brsg_zone_top
            else
                line.set_x2(brsg_line_top, bar_index)
            if na(brsg_line_bot) or brsg_zone_bot != brsg_line_bot_val
                brsg_line_bot := line.new(bar_index, brsg_zone_bot, bar_index, brsg_zone_bot, color=col_brsg_zone, width=cbc_flip_ltf_width, style=line.style_solid)
                brsg_line_bot_val := brsg_zone_bot
            else
                line.set_x2(brsg_line_bot, bar_index)
            if show_brsg_fill and not na(brsg_line_top) and not na(brsg_line_bot)
                brsg_fill := linefill.new(brsg_line_top, brsg_line_bot, color=col_brsg_fill)
        else if not show_brsg_zone
            line.delete(brsg_line_top)
            brsg_line_top := na
            brsg_line_top_val := na
            line.delete(brsg_line_bot)
            brsg_line_bot := na
            brsg_line_bot_val := na
        // HTF Flip FVG zone — same "redraw only when the bound actually changed, else just extend"
        // pattern as the BRSG zone just above, reusing htf_fvg_top/bot (computed once per flip cycle,
        // frozen — see the freeze block above) and cbc_flip_ltf_width. No new request.security call.
        if show_htf_flip_fvg and htf_fvg_exists
            if na(htf_fvg_line_top) or htf_fvg_top != htf_fvg_line_top_val
                htf_fvg_line_top := line.new(bar_index, htf_fvg_top, bar_index, htf_fvg_top, color=col_htf_flip_fvg, width=cbc_flip_ltf_width, style=line.style_solid)
                htf_fvg_line_top_val := htf_fvg_top
            else
                line.set_x2(htf_fvg_line_top, bar_index)
            if na(htf_fvg_line_bot) or htf_fvg_bot != htf_fvg_line_bot_val
                htf_fvg_line_bot := line.new(bar_index, htf_fvg_bot, bar_index, htf_fvg_bot, color=col_htf_flip_fvg, width=cbc_flip_ltf_width, style=line.style_solid)
                htf_fvg_line_bot_val := htf_fvg_bot
            else
                line.set_x2(htf_fvg_line_bot, bar_index)
            if not na(htf_fvg_line_top) and not na(htf_fvg_line_bot)
                htf_fvg_fill := linefill.new(htf_fvg_line_top, htf_fvg_line_bot, color=col_htf_flip_fvg)
        else if not show_htf_flip_fvg
            line.delete(htf_fvg_line_top)
            htf_fvg_line_top := na
            htf_fvg_line_top_val := na
            line.delete(htf_fvg_line_bot)
            htf_fvg_line_bot := na
            htf_fvg_line_bot_val := na
    if not na(ch_sec_lbl) and ch_sec_lbl > 0
        int piv_t_right = time_close + ch_sec_lbl * 1000
        if show_htf_pivots and htf_enabled
            string htf_piv_sty = f_ref_line_style(htf_piv_style)
            if not na(h_last_hi) and not na(h_last_hi_t)
                h_piv_hi_ln := line.new(h_last_hi_t, h_last_hi, piv_t_right, h_last_hi, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_htf_piv_res, style=htf_piv_sty, width=htf_piv_w, extend=extend.none)
            if not na(h_last_lo) and not na(h_last_lo_t)
                h_piv_lo_ln := line.new(h_last_lo_t, h_last_lo, piv_t_right, h_last_lo, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_htf_piv_sup, style=htf_piv_sty, width=htf_piv_w, extend=extend.none)
            if show_htf_piv_fill and not na(h_piv_hi_ln) and not na(h_piv_lo_ln)
                h_piv_fill := linefill.new(h_piv_hi_ln, h_piv_lo_ln, color=col_htf_piv_fill)
        if show_ltf_pivots
            string ltf_piv_sty = f_ref_line_style(ltf_piv_style)
            if not na(l_last_hi) and not na(l_last_hi_t)
                l_piv_hi_ln := line.new(l_last_hi_t, l_last_hi, piv_t_right, l_last_hi, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_ltf_piv_res, style=ltf_piv_sty, width=ltf_piv_w, extend=extend.none)
            if not na(l_last_lo) and not na(l_last_lo_t)
                l_piv_lo_ln := line.new(l_last_lo_t, l_last_lo, piv_t_right, l_last_lo, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_ltf_piv_sup, style=ltf_piv_sty, width=ltf_piv_w, extend=extend.none)
            if show_ltf_piv_fill and not na(l_piv_hi_ln) and not na(l_piv_lo_ln)
                l_piv_fill := linefill.new(l_piv_hi_ln, l_piv_lo_ln, color=col_ltf_piv_fill)
        if show_mtf_pivots and mtf_enabled
            string mtf_piv_sty = f_ref_line_style(mtf_piv_style)
            if not na(m_last_hi) and not na(m_last_hi_t)
                m_piv_hi_ln := line.new(m_last_hi_t, m_last_hi, piv_t_right, m_last_hi, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_mtf_piv_res, style=mtf_piv_sty, width=mtf_piv_w, extend=extend.none)
            if not na(m_last_lo) and not na(m_last_lo_t)
                m_piv_lo_ln := line.new(m_last_lo_t, m_last_lo, piv_t_right, m_last_lo, xloc=xloc.bar_time, color=col_mtf_piv_sup, style=mtf_piv_sty, width=mtf_piv_w, extend=extend.none)
            if show_mtf_piv_fill and not na(m_piv_hi_ln) and not na(m_piv_lo_ln)
                m_piv_fill := linefill.new(m_piv_hi_ln, m_piv_lo_ln, color=col_mtf_piv_fill)

// CBC agreement bars: chart vs HTF (optional). Single solid plotcandle — same color on body, wick, border (Style tab: Body/Wick/Border like v1.15). Confirmed bars only; no overlay on the forming bar.
both_cbc_long = cbc and htf_cbc
both_cbc_short = not cbc and not htf_cbc
cbc_in_agreement = both_cbc_long or both_cbc_short
// Alert-facing agreement uses htf_cbc_labels_f (the CONFIRMED, last-closed-HTF-bar value — same one shown as
// "CBC (HTF): Bullish/Bearish" in the alert message and the status table), not the live htf_cbc used for bar
// coloring above. Using the live value here previously let "Agreement" fire while the message still showed a
// stale/disagreeing HTF state, since the two used different underlying HTF signals.
agreement_htf_long = not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and htf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0
agreement_htf_short = not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and htf_cbc_labels_f == 0.0
agreement_confirmed_long = cbc and agreement_htf_long
agreement_confirmed_short = (not cbc) and agreement_htf_short
// Fires only on the transition INTO agreement (not every bar while agreement persists)
agreement_enter_long_raw = agreement_confirmed_long and not (agreement_confirmed_long[1] or agreement_confirmed_short[1])
agreement_enter_short_raw = agreement_confirmed_short and not (agreement_confirmed_long[1] or agreement_confirmed_short[1])
// De-dup against HTF-unchanged re-fires: if LTF briefly flickers out of agreement and back while the HTF
// side hasn't actually advanced to a new closed bar, that's LTF noise, not new information — don't email
// again for it. sig_htf_t_close (the last CLOSED HTF bar's close time) stays identical for every LTF bar
// within that same HTF window, so comparing it against the close-time we last fired on tells us whether
// anything genuinely new happened on the HTF side since the last Agreement email.
// v4.22: sentinel (-1) instead of na for these de-dup timestamps — a real HTF close time is always a
// huge positive ms-epoch value, never -1, so "sig_htf_t_close != last_X" alone already covers the
// "never fired yet" case without a separate na() branch. Same trick as htf_early_time_ok/
// ltf_last_touch_alert_bar earlier in this file — one fewer condition per line, same behavior.
// v4.26: Max Loss Per Trade — computed here at top level (never read from inside f_fire_alerts()
// itself) and folded straight into agreement_enter_long/short's own formula below, so the function
// keeps reading the exact same identifiers it already read before (agreement_enter_long/short/
// _live) — zero NEW external-scope references added to that function. This deliberately avoids the
// pattern that backfired twice this session (a brand-new top-level var read for the first time from
// inside an existing function) and matches this file's own v3.87 finding that a function's token/
// element cost scales with the SET of distinct externals it touches, not how many times each is read.
bool max_loss_ok = not max_loss_per_trade_on or na(htf_trail_stop) or math.abs(close - htf_trail_stop) <= max_loss_per_trade_pts
var int last_agree_long_htf_close_t = -1
var int last_agree_short_htf_close_t = -1
agreement_enter_long = agreement_enter_long_raw and sig_htf_t_close != last_agree_long_htf_close_t and max_loss_ok
agreement_enter_short = agreement_enter_short_raw and sig_htf_t_close != last_agree_short_htf_close_t and max_loss_ok
if agreement_enter_long
    last_agree_long_htf_close_t := sig_htf_t_close
    last_agree_short_htf_close_t := -1
if agreement_enter_short
    last_agree_short_htf_close_t := sig_htf_t_close
    last_agree_long_htf_close_t := -1
// Live/unconfirmed counterparts for the "Early" alert — uses the live-updating htf_cbc (same one driving the
// CBC agreement bar coloring), which can flip before its HTF bar actually closes.
agreement_enter_long_live_raw = both_cbc_long and not (both_cbc_long[1] or both_cbc_short[1])
agreement_enter_short_live_raw = both_cbc_short and not (both_cbc_long[1] or both_cbc_short[1])
// De-dup against re-fires within the SAME still-forming HTF bar: if the LTF/HTF live agreement direction
// flickers out and back in while the HTF bar hasn't actually advanced to a new one, don't email again — only
// a new still-forming HTF bar, or a genuine change to the OTHER live direction, counts as new information.
var int last_agree_long_live_htf_t = -1
var int last_agree_short_live_htf_t = -1
agreement_enter_long_live = agreement_enter_long_live_raw and htf_live_t_close != last_agree_long_live_htf_t and max_loss_ok
agreement_enter_short_live = agreement_enter_short_live_raw and htf_live_t_close != last_agree_short_live_htf_t and max_loss_ok
if agreement_enter_long_live
    last_agree_long_live_htf_t := htf_live_t_close
    last_agree_short_live_htf_t := -1
if agreement_enter_short_live
    last_agree_short_live_htf_t := htf_live_t_close
    last_agree_long_live_htf_t := -1
cbc_bar_paint_color = cbc_in_agreement ? (both_cbc_long ? col_cbc_bar_long : col_cbc_bar_short) : col_cbc_bar_neutral
cbc_agreement_plot_on = show_bar_coloring and barstate.isconfirmed
cbc_bar_final_color = cbc_agreement_plot_on ? cbc_bar_paint_color : na
pc_ag_o = cbc_agreement_plot_on ? open : na
pc_ag_h = cbc_agreement_plot_on ? high : na
pc_ag_l = cbc_agreement_plot_on ? low : na
pc_ag_c = cbc_agreement_plot_on ? close : na
plotcandle(pc_ag_o, pc_ag_h, pc_ag_l, pc_ag_c, title="CBC agreement candle", color=cbc_bar_final_color, wickcolor=cbc_bar_final_color, bordercolor=cbc_bar_final_color, display=display.all - display.status_line)

// Detect HTF CBC change when last closed HTF state changes (no signal while current HTF bar is still forming). htf_cbc_labels_f: 1=bull, 0=bear, na=no HTF data yet.
htf_flip_bull = htf_enabled and not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and not na(htf_cbc_labels_f[1]) and htf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0 and htf_cbc_labels_f[1] == 0.0
htf_flip_bear = htf_enabled and not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and not na(htf_cbc_labels_f[1]) and htf_cbc_labels_f == 0.0 and htf_cbc_labels_f[1] == 1.0
// Timestamp (the flipped HTF bar's own close time, sig_htf_t_close) of the last confirmed HTF CBC flip — for
// the "CBC (HTF) last flip" display in the Status table / Historical Bar Panel / alert email.
var int htf_last_flip_t = na
var bool htf_last_flip_bull = bool(na)
if htf_flip_bull or htf_flip_bear
    htf_last_flip_t := sig_htf_t_close
    htf_last_flip_bull := htf_flip_bull

// ===========================
// v3.75: Confluence Score (0-5) — v4.42: extended to 6 factors. Zone (was BRSG-only) now counts
// EITHER the BRSG zone touch OR price sitting inside the HTF Flip FVG (htf_fvg_exists/top/bot,
// already computed above — no new request.security call); either is the same "price converged back
// into the flip's own zone" idea. Added a new Liquidity Sweep factor. Both new/changed factors are
// gated by a recency window (confluence_recency_ltf_bars LTF bars since the HTF flip, reusing
// ltf_bars_since_htf_flip added in v4.41 for the FVG alert cutoff) so a stale, long-past zone/sweep
// can't keep contributing to the score indefinitely.
// ===========================
// v4.71: Flip (LTF) is now toggleable per follow-up ("can we have an input so it can be included or
// excluded from the confluence score - default is off"), matching the same touch/gated pattern as
// Structure/Volume/HTFFlip/Fib below — cf_f1_flip_touch is the raw signal, cf_f1_flip is what
// actually counts toward the score. Off by default (confluence_use_flip), unlike those 4 which
// default on — per the explicit request.
bool cf_f1_flip_touch = ltf_has_flipped
bool cf_f1_flip = confluence_use_flip and cf_f1_flip_touch
bool cf_f2_htf = (cbc and not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and htf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0) or ((not cbc) and not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and htf_cbc_labels_f == 0.0)
bool cf_f3_cloud = (cbc and ema_bullish) or ((not cbc) and not ema_bullish)
// v4.72: Level (factor #4) is now toggleable too, per follow-up ("same for key level confluence"),
// mirroring Flip (LTF)'s v4.71 treatment exactly — off by default.
bool cf_f4_level_touch = level_bullet != ""
bool cf_f4_level = confluence_use_level and cf_f4_level_touch
bool cf_f5_zone_touch = (not na(brsg_zone_top) and not na(htf_last_flip_t) and htf_last_flip_bull == cbc and low <= math.max(brsg_zone_top, brsg_zone_bot) and high >= math.min(brsg_zone_top, brsg_zone_bot)) or (htf_fvg_exists and close <= htf_fvg_top and close >= htf_fvg_bot and htf_fvg_bull == cbc)
bool cf_f5_zone = cf_f5_zone_touch and ltf_bars_since_htf_flip <= confluence_recency_ltf_bars
// Liquidity Sweep: price wicks past the last confirmed HTF swing low/high (h_last_lo/h_last_hi,
// already computed above for the Pivot rows — v4.45: was LTF's l_last_lo/l_last_hi, changed to HTF
// per follow-up) but closes back on the flip's side of it, agreeing with the current direction
// (cbc) — the classic stop-hunt-then-reversal pattern.
bool cf_f6_sweep_touch = (cbc and not na(h_last_lo) and low < h_last_lo and close > h_last_lo) or ((not cbc) and not na(h_last_hi) and high > h_last_hi and close < h_last_hi)
bool cf_f6_sweep = cf_f6_sweep_touch and ltf_bars_since_htf_flip <= confluence_recency_ltf_bars
// v4.43: Structure Break (BOS/CHoCH) — a bullish break (CHoCH or BOS, tracked above) within the last
// confluence_recency_ltf_bars LTF bars while the current direction is bullish, or the bearish
// equivalent. Gated by its own include/exclude toggle, unlike the other 6 factors.
bool cf_f7_structure_touch = (cbc and ltf_bars_since_htf_bull_break <= confluence_recency_ltf_bars) or ((not cbc) and ltf_bars_since_htf_bear_break <= confluence_recency_ltf_bars)
bool cf_f7_structure_break = confluence_use_structure_break and cf_f7_structure_touch
// v4.46: Volume Spike — the HTF flip candle's own volume (htf_flip_candle_vol, frozen at the flip
// alongside its hi/lo above) versus its trailing average (htf_flip_candle_vol_avg, same freeze) —
// confirms real participation behind the flip rather than a low-volume drift-through. Gated by the
// same recency window as Zone/Sweep/Structure. Has its own include/exclude toggle like Structure
// Break, unlike the original 5 factors.
float htf_flip_rvol = (not na(htf_flip_candle_vol) and not na(htf_flip_candle_vol_avg) and htf_flip_candle_vol_avg > 0) ? htf_flip_candle_vol / htf_flip_candle_vol_avg : na
bool cf_f8_volume_touch = not na(htf_flip_rvol) and htf_flip_rvol >= confluence_vol_mult and ltf_bars_since_htf_flip <= confluence_recency_ltf_bars
bool cf_f8_volume = confluence_use_volume and cf_f8_volume_touch
// v4.47: HTF Flip Recency — a separate factor from Flip(LTF) (cf_f1, which checks the LTF's OWN
// flip via ltf_has_flipped). v4.68: reworked per follow-up ("#9 uses fair value gaps, once filled
// they are invalid") — now true whenever the current HTF flip's own Fair Value Gap (htf_fvg_exists,
// computed above) still exists AND hasn't been filled yet (htf_fvg_filled, also above); goes false
// the instant price trades back into the gap, and only becomes true again once the NEXT flip opens a
// fresh, untouched gap. If a given flip produces no gap at all (htf_fvg_exists never true for it),
// this factor simply never fires for that flip. No direction check beyond the gap itself (matches
// the old behavior). Replaces the old htf_has_flipped one-way "has the HTF EVER flipped" latch, which
// never reset and so stayed true forever after the chart's very first flip.
bool cf_f9_htf_flip_touch = htf_fvg_exists and not htf_fvg_filled
bool cf_f9_htf_flip = confluence_use_htf_flip and cf_f9_htf_flip_touch
// v4.54: Fib Golden Pocket (50%-61.8%) — 10th confluence factor, per follow-up. Anchored to the last
// confirmed HTF swing high/low (h_last_hi/h_last_lo, already computed above for Sweep/Pivot(HTF) — no
// new request.security call). Direction-aware like Sweep: bullish measures the retracement DOWN from
// h_last_hi, bearish measures the retracement UP from h_last_lo. Gated by the same recency window as
// Zone/Sweep/Structure/Volume, plus its own include/exclude toggle. Also surfaced as its own always-
// visible (Essential-level, not Full-gated) Status table row and an a_status_msg alert line below,
// per follow-up ("added to the essential table and app").
bool fib_valid = not na(h_last_hi) and not na(h_last_lo) and h_last_hi > h_last_lo
float fib_50 = fib_valid ? (cbc ? h_last_hi - 0.5 * (h_last_hi - h_last_lo) : h_last_lo + 0.5 * (h_last_hi - h_last_lo)) : na
float fib_618 = fib_valid ? (cbc ? h_last_hi - 0.618 * (h_last_hi - h_last_lo) : h_last_lo + 0.618 * (h_last_hi - h_last_lo)) : na
float fib_zone_top = fib_valid ? math.max(fib_50, fib_618) : na
float fib_zone_bot = fib_valid ? math.min(fib_50, fib_618) : na
// v4.61: simple, non-recency-gated "is price literally between the two levels right now" check —
// deliberately separate from cf_f10_fib_touch below (which additionally requires the recency window,
// since that one feeds the Confluence Score). This one drives display coloring only (Status table
// row, on-chart box, alert text), per follow-up ("should change to green if price is in fib zone .5
// to .618") — the scoring factor's own recency behavior is unchanged.
bool fib_price_in_zone = fib_valid and low <= fib_zone_top and high >= fib_zone_bot
// v4.63: the "0%" and "100%" anchor levels themselves — i.e. the actual last-confirmed HTF swing
// high/low the retracement is measured from/to (same h_last_hi/h_last_lo as fib_50/fib_618 above,
// just the un-retraced endpoints). 0% is always the side the retracement is measured FROM (h_last_hi
// when bullish/cbc, h_last_lo when bearish), 100% is the far side — matching fib_50/fib_618's own
// cbc ? ... : ... direction convention exactly, so 0/50/61.8/100 all sit consistently along the same
// swing regardless of direction. Per follow-up ("can you put in the zero and 1 lines for fib").
float fib_0 = fib_valid ? (cbc ? h_last_hi : h_last_lo) : na
float fib_100 = fib_valid ? (cbc ? h_last_lo : h_last_hi) : na
bool cf_f10_fib_touch = fib_valid and low <= fib_zone_top and high >= fib_zone_bot and ltf_bars_since_htf_flip <= confluence_recency_ltf_bars
bool cf_f10_fib = confluence_use_fib and cf_f10_fib_touch
int confluence_score = (cf_f1_flip ? 1 : 0) + (cf_f2_htf ? 1 : 0) + (cf_f3_cloud ? 1 : 0) + (cf_f4_level ? 1 : 0) + (cf_f5_zone ? 1 : 0) + (cf_f6_sweep ? 1 : 0) + (cf_f7_structure_break ? 1 : 0) + (cf_f8_volume ? 1 : 0) + (cf_f9_htf_flip ? 1 : 0) + (cf_f10_fib ? 1 : 0)
// Short-form breakdown for the alert message — ✓/✗ per factor, HTF factor explicitly names which
// timeframe it checked (eliminates the "which CBC is this?" ambiguity when CBC15 and CBC60 disagree).
// Precomputed as a plain string HERE (not a function called from inside f_fire_alerts()) — Pine
// inlines user-defined function bodies at the call site, so calling a function that itself touches
// htf_timeframe (input-derived) from inside f_fire_alerts() was counting toward THAT function's own
// 254-external-element budget and pushed it over (CE10116). A plain precomputed string avoids that.
// v4.43: the Structure Break segment only appears when confluence_use_structure_break is on — kept
// as one appended, conditionally-empty string rather than restructuring the rest of the line.
// v4.74: reordered per follow-up (explicit new order: HTF/Cloud/Zone/Sweep unchanged as the core 4,
// then Volume/Fib/Structure/Level/Flip(LTF)/HTFFlip as the toggleable tail, in that exact sequence).
// Level and Flip's _reasons_part strings are now "leading-space" style (a leading "  " and no
// trailing space) to match Structure/Volume/HTFFlip/Fib's own format, since all 6 toggleable factors
// are now equally part of the appended tail rather than 2 of them being spliced inside the core
// string — every _reasons_part below can now be listed and reordered freely without touching
// cf_core_reasons_str itself.
string cf_f1_reasons_part = not confluence_use_flip ? "" : "  " + (cf_f1_flip ? "✓" : "✗") + "Flip(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + ")"
string cf_f4_reasons_part = not confluence_use_level ? "" : "  " + (cf_f4_level ? "✓" : "✗") + "Level"
string cf_f7_reasons_part = not confluence_use_structure_break ? "" : "  " + (cf_f7_structure_break ? "✓" : "✗") + "Structure"
string cf_f8_reasons_part = not confluence_use_volume ? "" : "  " + (cf_f8_volume ? "✓" : "✗") + "Volume"
string cf_f9_reasons_part = not confluence_use_htf_flip ? "" : "  " + (cf_f9_htf_flip ? "✓" : "✗") + "HTFFlip"
string cf_f10_reasons_part = not confluence_use_fib ? "" : "  " + (cf_f10_fib ? "✓" : "✗") + "Fib"
// v4.50: split into "core" (the always-on factors) and "extended" (the toggleable ones) pieces,
// reassembled into confluence_reasons_str below — the full-name alert breakdown used by a_status_msg.
// v4.74: core is now exactly HTF/Cloud/Zone/Sweep (Level and Flip(LTF) moved into the extended tail
// below, since both became toggleable in v4.71/v4.72); extended order is Volume, Fib, Structure,
// Level, Flip(LTF), HTFFlip.
string cf_core_reasons_str = (cf_f2_htf ? "✓" : "✗") + "HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ")  " + (cf_f3_cloud ? "✓" : "✗") + "Cloud  " + (cf_f5_zone ? "✓" : "✗") + "Zone  " + (cf_f6_sweep ? "✓" : "✗") + "Sweep"
string cf_extended_reasons_str = cf_f8_reasons_part + cf_f10_reasons_part + cf_f7_reasons_part + cf_f4_reasons_part + cf_f1_reasons_part + cf_f9_reasons_part
string confluence_reasons_str = cf_core_reasons_str + cf_extended_reasons_str
// v4.52: info-only diagnostic — the raw, ungated "_touch" state of the 5 factors that have something
// currently able to zero them out of the actual score. v4.55: this table-only breakdown text (and the
// abbreviated _tbl variants added in v4.53 for it) has been REMOVED — replaced by the dedicated
// Confluence Factors cells table (f_render_confluence_cells() below), which shows the same
// counted/excluded-but-true/not-true distinction with real per-factor color instead of packed ✓/✗
// text, so a separate Info row (or an abbreviated table string) is no longer needed. The alert-facing
// confluence_reasons_str (full factor names, used in a_status_msg) is untouched — this cleanup only
// removed the now-unused table-display strings.
int confluence_max_score = 4 + (confluence_use_flip ? 1 : 0) + (confluence_use_level ? 1 : 0) + (confluence_use_structure_break ? 1 : 0) + (confluence_use_volume ? 1 : 0) + (confluence_use_htf_flip ? 1 : 0) + (confluence_use_fib ? 1 : 0)
bool confluence_high_new = confluence_score >= confluence_alert_threshold and confluence_score[1] < confluence_alert_threshold
// v3.75: pre-combine this alert's toggle with its trigger condition OUT HERE, so f_fire_alerts()
// below only has to reference one plain bool instead of the raw input.bool() toggle —
// f_fire_alerts() is already near Pine's 254 external-element ceiling (CE10116), and a directly-
// referenced input.bool() inside it counts double.
bool fire_confluence_high = alert_on_confluence and confluence_high_new

// ===========================
// VWAP Bounce - High R:R: a CBC flip right at VWAP while price is clear of the nearest major
// support/resistance level (Pivot Points PP/R1/R2/R3/S1/S2/S3, or ORB high/low) — nothing
// structural nearby to cap the move, so reward is high relative to risk.
// ===========================
f_vwap_bounce_min_dist() =>
    string root = syminfo.root
    root == "MES" or root == "MGC" ? 6.0 : root == "M2K" ? 4.0 : root == "MNQ" ? 60.0 : 6.0

vwap_bounce_min_dist = vwap_bounce_min_dist_override > 0 ? vwap_bounce_min_dist_override : f_vwap_bounce_min_dist()
bool near_vwap_now = not na(vwap_value) and math.abs(close - vwap_value) <= prox_approach_dist
float vwap_bounce_pivot_dist = math.min(math.min(math.min(math.abs(close - piv_pp), math.abs(close - piv_r1)), math.min(math.abs(close - piv_r2), math.abs(close - piv_r3))), math.min(math.min(math.abs(close - piv_s1), math.abs(close - piv_s2)), math.abs(close - piv_s3)))
float vwap_bounce_orb_dist = math.min(na(prox_or_hi) ? 1e10 : math.abs(close - prox_or_hi), na(prox_or_lo) ? 1e10 : math.abs(close - prox_or_lo))
float vwap_bounce_dist = math.min(vwap_bounce_pivot_dist, vwap_bounce_orb_dist)
bool vwap_bounce_setup = any_flip and near_vwap_now and vwap_bounce_dist >= vwap_bounce_min_dist
bool fire_vwap_bounce = alert_on_vwap_bounce and vwap_bounce_setup

// ===========================
// EMA20 Touch - Pullback Entry: price touches EMA20 on a REGRESSIVE (counter-trend) candle — i.e.
// the touch bar itself moves opposite to the trend (down into it during an uptrend, up into it during
// a downtrend) — while CBC confirms the trend is CONTINUING, not the regression. Checked separately
// for the chart's own (LTF) EMA20 and the HTF's EMA20, each reusing values already computed above
// (price_touches_ema20, htf_ema20, cbc, htf_dir_bull_now/bear_now) — no new loop, no new request.security.
// ===========================
bool price_touches_htf_ema20 = not na(htf_ema20) and low <= htf_ema20 and high >= htf_ema20
bool ema20_touch_ltf_setup = price_touches_ema20 and ((cbc and close < close[1]) or ((not cbc) and close > close[1]))
bool ema20_touch_htf_setup = price_touches_htf_ema20 and ((htf_dir_bull_now and close < close[1]) or (htf_dir_bear_now and close > close[1]))

// v4.15 — LTF EMA20 Touch alert limiter: only the first 3 touch alerts after each CONFIRMED (bar-close)
// HTF CBC flip are allowed to fire. ltf_touch_alert_count resets to 0 on htf_flip_bull/htf_flip_bear —
// the same confirmed, last-closed-HTF-bar flip that drives the "HTF CBC Flip" alert itself (built off
// htf_cbc_labels_f, lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off) — NOT the live/still-forming HTF flip and NOT the
// LTF's own flip. Once a touch alert fires, the next one is blocked for 3 bars (ltf_last_touch_alert_bar
// cooldown) even if still within the first 3 — so a multi-bar hover on the EMA doesn't count as several
// separate touches. Counters update only when fire_ema20_touch_ltf itself is true (i.e. only while the
// alert is toggled on and a real touch/regression setup occurs), not gated by alert_session_ok, so the
// count reflects actual price action rather than which session hours alerts happen to be allowed in.
var int ltf_touch_alert_count = 0
if htf_flip_bull or htf_flip_bear
    ltf_touch_alert_count := 0
// Sentinel far in the past (instead of na) so the cooldown check below needs no na() guard clause —
// bar_index is never anywhere near this on a real chart, so "never touched yet" always reads as
// "cooldown satisfied" without a separate branch, same trick used for htf_early_time_ok above.
var int ltf_last_touch_alert_bar = -1000000
bool fire_ema20_touch_ltf = alert_on_ema20_touch and ema20_touch_ltf_setup and ltf_touch_alert_count < 3 and (bar_index - ltf_last_touch_alert_bar > 3)
if fire_ema20_touch_ltf
    ltf_touch_alert_count := ltf_touch_alert_count + 1
    ltf_last_touch_alert_bar := bar_index
// HTF touch had no cooldown at all before v4.38 ("unlimited"); now enforces the same minimum every
// touch alert gets regardless of the bar-close/intra-bar toggle above — at least 1 full bar must
// elapse, and if that next bar is still forming, its own close must happen first. Same sentinel
// trick as ltf_last_touch_alert_bar above (never near bar_index on a real chart).
var int htf_touch_last_alert_bar = -1000000
bool htf_touch_gap_ok = bar_index - htf_touch_last_alert_bar > 1 or (bar_index - htf_touch_last_alert_bar == 1 and barstate.isconfirmed)
bool fire_ema20_touch_htf = alert_on_ema20_touch and ema20_touch_htf_setup and htf_touch_gap_ok
if fire_ema20_touch_htf
    htf_touch_last_alert_bar := bar_index



// Live/unconfirmed counterparts — uses the live-updating htf_cbc (same one driving the CBC agreement bar
// coloring and the Agreement - Early alert), which can flip before its HTF bar actually closes.
htf_flip_bull_live_raw = htf_enabled and htf_cbc and not htf_cbc[1]
htf_flip_bear_live_raw = htf_enabled and (not htf_cbc) and htf_cbc[1]
// Same de-dup pattern as the live Agreement alert above: don't re-email for the same still-forming HTF
// bar + same live direction just because it flickered out and back before that HTF bar actually closed.
var int last_htf_flip_live_bull_t = -1
var int last_htf_flip_live_bear_t = -1
htf_flip_bull_live = htf_flip_bull_live_raw and htf_live_t_close != last_htf_flip_live_bull_t
htf_flip_bear_live = htf_flip_bear_live_raw and htf_live_t_close != last_htf_flip_live_bear_t
if htf_flip_bull_live
    last_htf_flip_live_bull_t := htf_live_t_close
    last_htf_flip_live_bear_t := -1
if htf_flip_bear_live
    last_htf_flip_live_bear_t := htf_live_t_close
    last_htf_flip_live_bull_t := -1
// HTF LONG/SHORT labels: fully controlled by the toggle above on any chart timeframe (defaults to on,
// same as before v2.93/v2.94 — Pine's input.bool requires a true compile-time constant for its default,
// so a timeframe-dependent default isn't achievable; only the toggle itself controls display now).
htf_bull_change = show_htf_cbc_labels and htf_flip_bull
htf_bear_change = show_htf_cbc_labels and htf_flip_bear

// Counts how many times price has moved INTO the zone between EMA9(HTF) and EMA20(HTF) since the last HTF flip.
// Resets to 0 on every HTF flip. Only counts the transition into the zone — consecutive bars that stay inside
// it don't add to the count; price has to leave and come back to count again.
float htf_ema_lo = math.min(htf_ema9, htf_ema20)
float htf_ema_hi = math.max(htf_ema9, htf_ema20)
bool htf_between_now = close >= htf_ema_lo and close <= htf_ema_hi
bool htf_between_enter = htf_between_now and not htf_between_now[1]
// "Price Enters HTF EMA9/20 Zone" entry signal: HTF already confirmed a direction, and price just
// pulled into the HTF's own EMA9/EMA20 zone (having been outside it the prior bar) — worth watching
// for an entry. Suggested stop is the HTF's own EMA20 (close below it invalidates Long, above invalidates Short).
bool ltf20_reclaim_long = htf_dir_bull_now and htf_between_enter
bool ltf20_reclaim_short = htf_dir_bear_now and htf_between_enter

// ===========================
// HTF Pullback Entry - Structure Confirmed: HTF CBC flipped recently, price pulled back INTO the
// HTF's own EMA9/20 zone (not just touching EMA20 alone), and HTF market structure confirms the
// same direction (HH/HL for Long, LH/LL for Short). Reuses existing values only — htf_between_enter
// (already computed just above for the "Price Enters HTF EMA9/20 Zone" alert), htf_hi_struct/
// htf_lo_struct, htf_bars_since_flip, sig_htf_lo/hi (risk reference) — no new loop, no new
// request.security call.
// ===========================
bool htf_pullback_structure_long = htf_dir_bull_now and htf_between_enter and htf_hi_struct == "Higher High" and htf_lo_struct == "Higher Low" and htf_bars_since_flip <= htf_pullback_recent_bars
bool htf_pullback_structure_short = htf_dir_bear_now and htf_between_enter and htf_hi_struct == "Lower High" and htf_lo_struct == "Lower Low" and htf_bars_since_flip <= htf_pullback_recent_bars
bool fire_htf_pullback_structure_long = alert_on_htf_pullback_structure and htf_pullback_structure_long
// v3.86: plain precomputed copy of alert_agreement_ltf_join — referencing the raw input directly
// inside f_fire_alerts() (as the two lines below used to) counts toward that function's own 254-
// external-element budget on top of everything else already referenced there, tripping CE10116.
bool agreement_ltf_join_enabled = alert_agreement_ltf_join
bool fire_htf_pullback_structure_short = alert_on_htf_pullback_structure and htf_pullback_structure_short
string htf_pullback_bars_str = str.tostring(htf_pullback_recent_bars)
var int htf_between_count = 0
if htf_flip_bull or htf_flip_bear
    htf_between_count := 0
if htf_between_enter
    htf_between_count := htf_between_count + 1

// "In FVG of HTF Flip Candle" — is price currently trading back inside the 3-candle Fair Value Gap
// formed around the last confirmed HTF flip (htf_fvg_top/bot, computed above)? Reuses
// htf_fvg_exists/top/bot only — no new request.security call.
bool htf_in_flip_fvg = htf_fvg_exists and close <= htf_fvg_top and close >= htf_fvg_bot
// v4.41: replaced the old single-shot "just entered" edge trigger with a throttled repeat-fire —
// alerts intrabar (see the alert.freq_once_per_bar switch at the alert() call) at most once every
// fvg_alert_min_gap_bars LTF bars for as long as price stays in the zone, manually gap-tracked via
// htf_fvg_last_alert_bar since Pine's alert.freq_once_per_bar only guarantees "at most once THIS
// bar," not N bars apart — and stops entirely once ltf_bars_since_htf_flip exceeds
// fvg_alert_max_bars_after_flip bars past the flip that formed this gap.
var int htf_fvg_last_alert_bar = -1
bool htf_fvg_gap_ok = (bar_index - htf_fvg_last_alert_bar) >= fvg_alert_min_gap_bars
bool htf_fvg_within_window = ltf_bars_since_htf_flip <= fvg_alert_max_bars_after_flip
bool fire_htf_flip_fvg = alert_on_htf_flip_fvg and htf_in_flip_fvg and htf_fvg_gap_ok and htf_fvg_within_window
if fire_htf_flip_fvg
    htf_fvg_last_alert_bar := bar_index

float htf_arrow_up_y = low - (high - low) * 0.35
float htf_arrow_dn_y = high + (high - low) * 0.35
htf_flip_arrow_sz = htf_flip_triangle_size == "Tiny" ? size.tiny : htf_flip_triangle_size == "Small" ? size.small : htf_flip_triangle_size == "Normal" ? size.normal : htf_flip_triangle_size == "Large" ? size.large : size.huge
if show_htf_flip_arrows
    if htf_flip_bull
        label.new(x=bar_index, y=htf_arrow_up_y, text="", color=col_htf_long, style=label.style_triangleup, size=htf_flip_arrow_sz)
    if htf_flip_bear
        label.new(x=bar_index, y=htf_arrow_dn_y, text="", color=col_htf_short, style=label.style_triangledown, size=htf_flip_arrow_sz)

// Draw labels for HTF changes (on LTF, x/y snap to LTF bar that best matches HTF signal low/high in the completed HTF window)
if htf_bull_change
    int bull_lbl_x = bar_index
    float bull_lbl_y = low - (high - low) * 0.3
    if ltf_relocate_htf_labels and not na(sig_htf_lo) and not na(sig_htf_t_open) and not na(sig_htf_t_close)
        [bull_anch_x, bull_anch_y] = f_htf_anchor_long(sig_htf_lo, sig_htf_t_open, sig_htf_t_close, max_htf_lbl_off)
        bull_lbl_x := bull_anch_x
        bull_lbl_y := bull_anch_y
    else if htf_same_tf_chart and bar_index > 0
        bull_lbl_x := bar_index - 1
        bull_lbl_y := low[1] - (high[1] - low[1]) * 0.3
    label.new(
         x=bull_lbl_x,
         y=bull_lbl_y,
         text=htf_label_abbreviate ? "L" : "LONG",
         color=color.new(col_htf_long, 80),
         textcolor=col_htf_long_text,
         style=label.style_label_up,
         size=htf_label_size == "tiny" ? size.tiny : htf_label_size == "small" ? size.small : htf_label_size == "normal" ? size.normal : htf_label_size == "large" ? size.large : size.huge
     )

if htf_bear_change
    int bear_lbl_x = bar_index
    float bear_lbl_y = high + (high - low) * 0.3
    if ltf_relocate_htf_labels and not na(sig_htf_hi) and not na(sig_htf_t_open) and not na(sig_htf_t_close)
        [bear_anch_x, bear_anch_y] = f_htf_anchor_short(sig_htf_hi, sig_htf_t_open, sig_htf_t_close, max_htf_lbl_off)
        bear_lbl_x := bear_anch_x
        bear_lbl_y := bear_anch_y
    else if htf_same_tf_chart and bar_index > 0
        bear_lbl_x := bar_index - 1
        bear_lbl_y := high[1] + (high[1] - low[1]) * 0.3
    label.new(
         x=bear_lbl_x,
         y=bear_lbl_y,
         text=htf_label_abbreviate ? "S" : "SHORT",
         color=color.new(col_htf_short, 80),
         textcolor=col_htf_short_text,
         style=label.style_label_down,
         size=htf_label_size == "tiny" ? size.tiny : htf_label_size == "small" ? size.small : htf_label_size == "normal" ? size.normal : htf_label_size == "large" ? size.large : size.huge
     )
