// ===========================
// Status table (Tier 1, color-coded)
// ===========================
f_status_pos(string p) =>
    switch p
        "Top left" => position.top_left
        "Top center" => position.top_center
        "Top right" => position.top_right
        "Middle left" => position.middle_left
        "Middle center" => position.middle_center
        "Middle right" => position.middle_right
        "Bottom left" => position.bottom_left
        "Bottom center" => position.bottom_center
        "Bottom right" => position.bottom_right
        => position.top_right

chart_tf_disp() =>
    string r = timeframe.isminutes ? str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + "m" : timeframe.isseconds ? str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + "s" : timeframe.isdaily ? str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + "D" : timeframe.isweekly ? str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + "W" : timeframe.ismonthly ? str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + "M" : timeframe.period
    r

htf_tf_disp(string tf) =>
    int sec = timeframe.in_seconds(tf)
    string r = tf
    if not na(sec) and sec > 0
        if sec == 86400
            r := "1D"
        else if sec == 604800
            r := "1W"
        else if sec < 86400 and sec % 3600 == 0
            r := str.tostring(sec / 3600) + "H"
        else if sec < 86400 * 7 and sec % 60 == 0
            r := str.tostring(sec / 60) + "m"
        else
            r := tf
    r

// None of these three change during a compile run (chart timeframe, HTF, and MTF are all fixed
// once resolved), but chart_tf_disp()/htf_tf_disp() were being called 43 times combined throughout
// the file, each a genuine ternary/conditional chain re-evaluated from scratch. Computed once here
// and reused everywhere via find-and-replace on every call site — same value, far less repeated work.
chart_tf_disp_val = chart_tf_disp()
htf_tf_disp_val = htf_tf_disp(htf_timeframe)
mtf_tf_disp_val = htf_tf_disp(mtf_timeframe)

f_table_text_size(string s) =>
    switch s
        "Tiny" => size.tiny
        "Small" => size.small
        "Normal" => size.normal
        "Large" => size.large
        "Huge" => size.huge
        => size.small

tbl_label_bg = color.new(#1E293B, 0)
tbl_header_bg = color.new(#0C1222, 0)
tbl_border = color.new(#475569, 55)
tbl_frame = color.new(#64748B, 35)
st_bull = color.new(#047857, 12)
st_bear = color.new(#9B1C1C, 12)
st_neutral = color.new(#374151, 8)
// v4.55: amber — "true but excluded" state for the Confluence Factors cells table (a factor whose
// touch condition is met right now, but isn't counted because a recency window or an include/exclude
// toggle currently zeroes it out). Distinct from st_bull (counted) and st_neutral (not true at all).
st_amber = color.new(#B45309, 12)
st_or_in = color.new(#000000, 10)
txt_on = color.new(#F8FAFC, 0)
txt_label = color.new(#F1F5F9, 0)
txt_head = color.new(#CBD5E1, 0)
tbl_cbc_label_bg = color.new(#0A1628, 0)
txt_cbc_label = color.new(#93C5FD, 0)

f_render_status_table() =>
    var table status_tbl = na
    var string status_tbl_pos_last = ""
    var bool status_tbl_collapsed_last = false
    var string status_field_set_last = ""
    if not show_status_table
        table.delete(status_tbl)
        status_tbl := na
        status_tbl_pos_last := ""
    else
        if not na(status_tbl) and (status_tbl_pos_last != status_table_position or status_tbl_collapsed_last != status_tbl_collapsed or status_field_set_last != status_field_set)
            table.delete(status_tbl)
            status_tbl := na
        if na(status_tbl)
            int fld_grp = (show_ltf_in_tables ? 1 : 0) + (show_htf_in_tables ? 1 : 0) + (show_mtf_in_tables ? 1 : 0)
            int status_row_count = status_tbl_collapsed ? 1 : math.max(1, (show_fld_market ? 1 : 0) + (show_fld_price ? 1 : 0) + (show_fld_cbc ? fld_grp : 0) + (show_fld_opening_range ? 1 : 0) + (show_fld_ema_cloud ? fld_grp : 0) + (show_fld_vwap ? 1 : 0) + (show_fld_ema200 ? 1 : 0) + (show_fld_structure ? fld_grp : 0) + (eff_show_fld_last_flip ? fld_grp : 0) + (eff_show_fld_stop ? 1 : 0) + (eff_show_fld_buysell ? 1 : 0) + (eff_show_chart_info_tbl ? 1 : 0) + (eff_show_ltf_pivots_tbl ? 1 : 0) + (eff_show_htf_pivots_tbl ? 1 : 0) + (eff_show_mtf_pivots_tbl ? 1 : 0) + (eff_show_fld_flip_level ? fld_grp : 0) + (eff_show_fld_confluence ? 1 : 0) + (show_fld_fib_zone ? 1 : 0))
            status_tbl := table.new(f_status_pos(status_table_position), 2, status_row_count, bgcolor=color.new(#020617, 0), border_color=tbl_border, border_width=1, frame_color=tbl_frame, frame_width=2)
            if status_tbl_collapsed
                table.merge_cells(status_tbl, 0, 0, 1, 0)
            status_tbl_collapsed_last := status_tbl_collapsed
            status_tbl_pos_last := status_table_position
            status_field_set_last := status_field_set
        cloud_bull = ema_bullish
        vwap_bull = not na(vwap_value) and close > vwap_value
        ema200_bull = not na(ema200) and close > ema200
        string mkt_tbl = f_market_session_et()
        float or_f_hi = mkt_tbl == "Asian" ? ORHigh_AS : mkt_tbl == "London" ? ORHigh_LN : ORHigh_NY
        float or_f_lo = mkt_tbl == "Asian" ? ORLow_AS : mkt_tbl == "London" ? ORLow_LN : ORLow_NY
        bool or_f_on = (mkt_tbl == "Asian" and or_asian) or (mkt_tbl == "London" and or_london) or (mkt_tbl == "New York" and or_ny)
        int or_f_open = mkt_tbl == "Asian" ? as_or_open : mkt_tbl == "London" ? ln_or_open : ny_or_open
        or_right = st_neutral
        or_msg = "—"
        if not show_any_or
            or_msg := "Off"
        else if not or_f_on
            or_msg := "—"
        else if time < or_f_open
            or_msg := "Before open"
        else if na(or_f_hi) or na(or_f_lo)
            or_msg := "Pending"
        else if close > or_f_hi
            or_msg := str.tostring(or_f_hi, "#.##") + "  " + ((close - or_f_hi) >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(close - or_f_hi, "#.#")
            or_right := st_bull
        else if close < or_f_lo
            or_msg := str.tostring(or_f_lo, "#.##") + "  " + ((close - or_f_lo) >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(close - or_f_lo, "#.#")
            or_right := st_bear
        else
            or_msg := "In Range"
            or_right := st_or_in
        cloud_right = cloud_bull ? st_bull : st_bear
        cloud_msg = cloud_bull ? "Bullish" : "Bearish"
        v_right = not na(vwap_value) ? (vwap_bull ? st_bull : st_bear) : st_neutral
        float v_diff = not na(vwap_value) ? close - vwap_value : na
        v_msg = not na(vwap_value) ? (vwap_bull ? "Above " : "Below ") + (v_diff >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(v_diff, "#.#") : "—"
        e200_right = not na(ema200) ? (ema200_bull ? st_bull : st_bear) : st_neutral
        float e200_diff = not na(ema200) ? close - ema200 : na
        e200_msg = not na(ema200) ? (ema200_bull ? "Above " : "Below ") + (e200_diff >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(e200_diff, "#.#") : "—"
        htf_right = not htf_enabled ? st_neutral : na(htf_cbc_labels_f) ? st_neutral : (htf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0 ? st_bull : st_bear)
        bool htf_live_mismatch = htf_enabled and not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and ((htf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0) != htf_cbc)
        htf_msg = not htf_enabled ? "Off" : na(htf_cbc_labels_f) ? "—" : (htf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0 ? "Bullish" : "Bearish") + (htf_live_mismatch ? "  ⚠ live " + (htf_cbc ? "Bull" : "Bear") : "")
        ltf_right = cbc ? st_bull : st_bear
        ltf_msg = cbc ? "Bullish" : "Bearish"
        color mkt_session_bg = mkt_tbl == "Asian" ? col_orb_as_lbl : mkt_tbl == "London" ? col_orb_ln_lbl : color.new(col_orb_ny_lbl, 35)
        color mkt_session_txt = txt_on
        tbl_txt_sz = f_table_text_size(status_table_text_size)
        if status_tbl_collapsed
            tbl_hdr_txt = str.repeat("\n", status_table_header_newlines) + "MapleStax CBC  ·  " + syminfo.ticker
            table.cell(status_tbl, 0, 0, tbl_hdr_txt, bgcolor=tbl_header_bg, text_color=txt_head, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_center)
        int r = 0
        array<string> field_order = f_field_render_order()
        array<int> tf_order = f_ltf_htf_mtf_order()
        for i = 0 to (status_tbl_collapsed ? -1 : array.size(field_order) - 1)
            string fid = array.get(field_order, i)
            if fid == "market" and show_fld_market
                table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "Market", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="Which trading session is currently")
                table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, mkt_tbl, bgcolor=mkt_session_bg, text_color=mkt_session_txt, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                r := r + 1
            // v4.61: Price — new Essential-level row per follow-up ("add price to the essential below
            // market"), default order slot 2 so it sits directly under Market. Colored by simple tick
            // direction vs the prior bar's close (green up, red down, gray unchanged) — same st_bull/
            // st_bear/st_neutral used everywhere else in this table, not tied to CBC/HTF direction.
            else if fid == "price" and show_fld_price
                color price_right = close > close[1] ? st_bull : close < close[1] ? st_bear : st_neutral
                table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "Price", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="Current close. Green/red on an up/down tick vs the prior bar's close, gray when unchanged.")
                table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, str.tostring(close, "#.##"), bgcolor=price_right, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                r := r + 1
            else if fid == "cbc" and show_fld_cbc
                color mtf_right = not mtf_enabled ? st_neutral : na(mtf_cbc_labels_f) ? st_neutral : (mtf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0 ? st_bull : st_bear)
                string mtf_msg = not mtf_enabled ? "Off" : na(mtf_cbc_labels_f) ? "—" : (mtf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0 ? "Bullish" : "Bearish")
                r := f_add_tf_row(status_tbl, r, "CBC",
                                   ltf_msg, ltf_right,
                                   htf_msg, htf_right,
                                   mtf_msg, mtf_right,
                                   show_ltf_in_tables, show_htf_in_tables, show_mtf_in_tables, show_mtf_cbc,
                                   chart_tf_disp_val, htf_tf_disp_val, mtf_tf_disp_val,
                                   tbl_cbc_label_bg, txt_cbc_label, txt_on, tbl_txt_sz, tf_order,
                                   "Close-Break-Confirmation direction on the", "Same CBC logic, computed on the HTF.", "Same Close-Break-Confirmation logic as")
            else if fid == "or" and show_fld_opening_range
                table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "Opening range", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="Price vs. the current session's Opening")
                table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, or_msg, bgcolor=or_right, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                r := r + 1
            else if fid == "cloud" and show_fld_ema_cloud
                color htf_cloud_right = htf_ema_bullish ? st_bull : st_bear
                string htf_cloud_msg = htf_ema_bullish ? "Bullish" : "Bearish"
                color mtf_cloud_right = mtf_ema_bullish ? st_bull : st_bear
                string mtf_cloud_msg = mtf_ema_bullish ? "Bullish" : "Bearish"
                r := f_add_tf_row(status_tbl, r, "EMA cloud",
                                   cloud_msg, cloud_right,
                                   htf_cloud_msg, htf_cloud_right,
                                   mtf_cloud_msg, mtf_cloud_right,
                                   show_ltf_in_tables, show_htf_in_tables, show_mtf_in_tables, show_mtf_cloud,
                                   chart_tf_disp_val, htf_tf_disp_val, mtf_tf_disp_val,
                                   tbl_label_bg, txt_label, txt_on, tbl_txt_sz, tf_order,
                                   "Direction of the EMA9/EMA20 cloud on the", "Same EMA9/EMA20 cloud logic as the LTF", "Same EMA9/EMA20 cloud logic as the")
            else if fid == "vwap" and show_fld_vwap
                table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "VWAP", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="Whether price is currently Above or Below")
                table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, v_msg, bgcolor=v_right, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                r := r + 1
            else if fid == "ema200" and show_fld_ema200
                table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "EMA 200", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="Whether price is currently Above or Below")
                table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, e200_msg, bgcolor=e200_right, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                r := r + 1
            // v4.54: Fib Zone — Essential-level row (own checkbox, not Full-gated, per follow-up),
            // reusing fib_50/fib_618/fib_valid already computed above alongside the Fib confluence
            // factor. v4.61 changed the green/neutral rule to fib_price_in_zone (see below) — no

Green + \"IN\" whenever price is currently between the two levels (unlike the Confluence Score's own Fib factor, this row's color ignores the recency window).")

            else if fid == "structure" and show_fld_structure

                string ltf_struct_msg = f_struct_abbrev(ltf_hi_struct) + " / " + f_struct_abbrev(ltf_lo_struct)
                color htf_struct_right = (htf_hi_struct == "Higher High" and htf_lo_struct == "Higher Low") ? st_bull : (htf_hi_struct == "Lower High" and htf_lo_struct == "Lower Low") ? st_bear : st_neutral

                color mtf_struct_right = (mtf_hi_struct == "Higher High" and mtf_lo_struct == "Higher Low") ? st_bull : (mtf_hi_struct == "Lower High" and mtf_lo_struct == "Lower Low") ? st_bear : st_neutral

                                   chart_tf_disp_val, htf_tf_disp_val, mtf_tf_disp_val,

                    string ltf_flip_dir_s = na(ltf_last_flip_t) ? "" : (ltf_last_flip_bull ? "LONG  " : "SHORT  ")

                    table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "CBC (" + mtf_tf_disp_val + ") last flip", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="Same as the LTF/HTF last-flip rows")
                    table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, mtf_flip_dir_s + f_fmt_ny_time(mtf_last_flip_t) + "  #" + str.tostring(mtf_bars_since_flip), bgcolor=mtf_flip_bg, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                    r := r + 1
            else if fid == "stop" and eff_show_fld_stop
                table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "LTF Trailing Stop", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="'CBC': prior closed LTF candle's low/high")
                color stop_right = na(htf_trail_stop) ? st_neutral : (cbc ? st_bull : st_bear)

                table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, stop_msg, bgcolor=stop_right, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)

            else if fid == "fliplevel" and eff_show_fld_flip_level
                if show_ltf_in_tables
                    table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "Flip Level (" + chart_tf_disp_val + ")", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="The exact price that would trigger the")
                    color ltf_flip_lvl_right = na(ltf_flip_price) ? st_neutral : (cbc[1] ? st_bear : st_bull)
