                    color htf_flip_lvl_right = na(htf_flip_price) ? st_neutral : (htf_cbc[1] ? st_bear : st_bull)
                    string htf_flip_lvl_msg = na(htf_flip_price) ? "—" : str.tostring(htf_flip_price, "#.##") + "  " + (htf_cbc[1] ? "below" : "above")
                    table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, htf_flip_lvl_msg, bgcolor=htf_flip_lvl_right, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                    r := r + 1
                if show_mtf_in_tables and show_mtf_fliplevel
                    color mtf_flip_lvl_right = na(mtf_flip_price) ? st_neutral : (mtf_cbc[1] ? st_bear : st_bull)
                    string mtf_flip_lvl_msg = na(mtf_flip_price) ? "—" : str.tostring(mtf_flip_price, "#.##") + "  " + (mtf_cbc[1] ? "below" : "above")
                    table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "Flip Level (" + mtf_tf_disp_val + ")", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="Same as the LTF/HTF Flip Level rows,")
                    table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, mtf_flip_lvl_msg, bgcolor=mtf_flip_lvl_right, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                    r := r + 1
            else if fid == "entryzone" and eff_show_fld_buysell
                table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "Entry Zone / FVG", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="BRSG retracement zone anchored to the")
                float brsg_zone_lo = na(brsg_zone_top) ? na : math.min(brsg_zone_top, brsg_zone_bot)
                float brsg_zone_hi = na(brsg_zone_top) ? na : math.max(brsg_zone_top, brsg_zone_bot)
                color brsg_zone_right = na(brsg_zone_top) ? st_neutral : (htf_last_flip_bull ? st_bull : st_bear)
                // v4.40: appended the HTF Flip FVG range (htf_fvg_top/bot, same freeze block that
                // feeds the new chart zone) plus an "IN" tag when price is currently trading back
                // inside it (htf_in_flip_fvg) — folded into this existing row rather than a new one.
                string fvg_part = htf_fvg_exists ? ("  |  FVG " + str.tostring(htf_fvg_bot, "#.##") + "–" + str.tostring(htf_fvg_top, "#.##") + (htf_in_flip_fvg ? " IN" : "")) : ""
                string brsg_zone_msg = na(brsg_zone_top) ? "—" : (htf_last_flip_bull ? "LONG  " : "SHORT  ") + str.tostring(brsg_zone_lo, "#.##") + " – " + str.tostring(brsg_zone_hi, "#.##") + fvg_part
                table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, brsg_zone_msg, bgcolor=brsg_zone_right, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                r := r + 1
            else if fid == "chart" and eff_show_chart_info_tbl
                table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "Chart", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="The symbol/ticker this chart is on, plus")
                table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, syminfo.ticker + "  " + chart_tf_disp_val, bgcolor=st_neutral, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                r := r + 1
            else if fid == "pivltf" and eff_show_ltf_pivots_tbl
                string ltf_piv_msg = (na(l_last_hi) and na(l_last_lo)) ? "—" : "H " + (na(l_last_hi) ? "—" : str.tostring(l_last_hi, "#.##")) + "  /  L " + (na(l_last_lo) ? "—" : str.tostring(l_last_lo, "#.##"))
                table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "Pivot (" + chart_tf_disp_val + ")", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="Most recent LTF (chart-timeframe) pivot")
                table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, ltf_piv_msg, bgcolor=st_neutral, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                r := r + 1
            else if fid == "pivhtf" and eff_show_htf_pivots_tbl
                string htf_piv_msg = (na(h_last_hi) and na(h_last_lo)) ? "—" : "H " + (na(h_last_hi) ? "—" : str.tostring(h_last_hi, "#.##")) + "  /  L " + (na(h_last_lo) ? "—" : str.tostring(h_last_lo, "#.##"))
                table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "Pivot (" + htf_tf_disp_val + ")", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="Most recent HTF pivot high and pivot low")
                table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, htf_piv_msg, bgcolor=st_neutral, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                r := r + 1
            else if fid == "pivmtf" and eff_show_mtf_pivots_tbl
                string mtf_piv_msg = (na(m_last_hi) and na(m_last_lo)) ? "—" : "H " + (na(m_last_hi) ? "—" : str.tostring(m_last_hi, "#.##")) + "  /  L " + (na(m_last_lo) ? "—" : str.tostring(m_last_lo, "#.##"))
                table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "Pivot (" + mtf_tf_disp_val + ")", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="Most recent MTF pivot high and pivot low")
                table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, mtf_piv_msg, bgcolor=st_neutral, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                r := r + 1
            // v4.48: combined row for all Confluence Score factors — reuses
            // confluence_score/confluence_max_score/confluence_alert_threshold, already computed
            // above. v4.55: collapsed back down to ONE simple row per follow-up ("Info seems
            // redundant... distinguish the counted ones") — the packed-string breakdown (rows 1-2) and
            // the Info row are both gone. The full per-factor breakdown, now with real per-factor
            // color instead of packed text, lives in the new dedicated Confluence Factors cells table
            // (f_render_confluence_cells() below) — see confluence_use_cells_table. This row just
            // shows the number.
            else if fid == "confluence" and eff_show_fld_confluence
                color confluence_right = confluence_score >= confluence_alert_threshold ? st_bull : st_neutral
                table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "Confluence Score", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_label, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left, tooltip="Live score across all enabled confluence factors. Green when at/above the alert threshold. See the separate Confluence Factors table for the per-factor breakdown.")
                table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, "S:" + str.tostring(confluence_score) + "/" + str.tostring(confluence_max_score), bgcolor=confluence_right, text_color=txt_on, text_size=tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_right)
                r := r + 1
    true

// Dedicated small table for Pivot Points (Daily, Standard) — kept entirely separate from the
// Status table / Historical Bar Panel on purpose, since those two functions already sit close to
// Pine's 254-external-element ceiling (hit it once already, CE10116, in v3.53) and cramming more
// rows into them risked tripping it again for no real benefit — this data doesn't need to share a
// table with everything else.
f_render_pivot_table() =>
    var table piv_tbl = na
    if show_pivot_table
        // "Closest 3" filtering: distance-sort the ENABLED levels only (disabled ones get a huge
        // placeholder distance so they never get picked), then keep just the 3 nearest to price.
        // Kept entirely local to this table — the alert email line and Key Level Alert still use
        // the full piv_enabled set regardless of this toggle.
        var float[] piv_dist_arr = array.new_float(7, na)
        for di = 0 to 6
            array.set(piv_dist_arr, di, array.get(piv_enabled, di) ? math.abs(close - array.get(piv_price_arr, di)) : 1e10)
        array<int> piv_dist_order = array.sort_indices(piv_dist_arr, order.ascending)
        var bool[] piv_show_final = array.new_bool(7, false)
        if show_pivot_closest3
            for si = 0 to 6
                array.set(piv_show_final, si, false)
            for ci = 0 to 2
                array.set(piv_show_final, array.get(piv_dist_order, ci), true)
        else
            for si = 0 to 6
                array.set(piv_show_final, si, array.get(piv_enabled, si))

        int piv_cols_count = 0
        for pci = 0 to 6
            if array.get(piv_show_final, pci)
                piv_cols_count := piv_cols_count + 1
        int piv_cols = math.max(1, piv_cols_count)
        if na(piv_tbl)
            piv_tbl := table.new(f_status_pos(pivot_table_position), piv_cols, 2, bgcolor=color.new(#020617, 0), border_color=tbl_border, border_width=1, frame_color=tbl_frame, frame_width=2)
        string piv_txt_sz = pivot_table_text_size == "Tiny" ? size.tiny : pivot_table_text_size == "Small" ? size.small : pivot_table_text_size == "Normal" ? size.normal : pivot_table_text_size == "Large" ? size.large : size.huge
        int piv_c = 0
        for pi = 0 to 6
            if array.get(piv_show_final, pi)
                float pi_val = array.get(piv_price_arr, pi)
                color pi_lbl_bg = pi == 3 ? col_piv_pp : pi < 3 ? col_piv_r : col_piv_s
                table.cell(piv_tbl, piv_c, 0, array.get(piv_labels, pi), bgcolor=pi_lbl_bg, text_color=txt_on, text_size=piv_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_center)
                table.cell(piv_tbl, piv_c, 1, str.tostring(pi_val, "#.##"), bgcolor=close > pi_val ? st_bull : st_bear, text_color=txt_on, text_size=piv_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_center)
                piv_c := piv_c + 1
    true

// v4.55: Confluence Factors cells table — dedicated small table (same pattern as the Pivot Points
// table above), one cell per confluence factor so each can carry its own color. v4.74 order: HTF/
// Cloud/Zone/Sweep/Volume/Fib/Structure/Level/Flip(LTF)/HTFFlip. Color rule, uniform across all 10 factor
// cells: counted (contributes to confluence_score right now) = green; not counted but its raw touch
// condition IS true (excluded only by a recency window or its own include/exclude toggle) = amber;
// otherwise = gray. Reuses every cf_f*/cf_f*_touch boolean already computed above — no new signal
// logic, just a different display of existing values. Replaces the old packed-string row 1/row 2/Info
// row entirely.
// v4.69: two follow-ups. (1) Added a leading Score cell ("S:count/max", same text/color rule as the
// Status table's own Confluence Score row) at the very front, per follow-up ("where is the confluence
// score at the beginning") — the table is now 11 cells, not 10. (2) The HTF/Cloud/Level/Zone group
// (the four requested for a border) is bracketed with literal "[" / "]" text markers on the first and
// last cell of that group, per follow-up with an annotated screenshot circling them. This is a text-
// based visual approximation, not a real border: Pine's table API (table.new/table.cell) has no way to
// draw a border, outline, or distinct gridline color around an arbitrary subset of cells — border_
// width/border_color draw uniform lines between EVERY cell in the whole table, and frame_width/frame_
// color only wrap the table's own outer edge. A real border would require a second, separate table
// (its own frame IS a real border) — but Pine tables only snap to 9 fixed screen positions, so a
// second table can't be guaranteed to sit pixel-adjacent to this one without a gap or overlap, and at
// the same corner position two tables actually overlap each other rather than sitting side by side.
// The bracket text is the safest working approximation; happy to try the two-table approach instead
// if the brackets don't read well on the chart.
f_render_confluence_cells() =>
    var table conf_tbl = na
    var string conf_pos_last = ""
    var int conf_rows_last = 0
    if not confluence_use_cells_table
        table.delete(conf_tbl)
        conf_tbl := na
        conf_pos_last := ""
        conf_rows_last := 0
    else
        int conf_rows = math.min(11, math.max(1, confluence_cells_rows))
        int conf_cols = int(math.ceil(11.0 / conf_rows))
        // v4.58: re-create (delete + rebuild) whenever position or the row count changes, same
        // pattern f_render_status_table() already uses for its own position/collapsed/field-set
        // changes — a table's column/row COUNT is fixed at table.new() time and won't auto-resize.
        if not na(conf_tbl) and (conf_pos_last != confluence_cells_position or conf_rows_last != conf_rows)
            table.delete(conf_tbl)
            conf_tbl := na
        // v4.56 fix (CE10272, undeclared identifier "tbl_txt_sz"): tbl_txt_sz is local to
        // f_render_status_table()'s own scope, not visible here in this sibling function — Pine
        // functions don't share local variables. Same fix pattern the Pivot Points table already
        // uses for piv_txt_sz: compute a local text-size string here via the top-level
        // f_table_text_size() helper, reusing the same status_table_text_size input so it still
        // visually matches the main Status table's size setting.
        string conf_txt_sz = f_table_text_size(status_table_text_size)
        if na(conf_tbl)
            conf_tbl := table.new(f_status_pos(confluence_cells_position), conf_cols, conf_rows, bgcolor=color.new(#020617, 0), border_color=tbl_border, border_width=1, frame_color=tbl_frame, frame_width=2)
            conf_pos_last := confluence_cells_position
            conf_rows_last := conf_rows
        // v4.58: the 10 factors as parallel arrays (fixed order: Flip/HTF/Cloud/Level/Zone/Sweep/
        // Structure/Volume/HTFFlip/Fib) instead of 10 hardcoded table.cell calls — lets the loop below
        // place them into however many rows/columns confluence_cells_rows/conf_cols works out to,
        // filling left-to-right then wrapping (row = i / conf_cols, col = i % conf_cols). Same
        // ✓/✗ text and green/amber/gray color rule as before — no signal-logic change, just a display
        // reflow.
        // v4.74: reordered again per explicit follow-up — core 4 (HTF/Cloud/Zone/Sweep, cells 0-3)
        // unchanged, followed by the toggleable tail in this exact requested order: Volume, Fib,
        // Structure, Level, Flip(LTF), HTFFlip (cells 4-9). The "[" "]" bracket markers moved with the
        // core group — they now wrap HTF..Sweep (the new cells 0-3) instead of the old HTF/Cloud/
        // Level/Zone grouping, since Level is no longer adjacent to HTF/Cloud/Zone. Flagged to the
        // user: shout if this isn't the bracket placement you wanted now that Level moved.
        array<string> conf_texts = array.from("[" + (cf_f2_htf ? "✓" : "✗") + "H" + htf_timeframe, (cf_f3_cloud ? "✓" : "✗") + "Cld", (cf_f5_zone ? "✓" : "✗") + "Zn", (cf_f6_sweep ? "✓" : "✗") + "Swp" + "]", (cf_f8_volume ? "✓" : "✗") + "Vol", (cf_f10_fib ? "✓" : "✗") + "Fib", (cf_f7_structure_break ? "✓" : "✗") + "Str", (cf_f4_level ? "✓" : "✗") + "Lvl", (cf_f1_flip ? "✓" : "✗") + "Flp" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier), (cf_f9_htf_flip ? "✓" : "✗") + "HFV")
        array<color> conf_colors = array.from(cf_f2_htf ? st_bull : st_neutral, cf_f3_cloud ? st_bull : st_neutral, cf_f5_zone ? st_bull : (cf_f5_zone_touch ? st_amber : st_neutral), cf_f6_sweep ? st_bull : (cf_f6_sweep_touch ? st_amber : st_neutral), cf_f8_volume ? st_bull : (cf_f8_volume_touch ? st_amber : st_neutral), cf_f10_fib ? st_bull : (cf_f10_fib_touch ? st_amber : st_neutral), cf_f7_structure_break ? st_bull : (cf_f7_structure_touch ? st_amber : st_neutral), cf_f4_level ? st_bull : (cf_f4_level_touch ? st_amber : st_neutral), cf_f1_flip ? st_bull : (cf_f1_flip_touch ? st_amber : st_neutral), cf_f9_htf_flip ? st_bull : (cf_f9_htf_flip_touch ? st_amber : st_neutral))
        array<string> conf_tips = array.from("HTF — the HTF CBC direction agrees with the current LTF direction.", "Cloud — the LTF EMA9/20 cloud agrees with the current direction.", "Zone — BRSG zone or HTF Flip FVG touch. Amber = touched, but outside the recency window.", "Sweep — liquidity sweep of the last confirmed HTF swing. Amber = touched, but outside the recency window.", "Volume Spike on the HTF flip candle. Amber = touched, but its toggle is off.", "Fib Golden Pocket (50%-61.8%) touch. Amber = touched, but outside the recency window or its toggle is off.", "Structure Break (BOS/CHoCH). Amber = touched, but its toggle is off.", "Level — price is currently near a key level. Amber = touched, but its toggle is off (off by default).", "Flip (LTF) — valid from the LTF's own flip until the next reverse flip. Amber = touched, but its toggle is off (off by default).", "HTF Flip FVG — true while the current flip's Fair Value Gap remains unfilled; invalidated the instant price trades back into it. Amber = true, but its toggle is off (off by default).")
        // v4.69: leading Score cell (index 0) reuses confluence_score/confluence_max_score/
        // confluence_alert_threshold — same values and same green/gray rule as the Status table's own
        // Confluence Score row — so the total is visible right on this table too, not just there.
        string conf_score_text = "S:" + str.tostring(confluence_score) + "/" + str.tostring(confluence_max_score)
        color conf_score_color = confluence_score >= confluence_alert_threshold ? st_bull : st_neutral
        for i = 0 to 10
            string conf_cell_txt = i == 0 ? conf_score_text : array.get(conf_texts, i - 1)
            color conf_cell_bg = i == 0 ? conf_score_color : array.get(conf_colors, i - 1)
            string conf_cell_tip = i == 0 ? "Live Confluence Score — counted factors / max possible (the denominator shrinks if a toggleable factor is switched off above)." : array.get(conf_tips, i - 1)
            table.cell(conf_tbl, i % conf_cols, i / conf_cols, conf_cell_txt, bgcolor=conf_cell_bg, text_color=txt_on, text_size=conf_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_center, tooltip=conf_cell_tip)
    true

// v4.60: on-chart Fib Zone box — a small shaded box near the current bar showing the 50%-61.8%
// retracement levels as text. v4.61: colored by fib_price_in_zone (plain in-zone check) rather than
// the Fib Golden Pocket confluence factor's recency-gated touch state — see fib_price_in_zone's own
// comment. Reuses fib_valid/fib_zone_top/fib_zone_bot/fib_50/fib_618/fib_price_in_zone (all top-level,
// computed once above alongside Liquidity Sweep/Pivot(HTF)) plus htf_tf_disp_val/st_bull/st_neutral/
// txt_on (also top-level, already proven safe to read from a sibling function by
// f_render_confluence_cells()). Per the v4.56/v4.57 lesson, computes its OWN local text-size
// (fib_box_txt_sz) via f_table_text_size() rather than reaching into f_render_status_table()'s
// tbl_txt_sz or f_render_confluence_cells()'s conf_txt_sz — those are local to their own functions
// and not visible here. Uses box.set_*() setters every bar instead of delete+recreate (a box's
// top/bottom/text/colors are all mutable in place, unlike a table's fixed row/column count), so this
// never churns box IDs against max_boxes_count.
f_render_fib_zone_box() =>
    var box fib_zone_bx = na
    // v4.63: two extra reference lines at the 0%/100% swing endpoints (fib_0/fib_100), each with a
    // small "0"/"1" text label at the right edge (line.new has no text param, unlike box.new — hence
    // the separate label objects). Independently toggled (show_fib_01_lines) from the golden-pocket
    // box above (show_fib_zone_box), per follow-up ("can you put in the zero and 1 lines for fib").
    // Same var-persisted, update-in-place pattern (no delete+recreate churn) as the box.
    var line fib_0_ln = na
    var line fib_100_ln = na
    var label fib_0_lbl = na
    var label fib_100_lbl = na
    if not fib_valid
        box.delete(fib_zone_bx)
        fib_zone_bx := na
        line.delete(fib_0_ln)
        fib_0_ln := na
        line.delete(fib_100_ln)
        fib_100_ln := na
        label.delete(fib_0_lbl)
        fib_0_lbl := na
        label.delete(fib_100_lbl)
        fib_100_lbl := na
    else
        int fib_box_left = math.max(0, bar_index - fib_zone_box_bars)
        if not show_fib_zone_box
            box.delete(fib_zone_bx)
            fib_zone_bx := na
        else
            string fib_box_txt_sz = f_table_text_size(status_table_text_size)
            // v4.61: color/text now driven by fib_price_in_zone (plain "is price between the two
            // levels right now" check) instead of cf_f10_fib_touch, matching the Status table row's
            // same change — see fib_price_in_zone's own comment above for why this is deliberately
            // not recency-gated.
            color fib_box_bg = fib_price_in_zone ? color.new(st_bull, 70) : color.new(st_neutral, 85)
            color fib_box_bd = fib_price_in_zone ? st_bull : st_neutral
            string fib_box_txt = "Fib " + htf_tf_disp_val + "  " + str.tostring(fib_50, "#.#") + " / " + str.tostring(fib_618, "#.#") + (fib_price_in_zone ? "  IN" : "")
            if na(fib_zone_bx)
                fib_zone_bx := box.new(left=fib_box_left, top=fib_zone_top, right=bar_index, bottom=fib_zone_bot, border_color=fib_box_bd, border_width=1, bgcolor=fib_box_bg, extend=extend.none, text=fib_box_txt, text_color=txt_on, text_size=fib_box_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_center, text_valign=text.align_center)
            else
                box.set_left(fib_zone_bx, fib_box_left)
                box.set_right(fib_zone_bx, bar_index)
                box.set_top(fib_zone_bx, fib_zone_top)
                box.set_bottom(fib_zone_bx, fib_zone_bot)
                box.set_bgcolor(fib_zone_bx, fib_box_bg)
                box.set_border_color(fib_zone_bx, fib_box_bd)
                box.set_text(fib_zone_bx, fib_box_txt)
                box.set_text_color(fib_zone_bx, txt_on)
                box.set_text_size(fib_zone_bx, fib_box_txt_sz)
        if not show_fib_01_lines
            line.delete(fib_0_ln)
            fib_0_ln := na
            line.delete(fib_100_ln)
            fib_100_ln := na
            label.delete(fib_0_lbl)
            fib_0_lbl := na
            label.delete(fib_100_lbl)
            fib_100_lbl := na
        else
            if na(fib_0_ln)
                fib_0_ln := line.new(fib_box_left, fib_0, bar_index, fib_0, color=col_fib_01_lines, width=1, style=f_ref_line_style(fib_01_line_style), extend=extend.none)
                fib_0_lbl := label.new(bar_index, fib_0, "FIB 0", color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=txt_fib_01_lines, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
            else
                line.set_xy1(fib_0_ln, fib_box_left, fib_0)
                line.set_xy2(fib_0_ln, bar_index, fib_0)
                label.set_xy(fib_0_lbl, bar_index, fib_0)
            if na(fib_100_ln)
                fib_100_ln := line.new(fib_box_left, fib_100, bar_index, fib_100, color=col_fib_01_lines, width=1, style=f_ref_line_style(fib_01_line_style), extend=extend.none)
                fib_100_lbl := label.new(bar_index, fib_100, "FIB 1", color=color.new(color.white, 100), textcolor=txt_fib_01_lines, style=label.style_label_left, size=size.small)
            else
                line.set_xy1(fib_100_ln, fib_box_left, fib_100)
                line.set_xy2(fib_100_ln, bar_index, fib_100)
                label.set_xy(fib_100_lbl, bar_index, fib_100)
    true

if barstate.islast
    f_render_status_table()
    f_render_pivot_table()
    f_render_confluence_cells()
    f_render_fib_zone_box()

// ===========================
// Alerts — deliberately independent of "show_status_table": these should fire whether or not the
// on-chart table itself is visible. Rebuilds its own copy of the same status-snapshot text so the
// alert email/notification body matches what the status table would show at that moment.
// ===========================
// v3.87: message-building block moved OUT of f_fire_alerts() to top-level (still runs every
// bar, same as before) — every input.*() this block touches (alert_show_structure, alert_show_stop,
// alert_show_flip_level, alert_show_pivot_points, show_pivot_pp/r1/r2/r3/s1/s2/s3, alert_session_*,
// show_chart_info, and more) was counting toward f_fire_alerts()'s own 254-external-element
// budget just by being referenced inside it, even though none of this needs function scope —
// it's just building a_status_msg. This was almost certainly the real, larger cause of the
// persistent CE10116 (258/254), not just the smaller single-variable fixes in v3.81/v3.86.
string a_mkt = f_market_session_et()
string a_chart_line = not show_chart_info ? "" : "\nChart: " + syminfo.ticker + "  " + chart_tf_disp_val
bool in_alert_asia = alert_session_asian and not na(time(timeframe.period, alert_asia_window, ny_tz))
bool in_alert_london = alert_session_london and not na(time(timeframe.period, alert_london_window, ny_tz))
bool in_alert_ny = alert_session_ny and not na(time(timeframe.period, alert_ny_window, ny_tz))
bool alert_session_ok = in_alert_asia or in_alert_london or in_alert_ny
bool a_cloud_bull = ema_bullish
bool a_vwap_bull = not na(vwap_value) and close > vwap_value
bool a_ema200_bull = not na(ema200) and close > ema200
float a_or_hi = a_mkt == "Asian" ? ORHigh_AS : a_mkt == "London" ? ORHigh_LN : ORHigh_NY
float a_or_lo = a_mkt == "Asian" ? ORLow_AS : a_mkt == "London" ? ORLow_LN : ORLow_NY
bool a_or_on = (a_mkt == "Asian" and or_asian) or (a_mkt == "London" and or_london) or (a_mkt == "New York" and or_ny)
// Explicitly states, in the CBC Flip message itself, whether THIS flip happened inside or outside
// the current session's Opening Range — the existing "OR IN"/"OR OUT" badge shows the current state,
// this ties it directly to the flip event in words. Empty string if OR tracking is off/not yet set.
string a_or_flip_note = (not a_or_on or na(a_or_hi) or na(a_or_lo)) ? "" : (close >= a_or_lo and close <= a_or_hi) ? " — flip occurred INSIDE the Opening Range (" + str.tostring(a_or_lo, "#.##") + "–" + str.tostring(a_or_hi, "#.##") + ")" : (close > a_or_hi) ? " — flip occurred OUTSIDE the Opening Range (above " + str.tostring(a_or_hi, "#.##") + ")" : " — flip occurred OUTSIDE the Opening Range (below " + str.tostring(a_or_lo, "#.##") + ")"
// HTF CBC Flip vs. HTF's own EMA9/20 — 3 zones relative to the flip's direction: fully agreeing
// (price already cleared both EMAs the same way as the flip), partially agreeing (price is between
// the two EMAs), or fully disagreeing (price hasn't cleared either EMA yet) — the cautionary case.
float a_htf_ema_hi = math.max(htf_ema9, htf_ema20)
float a_htf_ema_lo = math.min(htf_ema9, htf_ema20)
bool a_htf_flip_between = close >= a_htf_ema_lo and close <= a_htf_ema_hi
string a_htf_flip_long_suffix = close < a_htf_ema_lo ? " - ⚠ CAUTIONARY" : a_htf_flip_between ? " - partial confirmation" : ""
string a_htf_flip_long_note = close < a_htf_ema_lo ? "\n⚠ CAUTIONARY SETUP — TREAD LIGHTLY: price still fully below HTF EMA9/20\nCaution - Possible short dont long" : a_htf_flip_between ? "\n— price inside HTF EMA9/20 zone, partial confirmation" : ""
string a_htf_flip_short_suffix = close > a_htf_ema_hi ? " - ⚠ CAUTIONARY" : a_htf_flip_between ? " - partial confirmation" : ""
string a_htf_flip_short_note = close > a_htf_ema_hi ? "\n⚠ CAUTIONARY SETUP — TREAD LIGHTLY: price still fully above HTF EMA9/20\nCaution - Possible long dont short" : a_htf_flip_between ? "\n— price inside HTF EMA9/20 zone, partial confirmation" : ""
int a_or_open = a_mkt == "Asian" ? as_or_open : a_mkt == "London" ? ln_or_open : ny_or_open

// ORB (Opening Range) high/low and the window it was set over, for the alert email.
int a_or_end = a_or_open + or_ms
int a_orb_open_hh = hour(a_or_open, ny_tz)
int a_orb_open_mi = minute(a_or_open, ny_tz)
string a_orb_open_hh_s = (a_orb_open_hh < 10 ? "0" : "") + str.tostring(a_orb_open_hh, "#")
string a_orb_open_mi_s = (a_orb_open_mi < 10 ? "0" : "") + str.tostring(a_orb_open_mi, "#")
int a_orb_end_hh = hour(a_or_end, ny_tz)
int a_orb_end_mi = minute(a_or_end, ny_tz)
string a_orb_end_hh_s = (a_orb_end_hh < 10 ? "0" : "") + str.tostring(a_orb_end_hh, "#")
string a_orb_end_mi_s = (a_orb_end_mi < 10 ? "0" : "") + str.tostring(a_orb_end_mi, "#")
string a_orb_time_s = a_orb_open_hh_s + ":" + a_orb_open_mi_s + "-" + a_orb_end_hh_s + ":" + a_orb_end_mi_s + " ET"
string a_orb_high_rel = close > a_or_hi ? "above" : close < a_or_hi ? "below" : "at"
string a_orb_low_rel = close > a_or_lo ? "above" : close < a_or_lo ? "below" : "at"
float a_orb_high_diff = not na(a_or_hi) ? close - a_or_hi : na
float a_orb_low_diff = not na(a_or_lo) ? close - a_or_lo : na
string a_orb_high_line = na(a_or_hi) ? "ORB High —" : "Price " + str.tostring(close, "#.##") + " is " + a_orb_high_rel + " ORB High " + str.tostring(a_or_hi, "#.##") + " (" + (a_orb_high_diff >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(a_orb_high_diff, "#.##") + ")"
string a_orb_low_line = na(a_or_lo) ? "ORB Low —" : "Price " + str.tostring(close, "#.##") + " is " + a_orb_low_rel + " ORB Low " + str.tostring(a_or_lo, "#.##") + " (" + (a_orb_low_diff >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(a_orb_low_diff, "#.##") + ")"
string a_orb_info = (not show_any_or or not a_or_on) ? "" : "\n" + a_orb_high_line + "\n" + a_orb_low_line + "\nORB set: " + a_orb_time_s

string a_or_msg = "—"
if not show_any_or
    a_or_msg := "Off"
else if not a_or_on
    a_or_msg := "—"
else if time < a_or_open
    a_or_msg := "Before open"
else if na(a_or_hi) or na(a_or_lo)
    a_or_msg := "Pending"
else if close > a_or_hi
    a_or_msg := "Above range"
else if close < a_or_lo
    a_or_msg := "Below range"
else
    a_or_msg := "Inside range"

string a_cloud_msg = a_cloud_bull ? "Bullish" : "Bearish"
string a_htf_cloud_msg = htf_ema_bullish ? "Bullish" : "Bearish"
float a_vwap_diff = not na(vwap_value) ? close - vwap_value : na
float a_ema200_diff = not na(ema200) ? close - ema200 : na
string a_v_msg = not na(vwap_value) ? (a_vwap_bull ? "Above" : "Below") + " (" + str.tostring(vwap_value, "#.##") + ", " + (a_vwap_diff >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(a_vwap_diff, "#.##") + ")" : "—"
string a_e200_msg = not na(ema200) ? (a_ema200_bull ? "Above" : "Below") + " (" + str.tostring(ema200, "#.##") + ", " + (a_ema200_diff >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(a_ema200_diff, "#.##") + ")" : "—"
string a_htf_msg = not htf_enabled ? "Off" : na(htf_cbc_labels_f) ? "—" : (htf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0 ? "Bullish" : "Bearish")
string a_htf_live_msg = not htf_enabled ? "Off" : (htf_cbc ? "Bullish" : "Bearish")
string a_mtf_msg = not mtf_enabled ? "Off" : na(mtf_cbc_labels_f) ? "—" : (mtf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0 ? "Bullish" : "Bearish")
string a_mtf_live_msg = not mtf_enabled ? "Off" : (mtf_cbc ? "Bullish" : "Bearish")
string a_mtf_cloud_msg = mtf_ema_bullish ? "Bullish" : "Bearish"
string a_ltf_msg = cbc ? "Bullish" : "Bearish"

// Words used to describe the HTF confirmed->live transition in the early/unconfirmed Agreement alert
// (e.g. "was short and now long"). Falls back to the live word if HTF has never confirmed yet (na msg).
string a_htf_dir_word_confirmed = a_htf_msg == "Bullish" ? "long" : a_htf_msg == "Bearish" ? "short" : a_htf_live_msg == "Bullish" ? "long" : "short"
string a_htf_dir_word_live = a_htf_live_msg == "Bullish" ? "long" : "short"
string a_htf_transition = "the htf (" + htf_tf_disp_val + ") candle is still open — " + (a_htf_dir_word_confirmed != a_htf_dir_word_live ? "last htf candle : was " + a_htf_dir_word_confirmed + " and now " + a_htf_dir_word_live : "still reads " + a_htf_dir_word_live + ", unchanged from the last confirmed candle — it's the ltf that just joined it going from " + (cbc ? "short" : "long") + " to " + (cbc ? "long" : "short"))
// LTF's own last confirmed flip transition — appended to the Agreement titles so the LTF side's state
// is always spelled out too, not just the HTF side.
string a_ltf_state_note = " — ltf was " + (ltf_last_flip_bull ? "short" : "long") + ", now " + (ltf_last_flip_bull ? "long" : "short")
string a_ltf_dir_word = cbc ? "long" : "short"

int a_hh = hour(time, ny_tz)
int a_mi = minute(time, ny_tz)
string a_hh_s = (a_hh < 10 ? "0" : "") + str.tostring(a_hh, "#")
string a_mm_s = (a_mi < 10 ? "0" : "") + str.tostring(a_mi, "#")
int a_hh_close = hour(time_close, ny_tz)
int a_mi_close = minute(time_close, ny_tz)
string a_hh_close_s = (a_hh_close < 10 ? "0" : "") + str.tostring(a_hh_close, "#")
string a_mm_close_s = (a_mi_close < 10 ? "0" : "") + str.tostring(a_mi_close, "#")
string a_time_s = str.tostring(month(time, ny_tz), "#") + "/" + str.tostring(dayofmonth(time, ny_tz), "#") + " " + a_hh_s + ":" + a_mm_s + "-" + a_hh_close_s + ":" + a_mm_close_s + " ET"
string a_ltf_tf_num = str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier)
string a_ema_line_ltf = a_ltf_tf_num + "min - " + f_price_vs_emas(close, ema9, ema20) + ": " + f_order3(ema9, "ema9(" + a_ltf_tf_num + ")", ema20, "ema20(" + a_ltf_tf_num + ")", close, "price:", "price:")
string a_ema_line_htf = htf_timeframe + "min - " + f_price_vs_emas(close, htf_ema9, htf_ema20) + ": " + f_order3(htf_ema9, "ema9(" + htf_timeframe + ")", htf_ema20, "ema20(" + htf_timeframe + ")", close, "price:", "price:")
string a_prev_candle_line = "\nPrev Candle (LTF, " + chart_tf_disp_val + "): H=" + str.tostring(high[1], "#.##") + " L=" + str.tostring(low[1], "#.##") + " C=" + str.tostring(close[1], "#.##")
string a_htf_candle_line = "\nHTF Candle (" + htf_tf_disp_val + ", the one that flipped): H=" + (na(sig_htf_hi) ? "—" : str.tostring(sig_htf_hi, "#.##")) + " L=" + (na(sig_htf_lo) ? "—" : str.tostring(sig_htf_lo, "#.##")) + " C=" + (na(sig_htf_cl) ? "—" : str.tostring(sig_htf_cl, "#.##")) + " Height=" + (na(sig_htf_candle_h) ? "—" : sig_htf_candle_h_s) + " Retracement%=" + sig_htf_close_pct_s
string a_ltf_flip_dir_s = na(ltf_last_flip_t) ? "" : (ltf_last_flip_bull ? "LONG " : "SHORT ")
string a_htf_flip_dir_s = na(htf_last_flip_t) ? "" : (htf_last_flip_bull ? "LONG " : "SHORT ")
string a_mtf_flip_dir_s = na(mtf_last_flip_t) ? "" : (mtf_last_flip_bull ? "LONG " : "SHORT ")
string a_mtf_structure_part = not show_mtf_in_tables ? "" : "\nStructure (" + mtf_timeframe + "): " + f_struct_abbrev(mtf_hi_struct) + " / " + f_struct_abbrev(mtf_lo_struct)
string a_mtf_last_flip_part = not show_mtf_in_tables ? "" : "\nCBC (" + mtf_timeframe + ") last flip: " + a_mtf_flip_dir_s + f_fmt_ny_time(mtf_last_flip_t) + (na(mtf_last_flip_t) ? "" : "  #" + str.tostring(mtf_bars_since_flip))
string a_structure_line = not alert_show_structure ? "" : "\nStructure (" + chart_tf_disp_val + "): " + f_struct_abbrev(ltf_hi_struct) + " / " + f_struct_abbrev(ltf_lo_struct) + "\nStructure (" + htf_tf_disp_val + "): " + f_struct_abbrev(htf_hi_struct) + " / " + f_struct_abbrev(htf_lo_struct) + a_mtf_structure_part + "\nCBC (" + chart_tf_disp_val + ") last flip: " + a_ltf_flip_dir_s + f_fmt_ny_time(ltf_last_flip_t) + (na(ltf_last_flip_t) ? "" : "  #" + str.tostring(ltf_bars_since_flip)) + "\nCBC (" + htf_tf_disp_val + ") last flip: " + a_htf_flip_dir_s + f_fmt_ny_time(htf_last_flip_t) + (na(htf_last_flip_t) ? "" : "  #" + str.tostring(htf_bars_since_flip)) + a_mtf_last_flip_part
float a_stop_diff = na(htf_trail_stop) ? na : close - htf_trail_stop
string a_stop_line = (not alert_show_stop or na(htf_trail_stop)) ? "" : "\nLTF Trailing Stop: " + str.tostring(htf_trail_stop, "#.##") + " " + htf_stop_method + " (" + (a_stop_diff >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(a_stop_diff, "#.##") + ")"

// CBC Flip Level: the exact price that would trigger the NEXT CBC state change on each
// timeframe — same value as the on-chart flip-level dash (CBC flip levels group), just spelled
// out in text. Direction mirrors that dash's H/L coloring: currently bullish -> next flip needs
// a close BELOW the prior bar's low; currently bearish -> needs a close ABOVE the prior high.
string a_ltf_flip_lvl_dir = cbc[1] ? "close below" : "close above"
string a_htf_flip_lvl_dir = htf_cbc[1] ? "close below" : "close above"
string a_mtf_flip_lvl_dir = mtf_cbc[1] ? "close below" : "close above"
string a_mtf_flip_level_part = not show_mtf_in_tables ? "" : "\nCBC Flip Level (" + mtf_timeframe + "): " + (na(mtf_flip_price) ? "—" : str.tostring(mtf_flip_price, "#.##") + " (" + a_mtf_flip_lvl_dir + ")")
string a_flip_level_line = not alert_show_flip_level ? "" : "\nCBC Flip Level (" + chart_tf_disp_val + "): " + (na(ltf_flip_price) ? "—" : str.tostring(ltf_flip_price, "#.##") + " (" + a_ltf_flip_lvl_dir + ")") + "\nCBC Flip Level (" + htf_tf_disp_val + "): " + (na(htf_flip_price) ? "—" : str.tostring(htf_flip_price, "#.##") + " (" + a_htf_flip_lvl_dir + ")") + a_mtf_flip_level_part

// Pivot Points (Daily, Standard) — same values as the on-chart table and the Key Level Alert,
// one independent check per level (matches the table/lines/alerts exactly). Also fixes
// a pre-existing indexing bug here: this used to read piv_price_arr with the OLD pre-v3.59
// index order (0=PP,1=R1,2=S1...) after the array's canonical order had already been changed
// to 0=R3,1=R2,2=R1,3=PP,4=S1,5=S2,6=S3 — meaning "Pivot PP:" in emails was actually showing
// R3's price, "Pivot R1:" was showing R2's, etc. Every label below now matches its true index.
string a_pivot_r3_part = not (show_pivot_points and show_pivot_r3) ? "" : "\nPivot R3: " + str.tostring(array.get(piv_price_arr, 0), "#.##")
string a_pivot_r2_part = not (show_pivot_points and show_pivot_r2) ? "" : "\nPivot R2: " + str.tostring(array.get(piv_price_arr, 1), "#.##")
string a_pivot_r1_part = not (show_pivot_points and show_pivot_r1) ? "" : "\nPivot R1: " + str.tostring(array.get(piv_price_arr, 2), "#.##")
string a_pivot_pp_part = not (show_pivot_points and show_pivot_pp) ? "" : "\nPivot PP: " + str.tostring(array.get(piv_price_arr, 3), "#.##")
string a_pivot_s1_part = not (show_pivot_points and show_pivot_s1) ? "" : "\nPivot S1: " + str.tostring(array.get(piv_price_arr, 4), "#.##")
string a_pivot_s2_part = not (show_pivot_points and show_pivot_s2) ? "" : "\nPivot S2: " + str.tostring(array.get(piv_price_arr, 5), "#.##")
string a_pivot_s3_part = not (show_pivot_points and show_pivot_s3) ? "" : "\nPivot S3: " + str.tostring(array.get(piv_price_arr, 6), "#.##")
string a_pivot_points_line = not alert_show_pivot_points ? "" : a_pivot_r3_part + a_pivot_r2_part + a_pivot_r1_part + a_pivot_pp_part + a_pivot_s1_part + a_pivot_s2_part + a_pivot_s3_part
// v4.54: Fib Zone alert line — always included (no separate email toggle, matching how VWAP/EMA200
// lines below are unconditional), reusing fib_valid/fib_50/fib_618/cf_f10_fib_touch computed above.
// v4.61: same "50%"/"61.8%"-prefix drop, 1-decimal, and fib_price_in_zone (not recency-gated) change
// as the Status table row and on-chart box — this is the copy of that same text the app parses out
// of the alert body into its own "Fib Zone (Nm)" field/row.
string a_fib_line = not fib_valid ? "" : "\nFib Zone (" + htf_tf_disp_val + "): " + str.tostring(fib_50, "#.#") + " / " + str.tostring(fib_618, "#.#") + (fib_price_in_zone ? " [IN ZONE]" : "")

string a_mtf_cbc_part = not show_mtf_in_tables ? "" : "\nCBC (" + mtf_timeframe + "): " + a_mtf_msg + "\nCBC (" + mtf_timeframe + ", live/unconfirmed): " + a_mtf_live_msg
string a_mtf_cloud_part = not show_mtf_in_tables ? "" : "\nEMA cloud (" + mtf_timeframe + "): " + a_mtf_cloud_msg
// v4.47: appended a Confluence Score line here (B2) — a_status_msg feeds every alert type that
// includes it (9+ call sites), so this one addition surfaces the confluence breakdown everywhere,
// not just the dedicated "High Confluence Setup" alert (which still has its own richer message).
string a_status_msg = "Time: " + a_time_s + "\nMarket: " + a_mkt + a_chart_line + "\nPrice: " + str.tostring(close, "#.##") + "\nCBC (" + chart_tf_disp_val + "): " + a_ltf_msg + "\nCBC (" + htf_tf_disp_val + "): " + a_htf_msg + "\nCBC (" + htf_tf_disp_val + ", live/unconfirmed): " + a_htf_live_msg + a_mtf_cbc_part + "\nOpening range: " + a_or_msg + a_orb_info + a_structure_line + a_stop_line + a_flip_level_line + a_pivot_points_line + "\nEMA cloud (" + chart_tf_disp_val + "): " + a_cloud_msg + "\nEMA cloud (" + htf_tf_disp_val + "): " + a_htf_cloud_msg + a_mtf_cloud_part + "\nVWAP: " + a_v_msg + "\nEMA 200: " + a_e200_msg + "\n" + a_ema_line_ltf + "\n" + a_ema_line_htf + "\nEMA9/20 (" + a_ltf_tf_num + ") zone entries since flip: " + str.tostring(ltf_between_count) + "\nEMA9/20 (" + htf_timeframe + ") zone entries since flip: " + str.tostring(htf_between_count) + a_prev_candle_line + a_htf_candle_line + "\nConfluence: " + str.tostring(confluence_score) + "/" + str.tostring(confluence_max_score) + "  " + confluence_reasons_str + a_fib_line

// A+ Setup - Major Pivot Proximity: builds a plain string listing every currently-enabled level
// price is within aplus_pivot_dist of, so f_fire_alerts() below only ever reads a precomputed
// string (not raw inputs directly) — same CE10116-avoidance pattern used throughout this script.
// f_apn compacts each check to one line instead of an if+assignment pair.
f_aplus_pivot_dist() =>
    string root = syminfo.root
    float auto_pts = root == "MNQ" ? 50.0 : 5.0
    aplus_pivot_dist_override > 0 ? aplus_pivot_dist_override : auto_pts
aplus_pivot_dist = f_aplus_pivot_dist()
f_apn(note, val, label) =>
    not na(val) and math.abs(close - val) <= aplus_pivot_dist ? note + (note != "" ? ", " : "") + label : note
string aplus_pivot_note = ""
if aplus_pivot_note_on
    if aplus_check_pivots
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, piv_r1, "R1")
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, piv_r2, "R2")
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, piv_r3, "R3")
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, piv_s1, "S1")
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, piv_s2, "S2")
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, piv_s3, "S3")
    if aplus_check_ema200
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, ema200, "EMA200")
    if aplus_check_htf_ema200
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, htf_ema200, "HTF EMA200")
    if aplus_check_vwap
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, vwap_value, "VWAP")
    if aplus_check_ydhl
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, YDH, "YDH")
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, YDL, "YDL")
    if aplus_check_orb
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, a_or_hi, "ORB High")
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, a_or_lo, "ORB Low")
    if aplus_check_swing_ltf
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, prox_last_ph, "Swing High")
        aplus_pivot_note := f_apn(aplus_pivot_note, prox_last_pl, "Swing Low")
string aplus_pivot_suffix = aplus_pivot_note == "" ? "" : " — but close to major pivot: " + aplus_pivot_note

// v4.34 token-trim: shared helper for the repeated "append a newline-joined bullet line" pattern
// used ~38 times below for confirmed_titles/early_titles — same append-if-nonempty logic as f_apn
// above, just generalized to any accumulator string instead of a fixed one.
f_add_line(string base, string ln_txt) =>
    base + (base != "" ? "\n" : "") + ln_txt

f_fire_alerts() =>

    // A+ Setup: confirmed HTF CBC direction + LTF EMA cloud + HTF EMA cloud all aligned. VWAP: CONFIRMED —
    // price already on the trade's side of VWAP (above for Long, below for Short). Opening Range: still
    // ANTICIPATORY — price hasn't broken the OR/pivot level yet (i.e. before that breakout, not after).
    bool a_plus_long_align = not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and htf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0 and ema_bullish and htf_ema_bullish
    bool a_plus_short_align = not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and htf_cbc_labels_f == 0.0 and (not ema_bullish) and (not htf_ema_bullish)
    bool a_plus_prior_long = close > vwap_value and (na(a_or_hi) or close <= a_or_hi)
    bool a_plus_prior_short = close < vwap_value and (na(a_or_lo) or close >= a_or_lo)
    bool a_plus_long_now = a_plus_long_align and a_plus_prior_long
    bool a_plus_short_now = a_plus_short_align and a_plus_prior_short
    bool a_plus_enter_long = a_plus_long_now and not (a_plus_long_now[1] or a_plus_short_now[1])
    bool a_plus_enter_short = a_plus_short_now and not (a_plus_long_now[1] or a_plus_short_now[1])
    string a_htf_msg_tagged = a_htf_msg + " [A+ SETUP]"
    string a_mtf_cbc_part_aplus = not show_mtf_in_tables ? "" : "\nCBC (" + mtf_timeframe + "): " + a_mtf_msg
    string a_status_msg_aplus_base = "Time: " + a_time_s + "\nMarket: " + a_mkt + a_chart_line + "\nPrice: " + str.tostring(close, "#.##") + "\nCBC (" + chart_tf_disp_val + "): " + a_ltf_msg + "\nCBC (" + htf_tf_disp_val + "): " + a_htf_msg_tagged + a_mtf_cbc_part_aplus + "\nOpening range: " + a_or_msg + a_orb_info + a_structure_line + a_stop_line + a_flip_level_line + a_pivot_points_line + "\nEMA cloud (" + chart_tf_disp_val + "): " + a_cloud_msg + "\nEMA cloud (" + htf_tf_disp_val + "): " + a_htf_cloud_msg + a_mtf_cloud_part + "\nVWAP: " + a_v_msg + "\nEMA 200: " + a_e200_msg + "\n" + a_ema_line_ltf + "\n" + a_ema_line_htf + "\nEMA9/20 (" + a_ltf_tf_num + ") zone entries since flip: " + str.tostring(ltf_between_count) + "\nEMA9/20 (" + htf_timeframe + ") zone entries since flip: " + str.tostring(htf_between_count) + a_prev_candle_line + a_htf_candle_line

    // VWAP Break, EMA200 Break, Outside HTF ORB — each fires once on the transition, same pattern as agreement/A+.
    bool a_vwap_break_bull_now = not na(vwap_value) and close > vwap_value
    bool a_vwap_break_bear_now = not na(vwap_value) and close < vwap_value
    bool a_vwap_break_enter_bull = a_vwap_break_bull_now and not (a_vwap_break_bull_now[1] or a_vwap_break_bear_now[1])
    bool a_vwap_break_enter_bear = a_vwap_break_bear_now and not (a_vwap_break_bull_now[1] or a_vwap_break_bear_now[1])

    bool a_e200_break_bull_now = not na(ema200) and close > ema200
    bool a_e200_break_bear_now = not na(ema200) and close < ema200
    bool a_e200_break_enter_bull = a_e200_break_bull_now and not (a_e200_break_bull_now[1] or a_e200_break_bear_now[1])
    bool a_e200_break_enter_bear = a_e200_break_bear_now and not (a_e200_break_bull_now[1] or a_e200_break_bear_now[1])

    // v4.27: HTF version of the same EMA 200 Break logic above — only new external this function
    // reads is htf_ema200 itself (already a top-level var from the request.security tuple at
    // ~line 710); everything else (close, na()) was already read here.
    bool a_htf_e200_break_bull_now = not na(htf_ema200) and close > htf_ema200
    bool a_htf_e200_break_bear_now = not na(htf_ema200) and close < htf_ema200
    bool a_htf_e200_break_enter_bull = a_htf_e200_break_bull_now and not (a_htf_e200_break_bull_now[1] or a_htf_e200_break_bear_now[1])
    bool a_htf_e200_break_enter_bear = a_htf_e200_break_bear_now and not (a_htf_e200_break_bull_now[1] or a_htf_e200_break_bear_now[1])

    bool a_orb_break_up_now = not na(a_or_hi) and close > a_or_hi
    bool a_orb_break_down_now = not na(a_or_lo) and close < a_or_lo
    bool a_orb_break_enter_up = a_orb_break_up_now and not (a_orb_break_up_now[1] or a_orb_break_down_now[1])
    bool a_orb_break_enter_down = a_orb_break_down_now and not (a_orb_break_up_now[1] or a_orb_break_down_now[1])

    // ===========================
    // Merge everything that's true on THIS bar into at most two alert() calls (one confirmed batch, one
    // unconfirmed/early batch) instead of firing a separate email per condition. This is what fixes getting
    // multiple emails at once when e.g. HTF CBC Flip and LTF + HTF Agreement both trigger on the same bar.
    // ===========================
    string confirmed_titles = ""
    bool any_confirmed = false
    // Tracks whether anything OTHER than Key Level Alert (PP/R1-3/S1-3) also fired this bar — that
    // alert type doesn't need the Entry BRSG Zone line on its own, but if it bundles with something
    // that does (A+ Setup, Agreement, etc.), the line should still show for that other bullet.
    bool needs_brsg_line = false
    bool use_aplus_body = false
    string aplus_entry_line = ""
    string orb_retest_entry_line = ""
    string ltf20_reclaim_line = ""
    float a_brsg_top = brsg_zone_top
    float a_brsg_bot = brsg_zone_bot
    string a_brsg_dir = na(a_brsg_top) ? "—" : (htf_last_flip_bull ? "LONG" : "SHORT")
    string a_brsg_line = "\nEntry Zone: " + a_brsg_dir + "\nEntry Zone High: " + (na(a_brsg_top) ? "—" : str.tostring(a_brsg_top, "#.##")) + "\nEntry Zone Low: " + (na(a_brsg_bot) ? "—" : str.tostring(a_brsg_bot, "#.##"))

    // v4.28 token-trim: this entire block (through the HTF Pullback Entry alerts, right
    // before the alert_on_startup exception) had "and alert_session_ok" appended to EVERY
    // single condition (34 occurrences) - collapsed to one wrapping "if alert_session_ok"
    // read instead, same proven pattern as htf_early_time_ok (v4.20). Safe because "if
    // any_confirmed" below only ever becomes true from conditions that already required
    // alert_session_ok, so wrapping it too changes nothing behaviorally - it just makes the
    // existing implication explicit instead of redundant.
    if alert_session_ok
        if (alert_on_ltf_flip or alert_on_ltf_flip_early) and bull_flip
            confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + "m) CBC Flip: LONG (confirmed, bar closed — was short, now long)" + a_or_flip_note)
            any_confirmed := true
            needs_brsg_line := true
        if (alert_on_ltf_flip or alert_on_ltf_flip_early) and bear_flip
            confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + "m) CBC Flip: SHORT (confirmed, bar closed — was long, now short)" + a_or_flip_note)
            any_confirmed := true
            needs_brsg_line := true
        // When HTF CBC Flip and LTF+HTF Agreement both confirm on the SAME bar (very common — the
        // Agreement condition often becomes true specifically BECAUSE the HTF just flipped to match
        // an LTF that was already there), combine into one bullet instead of two separate ones. Only
        // combines if BOTH alert types are actually enabled — if either is toggled off, falls through
        // to whichever single bullet still applies, unchanged from before.
        bool combo_confirmed_long = (alert_on_htf_flip or alert_on_htf_flip_early) and htf_flip_bull and (alert_on_agreement or alert_on_agreement_early) and agreement_enter_long
        bool combo_confirmed_short = (alert_on_htf_flip or alert_on_htf_flip_early) and htf_flip_bear and (alert_on_agreement or alert_on_agreement_early) and agreement_enter_short
        if combo_confirmed_long
            confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") CBC Flip + LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + ") Agreement: LONG (confirmed, HTF closed)")
            any_confirmed := true
            needs_brsg_line := true
        if combo_confirmed_short
            confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") CBC Flip + LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + ") Agreement: SHORT (confirmed, HTF closed)")
            any_confirmed := true
            needs_brsg_line := true
        if (alert_on_htf_flip or alert_on_htf_flip_early) and htf_flip_bull and not combo_confirmed_long
            confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") CBC Flip: LONG" + a_htf_flip_long_suffix + " (confirmed, HTF closed)" + a_or_flip_note + a_htf_flip_long_note)
            any_confirmed := true
            needs_brsg_line := true
        if (alert_on_htf_flip or alert_on_htf_flip_early) and htf_flip_bear and not combo_confirmed_short
            confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") CBC Flip: SHORT" + a_htf_flip_short_suffix + " (confirmed, HTF closed)" + a_or_flip_note + a_htf_flip_short_note)
            any_confirmed := true
            needs_brsg_line := true
        if (alert_on_agreement or alert_on_agreement_early) and agreement_enter_long and not combo_confirmed_long and agreement_ltf_join_enabled
            confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + ") " + a_ltf_dir_word + " + HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") Agreement: LONG (confirmed, HTF closed" + a_ltf_state_note + ")")
            any_confirmed := true
            needs_brsg_line := true
        if (alert_on_agreement or alert_on_agreement_early) and agreement_enter_short and not combo_confirmed_short and agreement_ltf_join_enabled
            confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + ") " + a_ltf_dir_word + " + HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") Agreement: SHORT (confirmed, HTF closed" + a_ltf_state_note + ")")
            any_confirmed := true
            needs_brsg_line := true
        if alert_on_a_plus
            if a_plus_enter_long
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• A+ Setup: LONG")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
                use_aplus_body := true
                aplus_entry_line := "\nEntry: below " + str.tostring(close, "#.##") + " (flip candle close) — look for LTF EMA20: " + str.tostring(ema20, "#.##") + aplus_pivot_suffix
            if a_plus_enter_short
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• A+ Setup: SHORT")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
                use_aplus_body := true
                aplus_entry_line := "\nEntry: above " + str.tostring(close, "#.##") + " (flip candle close) — look for LTF EMA20: " + str.tostring(ema20, "#.##") + aplus_pivot_suffix
        if alert_on_vwap_break
            if a_vwap_break_enter_bull
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• VWAP Break: Bullish (crossed above VWAP)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
            if a_vwap_break_enter_bear
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• VWAP Break: Bearish (crossed below VWAP)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
        if alert_on_ema200_break
            if a_e200_break_enter_bull
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• EMA 200 Break: Bullish (crossed above EMA200)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
            if a_e200_break_enter_bear
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• EMA 200 Break: Bearish (crossed below EMA200)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
        if alert_on_htf_ema200_break
            if a_htf_e200_break_enter_bull
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") EMA 200 Break: Bullish (crossed above HTF EMA200)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
            if a_htf_e200_break_enter_bear
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") EMA 200 Break: Bearish (crossed below HTF EMA200)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
        if alert_on_ema920_cross
            if ema920_cross_bull
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• EMA9/20 Cross: Bullish (crossed above)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
            if ema920_cross_bear
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• EMA9/20 Cross: Bearish (crossed below)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true

        // Key Level Alert: loops over all 7 pivot levels using the packed arrays (piv_enabled already
        // encodes each level's gating — the show_pivot_points master switch AND that level's own
        // independent checkbox, e.g. show_pivot_r2) instead of 14 near-identical if-blocks, to help
        // stay under Pine's compiled token ceiling (CE10117) now that the script has grown this large.
        for pi = 0 to 6
            if alert_on_pivot_cross and array.get(piv_enabled, pi)
                string pi_lbl = array.get(piv_labels, pi)
                string pi_price = str.tostring(array.get(piv_price_arr, pi), "#.##")
                if array.get(piv_cross_up_arr, pi)
                    confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• Key Level Alert: " + pi_lbl + " Bullish (crossed above " + pi_price + ")")
                    any_confirmed := true
                if array.get(piv_cross_dn_arr, pi)
                    confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• Key Level Alert: " + pi_lbl + " Bearish (crossed below " + pi_price + ")")
                    any_confirmed := true
        if alert_on_orb_break
            if a_orb_break_enter_up
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• Outside HTF ORB: Above (closed above ORB high)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
            if a_orb_break_enter_down
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• Outside HTF ORB: Below (closed below ORB low)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
        if alert_on_orb_retest
            if orb_r_enter_long
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• ORB Pass +: retested ORB High as new support, closed back above (breakout continues)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
                orb_retest_entry_line := "\nEntry: above " + str.tostring(close, "#.##") + " (base confirmation close) — ORB High acting as support: " + str.tostring(orb_r_hi, "#.##")
            if orb_r_enter_short
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• ORB Pass -: retested ORB Low as new resistance, closed back below (breakout continues)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
                orb_retest_entry_line := "\nEntry: below " + str.tostring(close, "#.##") + " (base confirmation close) — ORB Low acting as resistance: " + str.tostring(orb_r_lo, "#.##")
            // Same toggle as the Pass alerts above — Fail from and Flip are just the other two outcomes of
            // the same setup, not separate features. All three fire once, at the bar the outcome itself
            // happens (orb_r_fail_up/dn and orb_r_timeout_up/dn are already single-fire, same as the
            // on-chart markers).
            if orb_r_timeout_up
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• ORB Fail from +: broke above ORB High but never confirmed — stalled back inside the range before the retest window expired")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
            if orb_r_timeout_dn
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• ORB Fail from -: broke below ORB Low but never confirmed — stalled back inside the range before the retest window expired")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
            if orb_r_fail_up
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• ORB Flip +: broke above ORB High but fully round-tripped back below ORB Low before confirming — failed breakout")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
            if orb_r_fail_dn
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• ORB Flip -: broke below ORB Low but fully round-tripped back above ORB High before confirming — failed breakout")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
        if alert_on_ltf20_reclaim
            if ltf20_reclaim_long
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• Price Enters HTF EMA9/20 Zone: LONG (HTF confirmed bullish — look for entry)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
                ltf20_reclaim_line := "\nSuggested stop: close below HTF EMA20 " + str.tostring(htf_ema20, "#.##")
            if ltf20_reclaim_short
                confirmed_titles := f_add_line(confirmed_titles, "• Price Enters HTF EMA9/20 Zone: SHORT (HTF confirmed bearish — look for entry)")
                any_confirmed := true
                needs_brsg_line := true
                ltf20_reclaim_line := "\nSuggested stop: close above HTF EMA20 " + str.tostring(htf_ema20, "#.##")

        if any_confirmed
            string confirmed_body = use_aplus_body ? a_status_msg_aplus_base : a_status_msg
            alert(syminfo.ticker + " —\n" + confirmed_titles + "\n\n" + confirmed_body + aplus_entry_line + orb_retest_entry_line + ltf20_reclaim_line + (needs_brsg_line ? a_brsg_line : ""), alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)

        // Level Proximity Alerts: its own INDEPENDENT alert() call (not bundled into confirmed_titles
        // above), using freq_once_per_bar rather than once_per_bar_close — these are proximity events
        // that can enter and exit within a single bar, so waiting for bar close (like the rest of this
        // script's alerts) could miss the visit entirely, same reasoning as CBC Wire's own LEVEL channel.
        if alert_on_level_proximity and level_alert_new_visit
            alert(syminfo.ticker + " —\n• " + level_bullet + "\n\n" + a_status_msg + a_brsg_line, alert.freq_once_per_bar)

        // v3.75: High Confluence Setup — fires once when the 0-5 confluence score newly reaches the threshold.
        if fire_confluence_high
            // v4.47: dropped the score/reasons breakdown that used to be built inline here — it's
            // now redundant with the "Confluence: X/Y ..." line a_status_msg appends to every alert
            // (see B2 above), so this message keeps just the title/direction and lets a_status_msg
            // carry the breakdown once instead of printing it twice.
            alert(syminfo.ticker + " —\n• High Confluence Setup: " + (cbc ? "LONG" : "SHORT") + " LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + ") / HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ")\n\n" + a_status_msg + a_brsg_line, alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)

        // v3.75: VWAP Bounce - High R:R — CBC flip at VWAP, clear of the nearest major support/resistance level.
        if fire_vwap_bounce
            alert(syminfo.ticker + " —\n• VWAP Bounce - High R:R: " + (cbc ? "LONG" : "SHORT") + " (" + str.tostring(vwap_bounce_dist, "#.##") + " pts clear of nearest major support/resistance)\n\n" + a_status_msg + a_brsg_line, alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)

        // EMA20 Touch - Pullback Entry: regressive-candle touch of EMA20 while CBC confirms the trend
        // continuing (not the regression) — LTF and HTF checked and labeled separately. Fires on bar
        // close or intra-bar per the alert_ema20_touch_intrabar toggle (ema20_touch_freq, set above).
        // In bar-close mode barstate.isconfirmed is always true by the time freq_once_per_bar_close
        // actually lets the alert through, so these tags stay blank and the message is unchanged from
        // before v4.39. In intra-bar mode a still-forming touch is tagged UNCONFIRMED (dashboard-side
        // classifyTier() reads this to route it to Forming instead of Actionable) — if the touch only
        // completes right at the close tick, isConfirmed is already true there too, so it's tagged
        // exactly like a normal bar-close alert instead.
        string ema20_touch_ltf_tag = barstate.isconfirmed ? "" : " (UNCONFIRMED, mid-candle)"
        string ema20_touch_htf_tag = barstate.isconfirmed ? "" : " (UNCONFIRMED, still forming)"
        if fire_ema20_touch_ltf
            alert(syminfo.ticker + " —\n• LTF EMA20 Touch - Pullback Entry: " + (cbc ? "LONG" : "SHORT") + ema20_touch_ltf_tag + "\n\n" + a_status_msg, ema20_touch_freq)
        if fire_ema20_touch_htf
            alert(syminfo.ticker + " —\n• HTF EMA20 Touch - Pullback Entry: " + (htf_dir_bull_now ? "LONG" : "SHORT") + ema20_touch_htf_tag + "\n\n" + a_status_msg, ema20_touch_freq)

        // HTF Pullback Entry - Structure Confirmed: recent HTF flip + pullback into HTF EMA9/20 zone +
        // HH/HL (or LH/LL) structure confirmation, with a risk reference (prior HTF candle low/high).
        if fire_htf_pullback_structure_long
            alert(syminfo.ticker + " —\n• HTF Pullback Entry - Structure Confirmed: LONG (HTF flip within " + htf_pullback_bars_str + " bars, pullback into HTF EMA9/20 zone, HH/HL structure)\nRisk: below " + str.tostring(sig_htf_lo, "#.##") + " (prior HTF candle low)\n\n" + a_status_msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
        if fire_htf_pullback_structure_short
            alert(syminfo.ticker + " —\n• HTF Pullback Entry - Structure Confirmed: SHORT (HTF flip within " + htf_pullback_bars_str + " bars, pullback into HTF EMA9/20 zone, LH/LL structure)\nRisk: above " + str.tostring(sig_htf_hi, "#.##") + " (prior HTF candle high)\n\n" + a_status_msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)

        // In FVG of HTF Flip Candle — fires intrabar (v4.41: alert.freq_once_per_bar, not
        // _bar_close) while price sits inside the 3-candle Fair Value Gap formed around the last
        // confirmed HTF flip, throttled to at most once every fvg_alert_min_gap_bars LTF bars and
        // cut off after fvg_alert_max_bars_after_flip bars past the flip — both already folded into
        // fire_htf_flip_fvg above. fire_htf_flip_fvg/htf_fvg_bull/top/bot are all plain top-level
        // variables (not raw inputs), same CE10116-avoidance pattern as everything else referenced
        // inside this function.
        if fire_htf_flip_fvg
            alert(syminfo.ticker + " —\n• In FVG of HTF Flip Candle: " + (htf_fvg_bull ? "LONG" : "SHORT") + "\nFVG: " + str.tostring(htf_fvg_bot, "#.##") + " – " + str.tostring(htf_fvg_top, "#.##") + "\n\n" + a_status_msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar)

    // Startup / Connected: fires once, NOT gated by alert_session_ok — this is purely "confirm the
    // connection is live," so it shouldn't be silently suppressed just because the script happened
    // to load outside your configured alert session windows.
    if alert_on_startup and cbc_startup_new
        alert(syminfo.ticker + " —\n• MapleStax CBC Started (connection confirmed)", alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)

    string early_titles = ""
    bool any_early = false

    // v4.28 token-trim: same collapse as the confirmed block above, applied to the early/
    // unconfirmed block — "and alert_session_ok" was on all 6 relevant conditions (2 at this
    // level, 4 more inside the existing htf_early_time_ok wrap). One outer read instead.
    if alert_session_ok
        if alert_on_ltf_flip_early
            if bull_flip
                early_titles := f_add_line(early_titles, "• LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + "m) CBC Flip: LONG (UNCONFIRMED, mid-candle — was short, now long)")
                any_early := true
            if bear_flip
                early_titles := f_add_line(early_titles, "• LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + "m) CBC Flip: SHORT (UNCONFIRMED, mid-candle — was long, now short)")
                any_early := true
        // Same combining as the confirmed section above, but for the early/unconfirmed variants —
        // each side's own guard conditions (flip's revert-check, agreement's ltf-join/transition
        // check) are computed first as "would this fire on its own" booleans, so the combo check and
        // the individual fallbacks can't disagree about whether a given side actually applies.
        // v4.20: the whole block is now wrapped in "if htf_early_time_ok" (one read) instead of appending
        // "and htf_early_time_ok" to all 4 *_ok booleans separately (four reads) — same gating, cheaper.
        if htf_early_time_ok
            bool htf_bull_live_is_revert = not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and htf_cbc_labels_f == 1.0
            bool flip_early_long_ok = alert_on_htf_flip_early and htf_flip_bull_live and (not htf_bull_live_is_revert or alert_htf_flip_early_revert)
            bool htf_bear_live_is_revert = not na(htf_cbc_labels_f) and htf_cbc_labels_f == 0.0
            bool flip_early_short_ok = alert_on_htf_flip_early and htf_flip_bear_live and (not htf_bear_live_is_revert or alert_htf_flip_early_revert)
            bool agree_early_long_ok = alert_on_agreement_early and agreement_enter_long_live and (alert_agreement_early_ltf_join or a_htf_dir_word_confirmed != a_htf_dir_word_live)
            bool agree_early_short_ok = alert_on_agreement_early and agreement_enter_short_live and (alert_agreement_early_ltf_join or a_htf_dir_word_confirmed != a_htf_dir_word_live)
            bool combo_early_long = flip_early_long_ok and agree_early_long_ok
            bool combo_early_short = flip_early_short_ok and agree_early_short_ok
            if combo_early_long
                early_titles := f_add_line(early_titles, "• HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") CBC Flip + LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + ") Agreement: LONG (early/unconfirmed — " + a_htf_transition + (a_htf_dir_word_confirmed != a_htf_dir_word_live ? a_ltf_state_note : "") + (htf_bull_live_is_revert ? " — reverted back to the already-confirmed Bullish, not a new flip" : "") + ")")
                any_early := true
            if combo_early_short
                early_titles := f_add_line(early_titles, "• HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") CBC Flip + LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + ") Agreement: SHORT (early/unconfirmed — " + a_htf_transition + (a_htf_dir_word_confirmed != a_htf_dir_word_live ? a_ltf_state_note : "") + (htf_bear_live_is_revert ? " — reverted back to the already-confirmed Bearish, not a new flip" : "") + ")")
                any_early := true
            if flip_early_long_ok and not combo_early_long
                early_titles := f_add_line(early_titles, "• HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") CBC Flip: LONG (UNCONFIRMED, still forming" + (htf_bull_live_is_revert ? " — reverted back to the already-confirmed Bullish, not a new flip" : "") + ")")
                any_early := true
            if flip_early_short_ok and not combo_early_short
                early_titles := f_add_line(early_titles, "• HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") CBC Flip: SHORT (UNCONFIRMED, still forming" + (htf_bear_live_is_revert ? " — reverted back to the already-confirmed Bearish, not a new flip" : "") + ")")
                any_early := true
            if agree_early_long_ok and not combo_early_long
                early_titles := f_add_line(early_titles, "• LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + ") " + a_ltf_dir_word + " + HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") Agreement: LONG (early/unconfirmed — " + a_htf_transition + (a_htf_dir_word_confirmed != a_htf_dir_word_live ? a_ltf_state_note : "") + ")")
                any_early := true
            if agree_early_short_ok and not combo_early_short
                early_titles := f_add_line(early_titles, "• LTF(" + str.tostring(timeframe.multiplier) + ") " + a_ltf_dir_word + " + HTF(" + htf_timeframe + ") Agreement: SHORT (early/unconfirmed — " + a_htf_transition + (a_htf_dir_word_confirmed != a_htf_dir_word_live ? a_ltf_state_note : "") + ")")
                any_early := true

        if any_early
            alert(syminfo.ticker + " —\n" + early_titles + "\n\n" + a_status_msg + a_brsg_line, alert.freq_once_per_bar)
    true

if barstate.islast
    f_fire_alerts()

// ===========================
// CBC agreement bar colors — small color-key legend (only meaningful while show_bar_coloring is on)
// ===========================
var table cbc_legend_tbl = na
var string cbc_legend_pos_last = ""

if barstate.islast
    if not (show_bar_coloring and show_cbc_legend)
        table.delete(cbc_legend_tbl)
        cbc_legend_tbl := na
        cbc_legend_pos_last := ""
    else
        if not na(cbc_legend_tbl) and cbc_legend_pos_last != cbc_legend_position
            table.delete(cbc_legend_tbl)
            cbc_legend_tbl := na
        if na(cbc_legend_tbl)
            cbc_legend_tbl := table.new(f_status_pos(cbc_legend_position), 2, 4, bgcolor=color.new(#020617, 0), border_color=tbl_border, border_width=1, frame_color=tbl_frame, frame_width=2)
            table.merge_cells(cbc_legend_tbl, 0, 0, 1, 0)
            cbc_legend_pos_last := cbc_legend_position
        cbc_legend_txt_sz = f_table_text_size(cbc_legend_text_size)
        table.cell(cbc_legend_tbl, 0, 0, "CBC Agreement Colors", bgcolor=tbl_header_bg, text_color=txt_head, text_size=cbc_legend_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_center)
        table.cell(cbc_legend_tbl, 0, 1, "", bgcolor=col_cbc_bar_long)
        table.cell(cbc_legend_tbl, 1, 1, "Long agreement", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_on, text_size=cbc_legend_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left)
        table.cell(cbc_legend_tbl, 0, 2, "", bgcolor=col_cbc_bar_short)
        table.cell(cbc_legend_tbl, 1, 2, "Short agreement", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_on, text_size=cbc_legend_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left)
        table.cell(cbc_legend_tbl, 0, 3, "", bgcolor=col_cbc_bar_neutral)
        table.cell(cbc_legend_tbl, 1, 3, "No agreement", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_on, text_size=cbc_legend_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left)

// ===========================
// Definitions — condensed LONG/SHORT glossary table
// ===========================
var table def_tbl = na
var string def_tbl_pos_last = ""

if barstate.islast
    if not show_definitions_tbl
        table.delete(def_tbl)
        def_tbl := na
        def_tbl_pos_last := ""
    else
        if not na(def_tbl) and def_tbl_pos_last != definitions_tbl_position
            table.delete(def_tbl)
            def_tbl := na
        if na(def_tbl)
            def_tbl := table.new(f_status_pos(definitions_tbl_position), 2, 3, bgcolor=color.new(#020617, 0), border_color=tbl_border, border_width=1, frame_color=tbl_frame, frame_width=2)
            table.merge_cells(def_tbl, 0, 0, 1, 0)
            def_tbl_pos_last := definitions_tbl_position
        def_tbl_txt_sz = f_table_text_size(definitions_tbl_text_size)
        table.cell(def_tbl, 0, 0, "Definitions", bgcolor=tbl_header_bg, text_color=txt_head, text_size=def_tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_center)
        table.cell(def_tbl, 0, 1, "LONG", bgcolor=st_bull, text_color=txt_on, text_size=def_tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left)
        table.cell(def_tbl, 1, 1, "Bullish flip, price expected up", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_on, text_size=def_tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left)
        table.cell(def_tbl, 0, 2, "SHORT", bgcolor=st_bear, text_color=txt_on, text_size=def_tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left)
        table.cell(def_tbl, 1, 2, "Bearish flip, price expected down", bgcolor=tbl_label_bg, text_color=txt_on, text_size=def_tbl_txt_sz, text_halign=text.align_left)
